# Movie UI (piece F) and the Release-Prune Fix Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Add `bin/movie-ui`, a token-protected page on 127.0.0.1:8999 that builds `bin/ltx-movie`, `bin/character` and `bin/lora-catalog` command lines from forms, validates models, LoRAs and characters (checks V1-V7) before any GPU time is spent, runs heavy commands as detached, single-file-queued jobs that survive a server restart, edits `story.md` between the story stage and the render, and shows fleet traces as waterfalls; and make self-update keep every older release whose `generated/` holds data.

**Architecture:** `movie_ui_lib.py` (stdlib only) holds the STATE files and job store, the detached runner core that `bin/movie-ui-job` calls, liveness by `flock`, the FIFO dispatcher and startup recovery, the pure command builder, the help-flag parser, checks V1-V7, the HF-cache rules and the incremental progress parser. `movie_ui_server.py` (stdlib plus the UI workspace's own `fleet_lib`, read-only names only) holds the hardened `ThreadingHTTPServer`, the route table and every JSON endpoint, `/files` with byte ranges, the dispatcher and capability-probe threads, the trace proxy and `main()`, which `bin/movie-ui` calls. The page is three static files under `static/movie-ui/` with no network assets. The prune fix is one new `fleet_lib.release_holds_data`, one `protected=()` parameter of `releases_to_prune`, and a rewritten `_confirm_switch` in `bin/qwen-agentd`.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.13 (`python3`, pytest 8.3.4) and Python 3.9.6 (`/usr/bin/python3`, compile check plus a runtime smoke; it has no pytest); standard library only in every new module; vanilla ES2017 JavaScript, HTML and CSS; stub `bin/` tools, a fake daemon and canned logs from the new `tests/movie_ui_testutil.py` and `tests/fixtures/movie_ui/`; `node --check` for the page script; Playwright (Python, chromium) for the controller-run end-to-end check only.

**Spec:** `qwen-agent-workspace/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-movie-ui-design.md` at `a378d33` (the design committed at `0b9120d` plus the user's Q1-Q4 answers). Every Edit below is written against branch `qwen-agent-redteam` at `a378d33`, whose code tree is the code tree of `22dc4a1` (the spec's line numbers cite `22dc4a1`). The spec is binding; this plan changes no design decision. Where the plan had to read the spec, or the spec was silent or self-contradictory, the reading is listed in "Plan decisions" at the end.

**Rulings on the spec's open questions (user-confirmed at `a378d33`; each is isolated so it can be flipped):**

| Q1 (D1) | YES: the runner's descendant process-group sweep (MU23 step 1) is in. | Task 3 only (one Edit of `cancel_groups` plus two tests). Without Task 3, Task 2's `cancel_groups` signals the child's own group, and MUK4 becomes "the job's own group is gone within 16 s". |
| Q2 (D2) | A cast character's video-LoRA base-model mismatch is a non-blocking `skip` whose detail shows the training base. | Task 8, the `character <name> video base model` record in `compat_checks` (two `check(...)` calls) and its rows in `test_mut7_v5_compatible`. |

## Global Constraints

From the spec Section 1, verbatim:

- **GC1. Interpreters.** Every new or changed Python file compiles and runs on Python 3.9.6 and 3.13, on macOS and Linux. Forbidden: `match` statements, `X | Y` type expressions, `zip(..., strict=...)`, parenthesised context managers, the `cgi` module (removed in 3.13), `importlib`'s `load_module` in new code.
- **GC2. Standard library only.** New files import only the standard library, `movie_ui_lib`, `movie_ui_server`, and (in `movie_ui_server.py` only) the UI's own workspace `fleet_lib` (GC7).
- **GC3. HTTP clients.** Every outbound HTTP request uses `http.client` (here: only through `fleet_lib.fetch_trace`/`fetch_recent`). `urllib` is never imported.
- **GC4. No network assets.** The page loads nothing from the network: no CDN, no font, no external URL. Every asset is served by movie-ui from `static/movie-ui/`.
- **GC5. Subprocesses.** Every command is an argv list; `shell=True` never appears; no client-supplied argv is ever executed (MU40).
- **GC6. Secrets.** movie-ui never reads, stores, logs or passes the fleet token and never opens any `fleet.env`. It reports only whether `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is set (a bool), and passes its own environment unchanged to jobs so `--judge` works. The per-launch UI token is printed once to the terminal, never written to disk, never logged (MU7).
- **GC7. No pipeline imports.** `movie_ui_lib.py` and `movie_ui_server.py` never import or load by path any pipeline module (`lora_catalog`, `character_lib`, `story_engine`, `z_image_skill`, `ltx2_mlx_video_skill`, `pipeline_log`, ...). Catalog, character and dry-run data come from the selected workspace's own `bin/` tools run as subprocesses. `movie_ui_server.py` imports the UI's own `fleet_lib` (`import fleet_lib` with the UI workspace on `sys.path`) and uses only these read-only names: `render_trace`, `fetch_trace`, `fetch_recent`, `valid_trace_id`, `valid_trace_result`, `agent_ids_on_disk`, `agent_port`, `default_command_path`, `classify_command_path`, `deploy_root`, `releases_to_prune`, `release_holds_data`, `read_state`, `tag_key`, `DEFAULT_PORT`, `WS_SUBDIR`, `TRACE_RECENT_MAX`, `TRACE_RECENT_DEFAULT` **[spec choice: the brief named only `render_trace`; reusing the registry reader, wire clients and validators avoids a second copy of their rules, and `fleet_lib` is stdlib-only]**.
- **GC8. Tests.** Tests never use the real `HOME`, never render, never contact a real server or the Anthropic API, and keep `tests/conftest.py`'s autouse `LORA_CATALOG_DIR` isolation working (no test unsets it except by its own `monkeypatch`).
- **GC9. Times.** Every timestamp written by movie-ui is UTC `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ` (`time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime())`), called `utc_now()` below.
- **GC10. Atomic writes.** Every file movie-ui writes is written to `<path>.tmp-<pid>-<thread id>` in the same directory, closed, then `os.replace`d onto `<path>`; mode 0600. No `fsync` **[spec choice: losing the last status write on power loss costs one job record, never data]**. Directories movie-ui creates have mode 0700.

Added for this plan (the controller's rules; every task's requirements include them):

- Tests never use the real HOME, never render, never contact real servers, daemons or the Anthropic API (the movie-ui tests set `HOME` to a temp directory through `movie_ui_testutil.set_home`, and the one subprocess test of `bin/movie-ui` runs a copy of the UI whose `bin/story-server` is a stub).
- Never stage `../.DS_Store`, `../.claude/`, `../agent/__pycache__/` or the repo-root `../bin/`. Implementers never use `git stash`, `git reset` or `git checkout` of any path.
- `python3` on this Mac is 3.13.0 and `/usr/bin/python3` is 3.9.6; both must `py_compile` every new or changed `.py` file and `bin/` script (each task's Step 5).
- Baseline: the full suite (`python3 -m pytest tests/ -q -rA` from `WS`) currently gives `1 failed (tests/test_character_dataset.py::test_d2_copied_literals_match_the_spike, pre-existing), 1 skipped`; acceptance is no new failure (Task 18).

House rules:

- `WS` = `/Users/reubenpatterson/local_agent_harness/qwen-agent-workspace`. The git root is one level up, so `git diff --name-only` paths carry the `qwen-agent-workspace/` prefix. Branch `qwen-agent-redteam`. Run every command from `WS` unless a step says otherwise.
- Every code block in this plan is copied exactly, byte for byte. A **Create** writes a new file whose whole content is the block. An **Append** adds two empty lines after the file's current last line (which ends with one newline), then the block. An **Edit** replaces one block of old text, which occurs exactly once in the file at that moment, with the new text; if the old text does not occur exactly once, stop and report: do not adapt it. A **Run** executes the shell block as given. Never retype a block from the spec when this plan shows it.
- Every pytest run prints a `pytest_asyncio` `PytestDeprecationWarning` about `asyncio_default_fixture_loop_scope` before the results; it is expected. Durations vary; only the pass/fail counts matter. macOS has no `timeout` command; do not use it.
- `.gitignore` ignores `*.log`, so Task 9 stages its four fixture logs with `git add -f`; nothing else is force-added. `.gitignore` also ignores `__pycache__/`, so the `py_compile` runs leave nothing to stage.
- Commit hygiene: the working tree has unrelated untracked paths (`../.DS_Store`, `../.claude/`, `../agent/__pycache__/`, `../bin/`) and, while it is uncommitted, this plan file; never stage them. Stage only the paths each task names, check `git diff --cached --name-only` before each commit, put `(movie-ui ...)` requirement IDs in every commit subject, and end every commit message with the line `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>` (the commit commands pass it as a second `-m`).
- Pre-tested: every task below was replayed from this plan's blocks, in order, on a fresh clone of `a378d33` under a temporary `HOME`; each RED and GREEN line is the one observed there on this Mac, every changed file compiled under both interpreters, and the replayed final tree equals the tested tree byte for byte.

## Review Focus

These five inputs are implied by the spec, are the ones most likely to hit a real user first, and no MUT/MPT test exercises them. Each is pinned by a test in its owning task.

1. **A narrative and a `story.md` edit outside ASCII** (accents, CJK, an em dash, emoji). Expected: the previewed argv carries the narrative unchanged, `GET /api/stories/<id>` returns the text and its sha256, and `PUT .../story.md` writes exactly `text.encode("utf-8")`, so a reload shows the same text. Test: `test_review_focus_1_non_ascii_story_round_trip` (Task 14).
2. **Paths with spaces, `+` and non-ASCII letters in `/files` URLs** (a workspace under `~/My Movies/`, a still named `café still+1.png`; the page percent-encodes them). Expected: the local percent decoder recovers the exact path and the file is served with its type. Test: `test_review_focus_2_files_with_spaces_and_non_ascii_names` (Task 14).
3. **Cancel pressed in the instant between dispatch and the runner's start** (status `starting`, no child yet). Expected: the runner sees the marker, writes `cancelled` with a null exit code, prints `[movie-ui] cancelled before start`, and never starts the child. Test: `test_review_focus_3_cancel_while_starting` (Task 4).
4. **Two browser tabs (or a double click) submitting jobs at the same moment.** Expected: every submit answers 201 with a distinct job ID, and every ID is in `queue.json` exactly once (STORE_LOCK). Test: `test_review_focus_4_two_tabs_submit_at_once` (Task 12).
5. **A running job's log holding bytes that are not UTF-8, and a multibyte character split across polls** (ffmpeg and model output). Expected: progress and the log endpoint decode with replacement characters, the split character is parsed whole once its rest arrives, and nothing answers 500. Test: `test_review_focus_5_undecodable_bytes_in_a_running_log` (Task 12).

### Task 1: The release-prune fix: `release_holds_data`, `protected=`, `_confirm_switch`

Implements MP1-MP3 (spec Section 13) exactly as the spec's code blocks give them, and MPT1-MPT3. Independent of every movie-ui task.

**Files:**
- Modify: `tests/test_fleet_lib.py`
- Modify: `tests/test_fleet_self_update.py`
- Modify: `fleet_lib.py`
- Modify: `bin/qwen-agentd`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `fleet_lib.tag_key`, `KEEP_RELEASES`, `WS_SUBDIR`, `read_state`, `write_state`, `deploy_root` (existing); `bin/qwen-agentd`'s `state_path`, `unstage_release`, `log_to_daemon`, `LOG` (existing); `tests/test_fleet_self_update.py`'s `_setup`, `_deploy`, `_git`, `_version`, `_state`, `Supervisor`, `ftu.wait_until`, `ftu.read_log` (existing).
- Produces: `fleet_lib.release_holds_data(release_dir) -> bool` (True iff `<release_dir>/qwen-agent-workspace/generated/` holds a regular file at any depth; a symlinked `generated/` or symlinked files do not count); `fleet_lib.releases_to_prune(present, current, state, protected=()) -> [str]` (unchanged result when `protected` is empty). Task 11's `list_workspaces` calls both.

**1.1.** **Append** to `tests/test_fleet_lib.py`:

```python
# --- movie-ui spec MPT1, MPT2: data-holding releases are never pruned (MP1, MP2) ---------------

def test_mpt1_release_holds_data(tmp_path):
    rel = tmp_path / "v2026.01.01"
    assert fleet_lib.release_holds_data(str(rel)) is False
    gen = rel / fleet_lib.WS_SUBDIR / "generated"
    (gen / "stories" / "empty").mkdir(parents=True)
    assert fleet_lib.release_holds_data(str(rel)) is False
    outside = tmp_path / "outside.txt"
    outside.write_text("x")
    (gen / "stories" / "link.md").symlink_to(outside)
    assert fleet_lib.release_holds_data(str(rel)) is False
    deep = gen / "stories" / "x" / "images"
    deep.mkdir(parents=True)
    (deep / "panel_01.png").write_bytes(b"p")
    assert fleet_lib.release_holds_data(str(rel)) is True
    other = tmp_path / "v2026.01.02"
    (other / fleet_lib.WS_SUBDIR).mkdir(parents=True)
    (other / fleet_lib.WS_SUBDIR / "generated").symlink_to(gen)
    assert fleet_lib.release_holds_data(str(other)) is False


def test_mpt2_releases_to_prune_keeps_protected_names():
    present = ["v2026.01.01", "v2026.01.02", "v2026.01.03", "v2026.01.04", "v2026.01.05",
               "v2026.01.06", "notes", ".DS_Store"]
    st = {"from": "v2026.01.02", "to": "v2026.01.03", "status": "ok", "attempts": 0, "bad_tags": []}
    assert fleet_lib.releases_to_prune(present, "v2026.01.03", st, protected={"v2026.01.01"}) == []
    assert fleet_lib.releases_to_prune(present, "v2026.01.03", st, protected=()) == ["v2026.01.01"]
    st["from"] = None
    assert fleet_lib.releases_to_prune(present, "v2026.01.03", st, protected={"v2026.01.01"}) == ["v2026.01.02"]
    assert fleet_lib.releases_to_prune(present, "v2026.01.03", st, protected=()) == ["v2026.01.01", "v2026.01.02"]
    assert fleet_lib.releases_to_prune(present, "v2026.01.03", st) == ["v2026.01.01", "v2026.01.02"]
```

**1.2.** **Append** to `tests/test_fleet_self_update.py`:

```python
def test_mpt3_switch_keeps_data_holding_releases(tmp_path):
    """movie-ui spec MPT3 (MP3): after a switch, an old release whose generated/ holds a file is
    kept and logged; a data-less release beyond the newest 3 is still removed."""
    origin, home, key, port = _setup(tmp_path)
    d = _deploy(home)
    repo = os.path.join(d, "repo")
    old_data, old_empty = os.path.join(d, "releases", "v2025.12.01"), os.path.join(d, "releases", "v2025.11.30")
    for path in (old_data, old_empty, os.path.join(d, "releases", "v2025.12.02")):
        _git(repo, "worktree", "add", "-q", "--detach", path, "refs/tags/v2026.01.01")
    story = os.path.join(old_data, fleet_lib.WS_SUBDIR, "generated", "stories", "x")
    os.makedirs(story)
    with open(os.path.join(story, "story.md"), "w") as f:
        f.write("## Panel 1\n")
    sup = Supervisor(home)
    try:
        ftu.wait_until(lambda: _version(key, port) == "v2026.01.01", 20)
        origin.release("v2026.01.02")
        ftu.wait_until(lambda: _version(key, port) == "v2026.01.02" and _state(home)["status"] == "ok", 60)
        ftu.wait_until(lambda: not os.path.exists(old_empty), 30)
        assert os.path.isfile(os.path.join(story, "story.md"))
        assert sorted(os.listdir(os.path.join(d, "releases"))) == [
            "v2025.12.01", "v2025.12.02", "v2026.01.01", "v2026.01.02"]
        log = ftu.read_log(home, "h1")
        assert "update: keeping v2025.12.01: generated/ holds data" in log
        assert "keeping v2025.12.02" not in log and "keeping v2025.11.30" not in log
    finally:
        sup.close()
```

**3.2.** **Edit** `fleet_lib.py`: replace this text (it occurs exactly once):

```python
def releases_to_prune(present, current, state):
    """Release names to unstage: never current, state["from"], or the 3 newest (F88a)."""
    cands = [n for n in present if tag_key(n) is not None]
    keep = {current}
    if state.get("from") is not None:
        keep.add(state["from"])
    keep.update(sorted(cands, key=tag_key, reverse=True)[:KEEP_RELEASES])
    return sorted([n for n in cands if n not in keep], key=tag_key)


def release_version(repo_root, deploy_root):
```

with:

```python
def releases_to_prune(present, current, state, protected=()):
    """Release names to unstage: never current, state["from"], the 3 newest, or a protected name (F88a, movie-ui spec MP2)."""
    cands = [n for n in present if tag_key(n) is not None]
    keep = {current}
    if state.get("from") is not None:
        keep.add(state["from"])
    keep.update(protected)
    keep.update(sorted(cands, key=tag_key, reverse=True)[:KEEP_RELEASES])
    return sorted([n for n in cands if n not in keep], key=tag_key)


def release_holds_data(release_dir):
    """True iff <release_dir>/qwen-agent-workspace/generated/ holds a regular file at any depth (movie-ui spec MP1)."""
    top = os.path.join(release_dir, WS_SUBDIR, "generated")
    if os.path.islink(top) or not os.path.isdir(top):
        return False
    for dirpath, _dirnames, filenames in os.walk(top, followlinks=False):
        for name in filenames:
            try:
                if stat.S_ISREG(os.lstat(os.path.join(dirpath, name)).st_mode):
                    return True
            except OSError:
                continue
    return False


def release_version(repo_root, deploy_root):
```

```python
        releases = os.path.join(d, "releases")
        present = os.listdir(releases)
        protected = set(t for t in present if fleet_lib.release_holds_data(os.path.join(releases, t)))
        doomed = fleet_lib.releases_to_prune(present, self.version, new, protected)
        for tag in fleet_lib.releases_to_prune(present, self.version, new):
            if tag not in doomed:
                LOG.info("update: keeping %s: generated/ holds data" % tag)
        for tag in doomed:
            unstage_release(d, tag, log_to_daemon)

    def wait(self):
```

Tests: the store test, MUT10, MUT11 (TERM, KILL, same-group grandchild), MUT12, and the not-starting refusal.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/movie_ui_testutil.py`
- Create: `tests/test_movie_ui_jobs.py`
- Create: `movie_ui_lib.py`
- Create: `bin/movie-ui-job`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing from earlier tasks.
- Produces, in `movie_ui_lib`: constants `STATE_PATH`, `STATE_SUBDIRS = ("jobs", "uploads", "traces", "smoke")`, `JOB_SCHEMA = "movie-ui.job.v1"`, `SAVED_TRACE_SCHEMA`, `JOB_ID_RE`, `UPLOAD_ID_RE`, `STORY_ID_RE`, `CHARACTER_NAME_RE`, `SMOKE_STORY_ID = "movie-ui-smoke"`, `CANCEL_GRACE_S = 15.0`, `STARTING_GRACE_S = 10.0`, `ACTIVE_STATES = ("starting", "running")`, `FINAL_STATES = ("done", "failed", "cancelled")`, `FINISHED_STATES` (final plus `"lost"`), `RUNNERS` (list of runner `Popen`s); `utc_now() -> str`; `parse_utc(text) -> int or None`; `state_dir() -> str`; `make_dir(path)`; `ensure_state(state)`; `write_bytes_atomic(path, data)`; `write_json_atomic(path, obj)`; `read_json(path, default=None)`; `jobs_root(state)`; `job_dir(state, job_id)`; `queue_path(state)`; `read_job(jdir) -> dict or None`; `read_status(jdir) -> dict or None`; `write_status(jdir, status)`; `read_queue(state) -> [str]`; `write_queue(state, ids)`; `list_job_ids(state) -> [str]` (sorted); `new_job_id(state) -> str`; `create_job(state, job_id, kind, workspace, story_id, character, argv, fields) -> dict` (caller holds STORE_LOCK); `launch_runner(state, job_id, ui_ws) -> Popen` (raises `OSError`); `group_alive(group) -> bool`; `cancel_groups(child_pid) -> [int]`; `cancel_tree(child, grace)`; `run_job(jdir, grace=CANCEL_GRACE_S) -> int` (0, 1 or 3). `bin/movie-ui-job [--grace S] JOB_DIR`.
- Produces, in `tests/movie_ui_testutil.py` (imported as `mt`): `WS`, `TESTS`, `FIXTURES`; `set_home(monkeypatch, tmp_path, grace=0.5) -> state`; `wait_until(predicate, timeout, interval=0.05)` (returns the truthy value); `py_argv(code)`; `make_job(state, argv, workspace, kind="movie", story_id="s1", fields=None, status=None) -> job_id`; `dispatch(state, job_id) -> Popen`; `status_of(state, job_id) -> dict`; `read_log(state, job_id) -> str`; `pid_alive(pid) -> bool` (zombies count as gone); `kill_everything(state)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

**1.1.** **Create** `tests/movie_ui_testutil.py` with exactly this content:

```python
"""Shared helpers for the movie-ui tests (movie-ui spec 14.2,
docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-movie-ui-design.md).

Not a test module (pytest does not collect it). Every helper writes under a directory the caller
passes in; nothing here reads or writes the real HOME, renders, or contacts a real server.
"""

import http.client
import http.server
import json
import os
import shutil
import signal
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time

WS = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(__file__)))
TESTS = os.path.join(WS, "tests")
FIXTURES = os.path.join(TESTS, "fixtures", "movie_ui")
sys.path.insert(0, WS)

import fleet_lib  # noqa: E402
import movie_ui_lib  # noqa: E402


def set_home(monkeypatch, tmp_path, grace=0.5):
    """HOME=<tmp>/home, CANCEL_GRACE_S=grace, no ANTHROPIC_API_KEY; returns the created STATE."""
    home = os.path.join(str(tmp_path), "home")
    os.makedirs(home, exist_ok=True)
    monkeypatch.setenv("HOME", home)
    monkeypatch.delenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(movie_ui_lib, "CANCEL_GRACE_S", grace)
    state = movie_ui_lib.state_dir()
    movie_ui_lib.ensure_state(state)
    return state


def wait_until(predicate, timeout, interval=0.05):
    """Poll predicate() until truthy; return its value, or fail the test."""
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while True:
        value = predicate()
        if value:
            return value
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            raise AssertionError("condition not met within %.1f s" % timeout)
        time.sleep(interval)


def py_argv(code):
    """[sys.executable, "-c", code]: a real child process for runner tests."""
    return [sys.executable, "-c", code]


def make_job(state, argv, workspace, kind="movie", story_id="s1", fields=None, status=None):
    """Create a queued job (as the server does under STORE_LOCK); with status, also write it and
    drop the job from the queue. Returns the job ID."""
    job_id = movie_ui_lib.new_job_id(state)
    movie_ui_lib.create_job(state, job_id, kind, workspace, story_id, None, argv,
                            fields or {"action": "movie", "mode": "chained"})
    if status is not None:
        movie_ui_lib.write_queue(state, [i for i in movie_ui_lib.read_queue(state) if i != job_id])
        movie_ui_lib.write_status(movie_ui_lib.job_dir(state, job_id), status)
    return job_id


def dispatch(state, job_id):
    """Write the "starting" status and launch the runner, as dispatch_tick does; returns the Popen."""
    jdir = movie_ui_lib.job_dir(state, job_id)
    movie_ui_lib.write_queue(state, [i for i in movie_ui_lib.read_queue(state) if i != job_id])
    movie_ui_lib.write_status(jdir, {"state": "starting", "dispatched": movie_ui_lib.utc_now()})
    return movie_ui_lib.launch_runner(state, job_id, WS)


def cancel_marker(state, job_id):
    """Create the job's cancel marker, as cancel_job does for a starting or running job (MU17)."""
    movie_ui_lib.write_bytes_atomic(os.path.join(movie_ui_lib.job_dir(state, job_id), "cancel"), b"")


def status_of(state, job_id):
    return movie_ui_lib.read_status(movie_ui_lib.job_dir(state, job_id)) or {}


def read_log(state, job_id):
    with open(os.path.join(movie_ui_lib.job_dir(state, job_id), "log.txt"), "rb") as f:
        return f.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")


def pid_alive(pid):
    try:
        os.kill(pid, 0)
    except ProcessLookupError:
        return False
    except PermissionError:
        return True
    try:
        out = subprocess.run(["ps", "-o", "stat=", "-p", str(pid)], capture_output=True, timeout=5)
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return True
    return not out.stdout.decode("ascii", "replace").strip().startswith("Z")


def kill_everything(state):
    """Teardown: SIGKILL every job's recorded group and every launched runner."""
    for job_id in movie_ui_lib.list_job_ids(state):
        pgid = status_of(state, job_id).get("pgid")
        if isinstance(pgid, int) and pgid > 1:
            try:
                os.killpg(pgid, signal.SIGKILL)
            except OSError:
                pass
    for proc in list(movie_ui_lib.RUNNERS):
        try:
            os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
        except OSError:
            pass
        try:
            proc.wait(5)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            pass
    del movie_ui_lib.RUNNERS[:]
```

**1.2.** **Create** `tests/test_movie_ui_jobs.py` with exactly this content:

```python
"""movie-ui job store, runner, cancel, liveness, queue and server process tests (movie-ui spec
MUT10-MUT15). Run from the workspace root: python3 -m pytest tests/test_movie_ui_jobs.py -v
Every test runs under a temporary HOME; children are sys.executable -c scripts."""

import fcntl
import http.client
import json
import os
import re
import signal
import socket
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
import time

import pytest

import movie_ui_testutil as mt
import movie_ui_lib as lib

STATUS_KEYS_FINAL = {"state", "dispatched", "runner_pid", "pgid", "started", "exit_code", "ended"}


@pytest.fixture
def state(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    path = mt.set_home(monkeypatch, tmp_path)
    yield path
    mt.kill_everything(path)


def _final(state, job_id, timeout=20):
    return mt.wait_until(lambda: mt.status_of(state, job_id).get("state") in lib.FINISHED_STATES
                         and mt.status_of(state, job_id), timeout)


def test_store_writes_atomically_with_private_modes(state, tmp_path):
    for name in lib.STATE_SUBDIRS:
        assert stat.S_IMODE(os.stat(os.path.join(state, name)).st_mode) == 0o700
    assert stat.S_IMODE(os.stat(state).st_mode) == 0o700
    assert re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}Z", lib.utc_now())
    path = os.path.join(state, "x.json")
    lib.write_json_atomic(path, {"a": "é"})
    assert stat.S_IMODE(os.stat(path).st_mode) == 0o600
    assert lib.read_json(path) == {"a": "é"}
    assert [n for n in os.listdir(state) if ".tmp-" in n] == []
    job_id = lib.new_job_id(state)
    assert lib.JOB_ID_RE.fullmatch(job_id)
    for bad in ("20261019-142233-a1b", "20261019-142233-A1B2", "20261019-142233-a1b2\n", "x"):
        assert not lib.JOB_ID_RE.fullmatch(bad)
    job = lib.create_job(state, job_id, "movie", str(tmp_path), "s1", None, ["/bin/echo", "hi"], {"action": "movie"})
    jdir = lib.job_dir(state, job_id)
    assert stat.S_IMODE(os.stat(jdir).st_mode) == 0o700
    assert lib.read_job(jdir) == job
    assert set(job) == {"schema", "id", "kind", "workspace", "story_id", "character", "argv", "fields", "created"}
    assert job["schema"] == "movie-ui.job.v1"
    assert os.path.getsize(os.path.join(jdir, "log.txt")) == 0
    assert lib.read_status(jdir) is None
    assert lib.read_queue(state) == [job_id]
    with open(lib.queue_path(state), "w") as f:
        f.write("{not json")
    assert lib.read_queue(state) == []


def test_mut10_runner_outcomes_status_and_log(state, tmp_path):
    ok = mt.make_job(state, mt.py_argv("print('hello from the child')"), str(tmp_path))
    bad = mt.make_job(state, mt.py_argv("import sys; sys.stderr.write('boom\\n'); sys.exit(3)"), str(tmp_path))
    gone = mt.make_job(state, [os.path.join(str(tmp_path), "no-such-binary")], str(tmp_path))
    for job_id in (ok, bad, gone):
        mt.dispatch(state, job_id)
    s_ok, s_bad, s_gone = _final(state, ok), _final(state, bad), _final(state, gone)
    assert s_ok["state"] == "done" and s_ok["exit_code"] == 0
    assert s_bad["state"] == "failed" and s_bad["exit_code"] == 3
    assert set(s_ok) == STATUS_KEYS_FINAL and set(s_bad) == STATUS_KEYS_FINAL
    assert s_ok["pgid"] != s_ok["runner_pid"] and isinstance(s_ok["pgid"], int)
    assert set(s_gone) == STATUS_KEYS_FINAL | {"error"}
    assert s_gone["state"] == "failed" and s_gone["pgid"] is None and s_gone["started"] is None
    assert s_gone["exit_code"] is None and s_gone["error"].startswith("cannot start: ")
    log = mt.wait_until(lambda: "[movie-ui] exit 0 (done)" in mt.read_log(state, ok) and mt.read_log(state, ok), 5)
    assert "hello from the child\n" in log
    assert re.search(r"^\[movie-ui\] started pid [0-9]+: .+ -c ", log, re.M)
    bad_log = mt.wait_until(lambda: "[movie-ui] exit 3 (failed)" in mt.read_log(state, bad)
                            and mt.read_log(state, bad), 5)
    assert "boom\n" in bad_log
    assert "[movie-ui] cannot start: " in mt.wait_until(lambda: mt.read_log(state, gone), 5)


def test_mut12_runner_started_directly_without_a_server(state, tmp_path):
    job_id = mt.make_job(state, mt.py_argv("print('direct')"), str(tmp_path))
    jdir = lib.job_dir(state, job_id)
    lib.write_status(jdir, {"state": "starting", "dispatched": lib.utc_now()})
    proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(mt.WS, "bin", "movie-ui-job"), "--grace", "0.5", jdir],
                          capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stderr
    status = lib.read_status(jdir)
    assert status["state"] == "done" and status["exit_code"] == 0
    assert "[movie-ui] exit 0 (done)" in proc.stdout
    again = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(mt.WS, "bin", "movie-ui-job"), jdir],
                           capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    assert again.returncode == 3
    assert again.stdout == "[movie-ui] job is done; nothing to run\n"


def _ready_file(tmp_path, name):
    return os.path.join(str(tmp_path), name)


def test_mut11_cancel_term_then_kill_and_same_group_grandchild(state, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(lib, "CANCEL_GRACE_S", 1.0)
    ready = _ready_file(tmp_path, "ready")
    polite = mt.make_job(state, mt.py_argv("import time; open(%r, 'w').write('1'); time.sleep(60)" % ready),
                         str(tmp_path))
    mt.dispatch(state, polite)
    mt.wait_until(lambda: os.path.exists(ready), 10)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    mt.cancel_marker(state, polite)
    status = _final(state, polite)
    assert status["state"] == "cancelled" and status["exit_code"] == -signal.SIGTERM
    assert time.monotonic() - t0 < 1.0 + 1.0 + 1.0
    assert "[movie-ui] cancel: SIGTERM to process group(s) %d" % status["pgid"] in mt.read_log(state, polite)

    ready2, grand = _ready_file(tmp_path, "ready2"), _ready_file(tmp_path, "grand")
    code = ("import signal, subprocess, sys, time\n"
            "signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIG_IGN)\n"
            "g = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-c', 'import signal, time; "
            "signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIG_IGN); time.sleep(60)'])\n"
            "open(%r, 'w').write(str(g.pid))\n"
            "open(%r, 'w').write('1')\n"
            "time.sleep(60)\n" % (grand, ready2))
    stubborn = mt.make_job(state, mt.py_argv(code), str(tmp_path))
    mt.dispatch(state, stubborn)
    mt.wait_until(lambda: os.path.exists(ready2), 10)
    grand_pid = int(open(grand).read())
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    mt.cancel_marker(state, stubborn)
    status = _final(state, stubborn)
    assert status["state"] == "cancelled" and status["exit_code"] == -signal.SIGKILL
    assert time.monotonic() - t0 < 1.0 + 1.0 + 1.0
    log = mt.read_log(state, stubborn)
    assert "[movie-ui] cancel: SIGKILL to process group(s) %d" % status["pgid"] in log
    mt.wait_until(lambda: not mt.pid_alive(grand_pid), 5)
    assert not lib.group_alive(status["pgid"])


def test_runner_refuses_a_job_that_is_not_starting(state, tmp_path):
    job_id = mt.make_job(state, mt.py_argv("print(1)"), str(tmp_path))
    jdir = lib.job_dir(state, job_id)
    proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(mt.WS, "bin", "movie-ui-job"), jdir],
                          capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    assert (proc.returncode, proc.stdout) == (3, "[movie-ui] job is unknown; nothing to run\n")
    assert lib.read_status(jdir) is None
```

Expected (observed): `1 error`. Collection fails: `ERROR tests/test_movie_ui_jobs.py` with `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'movie_ui_lib'` (raised by `movie_ui_testutil`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

**3.1.** **Create** `movie_ui_lib.py` with exactly this content:

```python
"""movie_ui_lib -- the movie-ui core: STATE files, the job store and queue, the detached job
runner, the command builder, validation checks V1-V7 and the progress parser.

See docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-movie-ui-design.md (the movie-ui spec, piece F).
Standard library only (GC2): this module never imports or loads by path any pipeline module
(GC7); catalog, character and dry-run data come from the selected workspace's own bin/ tools
run as subprocesses. Valid on Python 3.9 and 3.13 (GC1). Every file it writes is written
atomically, mode 0600; every directory it creates is mode 0700 (GC10).
"""

import calendar
import fcntl
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import secrets
import shlex
import shutil
import signal
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Constants, times, atomic writes, STATE and the job store (GC9, GC10, MU2, MU15-MU19, MU28)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

STATE_PATH = "~/.local/share/qwen-agent/movie-ui"   # MU2; expanded at every use
STATE_SUBDIRS = ("jobs", "uploads", "traces", "smoke")
JOB_SCHEMA = "movie-ui.job.v1"
SAVED_TRACE_SCHEMA = "movie-ui.saved-trace.v1"
JOB_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9]{8}-[0-9]{6}-[0-9a-f]{4}$")
UPLOAD_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}\.(png|jpg|webp)$")
STORY_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,79}$")
CHARACTER_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,23}$")
SMOKE_STORY_ID = "movie-ui-smoke"
CANCEL_GRACE_S = 15.0       # MU21; tests set it lower
STARTING_GRACE_S = 10.0     # MU29: a dispatched runner has this long to take its lock
ACTIVE_STATES = ("starting", "running")
FINAL_STATES = ("done", "failed", "cancelled")
FINISHED_STATES = ("done", "failed", "cancelled", "lost")
# Every regex above is applied with .fullmatch().


def utc_now():
    """The current time as UTC YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ (GC9)."""
    return time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime())


def parse_utc(text):
    """Epoch seconds for a utc_now() string, or None."""
    try:
        return calendar.timegm(time.strptime(text, "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def state_dir():
    """STATE = ~/.local/share/qwen-agent/movie-ui, host-wide (MU2)."""
    return os.path.expanduser(STATE_PATH)


def make_dir(path):
    """Create path (and missing parents) if absent; the leaf is mode 0700 (GC10)."""
    if not os.path.isdir(path):
        os.makedirs(path, 0o700, exist_ok=True)
        os.chmod(path, 0o700)


def ensure_state(state):
    """Create STATE and its jobs, uploads, traces and smoke directories (MU2)."""
    make_dir(state)
    for name in STATE_SUBDIRS:
        make_dir(os.path.join(state, name))


def _unlink_quiet(path):
    try:
        os.unlink(path)
    except OSError:
        pass


def write_bytes_atomic(path, data):
    """Write data to <path>.tmp-<pid>-<thread id>, close it, os.replace it onto path; 0600 (GC10)."""
    tmp = "%s.tmp-%d-%d" % (path, os.getpid(), threading.get_ident())
    fd = os.open(tmp, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as f:
            os.fchmod(f.fileno(), 0o600)
            f.write(data)
        os.replace(tmp, path)
    except BaseException:
        _unlink_quiet(tmp)
        raise


def write_json_atomic(path, obj):
    """obj as indented UTF-8 JSON plus a newline, written by write_bytes_atomic."""
    write_bytes_atomic(path, (json.dumps(obj, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n").encode("utf-8"))


def read_json(path, default=None):
    """The parsed JSON file at path, or default when it is missing or does not parse."""
    try:
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            return json.loads(f.read().decode("utf-8"))
    except (OSError, ValueError, RecursionError):
        return default


def jobs_root(state):
    return os.path.join(state, "jobs")


def job_dir(state, job_id):
    return os.path.join(state, "jobs", job_id)


def queue_path(state):
    return os.path.join(state, "queue.json")


def read_job(jdir):
    """job.json of one job directory as a dict, or None (MU15)."""
    job = read_json(os.path.join(jdir, "job.json"))
    return job if isinstance(job, dict) else None


def read_status(jdir):
    """status.json of one job directory as a dict, or None while queued or unreadable (MU16)."""
    status = read_json(os.path.join(jdir, "status.json"))
    return status if isinstance(status, dict) else None


def write_status(jdir, status):
    write_json_atomic(os.path.join(jdir, "status.json"), status)


def read_queue(state):
    """queue.json as a list of job IDs; missing or invalid JSON gives [] (MU18, MU31)."""
    queue = read_json(queue_path(state), [])
    return [i for i in queue if isinstance(i, str)] if isinstance(queue, list) else []


def write_queue(state, ids):
    write_json_atomic(queue_path(state), list(ids))


def list_job_ids(state):
    """Sorted IDs of every jobs/<id> directory whose name matches JOB_ID_RE and that has a
    readable job.json (MU32)."""
    try:
        names = os.listdir(jobs_root(state))
    except OSError:
        return []
    return sorted(n for n in names
                  if JOB_ID_RE.fullmatch(n) and read_job(job_dir(state, n)) is not None)


def new_job_id(state):
    """A fresh job ID: UTC %Y%m%d-%H%M%S, "-", 4 hex digits; drawn again while jobs/<id>
    exists (MU28)."""
    while True:
        job_id = time.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S", time.gmtime()) + "-" + secrets.token_hex(2)
        if not os.path.lexists(job_dir(state, job_id)):
            return job_id


def create_job(state, job_id, kind, workspace, story_id, character, argv, fields):
    """Create jobs/<id>/ with job.json and an empty log.txt and append the ID to queue.json
    (MU15, MU28). The caller holds STORE_LOCK. Returns the job.json object."""
    jdir = job_dir(state, job_id)
    os.mkdir(jdir, 0o700)
    os.chmod(jdir, 0o700)
    job = {"schema": JOB_SCHEMA, "id": job_id, "kind": kind, "workspace": workspace,
           "story_id": story_id, "character": character, "argv": list(argv),
           "fields": fields, "created": utc_now()}
    write_json_atomic(os.path.join(jdir, "job.json"), job)
    write_bytes_atomic(os.path.join(jdir, "log.txt"), b"")
    write_queue(state, read_queue(state) + [job_id])
    return job


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The job runner (MU21-MU25): bin/movie-ui-job calls run_job
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

RUNNERS = []    # Popen objects of launched runners, polled (never waited for) to reap them


def _say(text):
    """One runner line on stdout (log.txt when dispatched), prefixed "[movie-ui] " (MU17)."""
    sys.stdout.write("[movie-ui] %s\n" % text)
    sys.stdout.flush()


def launch_runner(state, job_id, ui_ws):
    """Start bin/movie-ui-job for one job in its own session, its output appended to log.txt,
    without waiting for it (MU21). Raises OSError when it cannot be started."""
    jdir = job_dir(state, job_id)
    RUNNERS[:] = [p for p in RUNNERS if p.poll() is None]
    log = open(os.path.join(jdir, "log.txt"), "ab")
    try:
        proc = subprocess.Popen(
            [sys.executable, os.path.join(ui_ws, "bin", "movie-ui-job"), "--grace", repr(CANCEL_GRACE_S), jdir],
            start_new_session=True, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            cwd=jdir, env=dict(os.environ, PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1"), close_fds=True)
    finally:
        log.close()
    RUNNERS.append(proc)
    return proc


def _killpg_quiet(group, sig):
    try:
        os.killpg(group, sig)
    except (ProcessLookupError, PermissionError):
        pass


def group_alive(group):
    """False only when os.killpg(group, 0) raises ProcessLookupError."""
    try:
        os.killpg(group, 0)
    except ProcessLookupError:
        return False
    except PermissionError:
        return True
    return True


def cancel_groups(child_pid):
    """The process groups a cancel signals: the child's own group (MU23 step 1; the sweep of
    its descendants' groups, D1, is added separately, Q1)."""
    return [child_pid]


def cancel_tree(child, grace):
    """SIGTERM every group of cancel_groups, poll every 0.2 s for grace seconds, SIGKILL the
    groups still present, then reap the child (MU23 steps 2-4)."""
    groups = cancel_groups(child.pid)
    for group in groups:
        _killpg_quiet(group, signal.SIGTERM)
    _say("cancel: SIGTERM to process group(s) %s" % " ".join(str(g) for g in groups))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + grace
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        child.poll()
        if child.returncode is not None and not any(group_alive(g) for g in groups):
            break
        time.sleep(0.2)
    child.poll()
    left = [g for g in groups if group_alive(g)]
    for group in left:
        _killpg_quiet(group, signal.SIGKILL)
    if left:
        _say("cancel: SIGKILL to process group(s) %s" % " ".join(str(g) for g in left))
    child.wait()


def run_job(jdir, grace=CANCEL_GRACE_S):
    """Run one dispatched job to its end (MU22). Returns 0 after a final status, 1 when the job
    argv could not be started, 3 when the status was not "starting" (MU24)."""
    lock_fd = os.open(os.path.join(jdir, "lock"), os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o600)
    fcntl.flock(lock_fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
    status = read_status(jdir) or {}
    if status.get("state") != "starting":
        _say("job is %s; nothing to run" % (status.get("state") or "unknown"))
        return 3
    cancel = os.path.join(jdir, "cancel")
    if os.path.exists(cancel):
        write_status(jdir, {"state": "cancelled", "exit_code": None, "ended": utc_now()})
        _say("cancelled before start")
        return 0
    job = read_job(jdir)
    log = open(os.path.join(jdir, "log.txt"), "ab")
    try:
        child = subprocess.Popen(job["argv"], cwd=job["workspace"], env=dict(os.environ, PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1"),
                                 stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                                 start_new_session=True, close_fds=True)
    except OSError as e:
        error = "cannot start: %s" % (e.strerror or e)
        write_status(jdir, {"state": "failed", "dispatched": status.get("dispatched"),
                            "runner_pid": os.getpid(), "pgid": None, "started": None,
                            "exit_code": None, "ended": utc_now(), "error": error})
        _say(error)
        return 1
    finally:
        log.close()
    running = {"state": "running", "dispatched": status.get("dispatched"), "runner_pid": os.getpid(),
               "pgid": child.pid, "started": utc_now()}
    write_status(jdir, running)
    _say("started pid %d: %s" % (child.pid, shlex.join(job["argv"])))
    signalled = False
    while True:
        try:
            child.wait(timeout=1)
            break
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            if not signalled and os.path.exists(cancel):
                signalled = True
                cancel_tree(child, grace)
    code = child.returncode
    state = "cancelled" if os.path.exists(cancel) else ("done" if code == 0 else "failed")
    final = dict(running)
    final.update({"state": state, "exit_code": code, "ended": utc_now()})
    write_status(jdir, final)
    _say("exit %d (%s)" % (code, state))
    return 0
```

**3.2.** **Create** `bin/movie-ui-job` with exactly this content:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""movie-ui-job -- run one movie-ui job detached from the server (movie-ui spec MU21-MU25).

Usage: bin/movie-ui-job [--grace SECONDS] JOB_DIR

The movie-ui dispatcher starts this in its own session with stdout and stderr appended to
JOB_DIR/log.txt; it holds JOB_DIR/lock for its lifetime, runs the job's argv in a new session,
handles the cancel marker and writes the final status.json, also while no server is running.
"""

import argparse
import os
import sys

WS = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, WS)
import movie_ui_lib  # noqa: E402


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="movie-ui-job", description="Run one movie-ui job.")
    parser.add_argument("--grace", type=float, default=movie_ui_lib.CANCEL_GRACE_S,
                        help="seconds between SIGTERM and SIGKILL on cancel (default 15.0)")
    parser.add_argument("job_dir", metavar="JOB_DIR")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    return movie_ui_lib.run_job(os.path.abspath(args.job_dir), args.grace)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

### Task 3: The cancel sweep of every descendant process group (MU23 step 1; Q1)

Implements MU23 step 1 and D1: at the first sight of the cancel marker the runner snapshots `ps -A -o pid=,ppid=,pgid=`, collects the process groups of every transitive descendant of the child, and signals them all with the TERM-then-KILL rule; a failing `ps` falls back to the child's group with a log line. Tests: MUT11's own-session grandchild half (MUK4) and the pure tree walk. **This is the whole of Q1**: skipping this task leaves the child-group-only cancel of Task 2.

**Files:**
- Modify: `tests/test_movie_ui_jobs.py`
- Modify: `movie_ui_lib.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's `cancel_tree` (calls `cancel_groups(child.pid)`), `_say`, `group_alive`; testutil `make_job`, `dispatch`, `pid_alive`, `wait_until`.
- Produces: `movie_ui_lib.process_table() -> [(pid, ppid, pgid)] or None`; `descendant_groups(root_pid, table) -> [int]` (sorted, includes `root_pid`); `cancel_groups(child_pid) -> [int]` (replaced).

```python
def process_table():
    """[(pid, ppid, pgid)] from ps -A -o pid=,ppid=,pgid= (timeout 5 s), or None when ps fails."""
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(["ps", "-A", "-o", "pid=,ppid=,pgid="], stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                              capture_output=True, timeout=5)
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return None
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        return None
    rows = []
    for line in proc.stdout.decode("ascii", "replace").splitlines():
        parts = line.split()
        if len(parts) == 3 and all(p.isdigit() for p in parts):
            rows.append((int(parts[0]), int(parts[1]), int(parts[2])))
    return rows


def descendant_groups(root_pid, table):
    """Sorted {root_pid} | {pgid of every transitive descendant of root_pid} (MU23 step 1)."""
    children = {}
    for pid, ppid, pgid in table:
        children.setdefault(ppid, []).append((pid, pgid))
    groups, seen, stack = {root_pid}, set(), [root_pid]
    while stack:
        for pid, pgid in children.get(stack.pop(), []):
            if pid not in seen:
                seen.add(pid)
                groups.add(pgid)
                stack.append(pid)
    return sorted(groups)


def cancel_groups(child_pid):
    """The process groups a cancel signals: the child's group and every process group of its
    descendants (MU23 step 1, D1, Q1)."""
    table = process_table()
    if table is None:
        _say("ps failed; signalling process group %d only" % child_pid)
        return [child_pid]
    return descendant_groups(child_pid, table)


def cancel_tree(child, grace):
```

### Task 4: Liveness by lock, the FIFO dispatcher, startup recovery and job actions

Implements MU26, MU28's queue half, MU29, MU30 (the tick; Task 11 runs it on a thread), MU31, MU32 and the store side of MU68 (cancel, move, delete, kill-orphan). Tests: MUT13 and MUT14 at the library level (their HTTP halves are in Task 12) and Review Focus 3.

**Files:**
- Modify: `tests/test_movie_ui_jobs.py`
- Modify: `movie_ui_lib.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's store and runner (`read_status`, `write_status`, `read_queue`, `write_queue`, `list_job_ids`, `job_dir`, `read_job`, `launch_runner`, `group_alive`, `_killpg_quiet`, `parse_utc`, `utc_now`, `write_bytes_atomic`).
- Produces (every function that reads or writes the queue or a status is called with `movie_ui_server.STORE_LOCK` held): `job_alive(jdir, now=None) -> bool`; `active_job_ids(state) -> [str]`; `dispatch_tick(state, ui_ws) -> job_id or None`; `startup_recovery(state)`; `job_state(state, job_id, queue) -> str` (status state, else `"queued"` when in `queue`, else `"unknown"`); `cancel_job(state, job_id) -> "cancelled" | "cancelling" | None`; `move_job(state, job_id, direction) -> [str] or None`; `delete_job(state, job_id) -> bool`; `orphan_info(status) -> {"pgid", "alive": True} or None`; `kill_orphan(state, job_id) -> pgid or None`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

**1.1.** **Append** to `tests/test_movie_ui_jobs.py`:

```python
def _lock_held(state, job_id):
    fd = os.open(os.path.join(lib.job_dir(state, job_id), "lock"), os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o600)
    try:
        fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    except BlockingIOError:
        return True
    finally:
        os.close(fd)
    return False


def test_mut13_liveness_recovery_lost_and_orphans(state, tmp_path):
    ready = _ready_file(tmp_path, "ready")
    job_id = mt.make_job(state, mt.py_argv("import time; open(%r, 'w').write('1'); time.sleep(60)" % ready),
                         str(tmp_path))
    runner = mt.dispatch(state, job_id)
    mt.wait_until(lambda: os.path.exists(ready), 10)
    jdir = lib.job_dir(state, job_id)
    assert lib.job_alive(jdir) is True
    lib.startup_recovery(state)
    assert mt.status_of(state, job_id)["state"] == "running"
    pgid = mt.status_of(state, job_id)["pgid"]
    os.kill(runner.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
    runner.wait(5)
    assert lib.job_alive(jdir) is False
    lost = mt.status_of(state, job_id)
    assert lost["state"] == "lost" and lost["exit_code"] is None and lost["ended"] is None
    assert lost["pgid"] == pgid and lib.parse_utc(lost["detected"]) is not None
    assert lib.orphan_info(lost) == {"pgid": pgid, "alive": True}
    assert lib.kill_orphan(state, job_id) == pgid
    mt.wait_until(lambda: not lib.group_alive(pgid), 5)
    assert lib.orphan_info(mt.status_of(state, job_id)) is None
    assert lib.kill_orphan(state, job_id) is None
    # A running job with no runner at startup, and a starting job past the 10 s grace, are lost.
    dead = mt.make_job(state, ["true"], str(tmp_path), status={"state": "running", "dispatched": lib.utc_now(),
                                                               "runner_pid": 1, "pgid": None, "started": lib.utc_now()})
    old = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime(time.time() - 60))
    stale = mt.make_job(state, ["true"], str(tmp_path), status={"state": "starting", "dispatched": old})
    fresh = mt.make_job(state, ["true"], str(tmp_path), status={"state": "starting", "dispatched": lib.utc_now()})
    lib.startup_recovery(state)
    assert mt.status_of(state, dead)["state"] == "lost"
    assert mt.status_of(state, stale)["state"] == "lost"
    assert mt.status_of(state, fresh)["state"] == "starting"
    assert lib.job_alive(lib.job_dir(state, fresh)) is True


def test_mut14_queue_fifo_one_runner_at_a_time_and_actions(state, tmp_path):
    ws = str(tmp_path)
    ids = [mt.make_job(state, mt.py_argv("import time; time.sleep(0.6)"), ws) for _ in range(3)]
    assert lib.read_queue(state) == ids
    launched, max_held = [], 0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 30
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        got = lib.dispatch_tick(state, mt.WS)
        if got:
            launched.append(got)
        max_held = max(max_held, sum(1 for i in ids if _lock_held(state, i)))
        if all(mt.status_of(state, i).get("state") == "done" for i in ids):
            break
        time.sleep(0.1)
    assert launched == ids
    assert max_held == 1
    starts = [mt.status_of(state, i)["started"] for i in ids]
    assert starts == sorted(starts)
    # move, cancel, no-ops, and the queue across a restart
    a, b, c = (mt.make_job(state, ["true"], ws) for _ in range(3))
    assert lib.move_job(state, c, "up") == [a, c, b]
    assert lib.move_job(state, a, "up") == [a, c, b]
    assert lib.move_job(state, b, "down") == [a, c, b]
    assert lib.move_job(state, ids[0], "up") is None
    assert lib.cancel_job(state, c) == "cancelled"
    assert lib.read_queue(state) == [a, b]
    assert mt.status_of(state, c) == {"state": "cancelled", "exit_code": None, "ended": mt.status_of(state, c)["ended"]}
    assert lib.cancel_job(state, c) is None
    lib.write_queue(state, lib.read_queue(state) + ["bogus", c, a, "20261019-000000-ffff"])
    lib.startup_recovery(state)
    assert lib.read_queue(state) == [a, b]
    assert lib.delete_job(state, a) is False
    assert lib.delete_job(state, c) is True and not os.path.exists(lib.job_dir(state, c))
    # a job whose workspace was removed by self-update
    gone = os.path.join(ws, "pruned-release")
    os.mkdir(gone)
    lost_ws = mt.make_job(state, ["true"], gone)
    lib.write_queue(state, [lost_ws, a, b])
    os.rmdir(gone)
    assert lib.dispatch_tick(state, mt.WS) == a
    assert mt.status_of(state, lost_ws) == {"state": "failed", "exit_code": None,
                                            "ended": mt.status_of(state, lost_ws)["ended"],
                                            "error": "workspace no longer exists (removed by self-update)"}
    assert lib.read_queue(state) == [b]


def test_review_focus_3_cancel_while_starting(state, tmp_path):
    """Review Focus 3: Cancel pressed in the instant between dispatch and the runner's start."""
    job_id = mt.make_job(state, mt.py_argv("open(%r, 'w').write('ran')" % _ready_file(tmp_path, "ran")),
                         str(tmp_path))
    jdir = lib.job_dir(state, job_id)
    lib.write_queue(state, [])
    lib.write_status(jdir, {"state": "starting", "dispatched": lib.utc_now()})
    assert lib.cancel_job(state, job_id) == "cancelling"
    proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(mt.WS, "bin", "movie-ui-job"), jdir],
                          capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    assert (proc.returncode, proc.stdout) == (0, "[movie-ui] cancelled before start\n")
    status = lib.read_status(jdir)
    assert status == {"state": "cancelled", "exit_code": None, "ended": status["ended"]}
    assert not os.path.exists(_ready_file(tmp_path, "ran"))
```

Failing (observed): `test_mut13_liveness_recovery_lost_and_orphans`, `test_mut14_queue_fifo_one_runner_at_a_time_and_actions`, `test_review_focus_3_cancel_while_starting`.

**3.1.** **Append** to `movie_ui_lib.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Liveness, the dispatcher, startup recovery and job actions (MU26, MU29-MU31, MU68).
# Every function here that reads or writes queue.json or a status is called with STORE_LOCK
# held (movie_ui_server.STORE_LOCK).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def job_alive(jdir, now=None):
    """True while a runner holds jdir/lock, or the job is "starting" and was dispatched less than
    STARTING_GRACE_S ago; otherwise False, after writing the "lost" status when the job was
    starting or running (MU29). Never consults a PID (MU25)."""
    fd = os.open(os.path.join(jdir, "lock"), os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o600)
    try:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:
            return True
        try:
            status = read_status(jdir) or {}
            state = status.get("state")
            if state not in ACTIVE_STATES:
                return False
            if state == "starting":
                dispatched = parse_utc(status.get("dispatched"))
                current = time.time() if now is None else now
                if dispatched is not None and current - dispatched < STARTING_GRACE_S:
                    return True
            lost = dict(status)
            lost.update({"state": "lost", "exit_code": None, "ended": None, "detected": utc_now()})
            write_status(jdir, lost)
            return False
        finally:
            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    finally:
        os.close(fd)


def active_job_ids(state):
    """IDs of the jobs whose status state is "starting" or "running"."""
    return [i for i in list_job_ids(state)
            if (read_status(job_dir(state, i)) or {}).get("state") in ACTIVE_STATES]


def dispatch_tick(state, ui_ws):
    """One dispatcher tick (MU30). Returns the ID of the job it dispatched, else None."""
    for job_id in active_job_ids(state):
        if job_alive(job_dir(state, job_id)):
            return None
    queue = read_queue(state)
    while queue:
        job_id = queue.pop(0)
        write_queue(state, queue)
        jdir = job_dir(state, job_id)
        job = read_job(jdir)
        if job is None:
            continue
        if not os.path.isdir(job["workspace"]):
            write_status(jdir, {"state": "failed", "exit_code": None, "ended": utc_now(),
                                "error": "workspace no longer exists (removed by self-update)"})
            continue
        dispatched = utc_now()
        write_status(jdir, {"state": "starting", "dispatched": dispatched})
        try:
            launch_runner(state, job_id, ui_ws)
        except OSError as e:
            write_status(jdir, {"state": "failed", "dispatched": dispatched, "exit_code": None,
                                "ended": utc_now(), "error": "cannot start runner: %s" % (e.strerror or e)})
        return job_id
    return None


def startup_recovery(state):
    """Rewrite queue.json without IDs that are malformed, duplicated, lack job.json or already
    have a status; then re-attach or mark lost every starting/running job (MU31)."""
    kept = []
    for job_id in read_queue(state):
        jdir = job_dir(state, job_id)
        if (JOB_ID_RE.fullmatch(job_id) and job_id not in kept
                and os.path.isfile(os.path.join(jdir, "job.json"))
                and not os.path.lexists(os.path.join(jdir, "status.json"))):
            kept.append(job_id)
    write_queue(state, kept)
    for job_id in active_job_ids(state):
        job_alive(job_dir(state, job_id))


def job_state(state, job_id, queue):
    """The status state, else "queued" when job_id is in queue, else "unknown" (MU67)."""
    status = read_status(job_dir(state, job_id))
    if status is not None and isinstance(status.get("state"), str):
        return status["state"]
    return "queued" if job_id in queue else "unknown"


def cancel_job(state, job_id):
    """"cancelled" for a queued job (removed from the queue), "cancelling" for a starting or
    running one (the cancel marker is created), None otherwise (MU68)."""
    queue = read_queue(state)
    current = job_state(state, job_id, queue)
    jdir = job_dir(state, job_id)
    if current == "queued":
        write_queue(state, [i for i in queue if i != job_id])
        write_status(jdir, {"state": "cancelled", "exit_code": None, "ended": utc_now()})
        return "cancelled"
    if current in ACTIVE_STATES:
        write_bytes_atomic(os.path.join(jdir, "cancel"), b"")
        return "cancelling"
    return None


def move_job(state, job_id, direction):
    """Swap a queued job with its neighbour ("up" or "down"; a no-op at an end). Returns the new
    queue, or None when the job is not queued (MU68)."""
    queue = read_queue(state)
    if job_id not in queue or job_state(state, job_id, queue) != "queued":
        return None
    i = queue.index(job_id)
    j = i - 1 if direction == "up" else i + 1
    if 0 <= j < len(queue):
        queue[i], queue[j] = queue[j], queue[i]
        write_queue(state, queue)
    return queue


def delete_job(state, job_id):
    """Remove a finished (done, failed, cancelled, lost) job's directory; True when removed,
    False when the job is not finished (MU68). Story outputs are never touched."""
    if job_state(state, job_id, read_queue(state)) not in FINISHED_STATES:
        return False
    shutil.rmtree(job_dir(state, job_id), ignore_errors=True)
    return True


def orphan_info(status):
    """{"pgid": G, "alive": True} for a lost job whose recorded group still exists, else None
    (MU26)."""
    if not isinstance(status, dict) or status.get("state") != "lost":
        return None
    pgid = status.get("pgid")
    if not isinstance(pgid, int) or isinstance(pgid, bool) or pgid <= 1:
        return None
    return {"pgid": pgid, "alive": True} if group_alive(pgid) else None


def kill_orphan(state, job_id):
    """SIGKILL a lost job's live orphan group; returns the pgid, or None when there is none (MU68)."""
    info = orphan_info(read_status(job_dir(state, job_id)))
    if info is None:
        return None
    _killpg_quiet(info["pgid"], signal.SIGKILL)
    return info["pgid"]
```

### Task 5: Compose fields, the command builder, light subprocesses and version-aware help

Implements MU36 (the field object and its defaults), MU37 (`help_flags`, its cache and the field-to-flag map), MU39, MU40, MU41, MU47's `supported`/`defaults` data and MU59's `run_light`; creates the two help fixtures and the stub workspace of spec 14.2. Tests: MUT1 (except the smoke argv, Task 17), MUT3's parser, fixture and cache half.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/test_movie_ui_builder.py`
- Modify: `tests/movie_ui_testutil.py`
- Modify: `movie_ui_lib.py`
- Create (generated by a Run step): `tests/fixtures/movie_ui/ltx-movie-help-current.txt`
- Create (generated by a Run step): `tests/fixtures/movie_ui/ltx-movie-help-old.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing beyond Task 2's module header.
- Produces, in `movie_ui_lib`: `DEFAULT_MODEL`, `DEFAULT_GEMMA`, `STILLS_BASE_MODEL = "Tongyi-MAI/Z-Image-Turbo"`, `ZIMAGE_TEXT_ENCODER`, `ACTIONS = ("story", "regenerate", "movie", "redo")` (Task 17 adds `"smoke"`), `MODES`, `LORA_KINDS`, `OPTION_DEFAULTS`, `DEFAULT_FIELDS`, `LIGHT_TIMEOUT = 60`, `FIELD_FLAGS` (26 `(key, flags)` pairs); `compose_fields(body) -> dict`; `goal_text(goal) -> str`; `ordered(loras) -> list`; `lora_arg(item) -> "PATH:STRENGTH"`; `build_argv(fields, ws_real, state, py=sys.executable) -> [str]`; `character_argv(kind, fields, ws_real, state, py=sys.executable) -> [str]`; `run_light(argv, cwd, env=None, timeout=None) -> {"argv", "exit_code", "stdout", "stderr", "timed_out"}`; `tool_argv(ws_real, tool, *args)`; `last_line(text) -> str`; `parse_help_flags(text) -> set`; `help_flags(ws_real) -> (frozenset or None, error or None)`; `supported_map(flags) -> {key: bool}`.
- Produces, in testutil: `STUB_TOOLS`, `STUB_SOURCE`, `make_stub_workspace(tmp) -> ws realpath`, `stub_config(ws, config)`, `stub_calls(ws) -> [dict]`, `fields(ws, **overrides) -> dict` (a valid story-action field object for story `ronin-rescue`). Stub behaviour is documented in `make_stub_workspace`'s docstring; config keys are `"<tool> <every argument>"`, `"<tool> <sub>"` or `"<tool>"`.

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The stub workspace
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

STUB_TOOLS = ("ltx-movie", "lora-catalog", "character", "ltx-mlx-render", "story-server")

STUB_SOURCE = r'''
import json
import os
import sys
import time

WS = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(__file__)))
FIXTURES = __FIXTURES__
args = sys.argv[1:]
if TOOL in ("ltx-movie", "ltx-mlx-render"):
    sub = "--help" if "--help" in args else ("--dry-run" if "--dry-run" in args else "run")
else:
    sub = args[0] if args else ""
with open(os.path.join(WS, "stub_calls.jsonl"), "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(json.dumps({"tool": TOOL, "sub": sub, "argv": args, "cwd": os.getcwd(),
                        "env": {k: os.environ.get(k) for k in ("STORY_PIPELINE_LOGGED", "PYTHONUNBUFFERED",
                                                               "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")}}) + "\n")
try:
    with open(os.path.join(WS, "stub_config.json"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
        config = json.load(f)
except (OSError, ValueError):
    config = {}
entry = config.get(" ".join([TOOL] + args), config.get(TOOL + " " + sub, config.get(TOOL)))


def opt(name, default=None):
    return args[args.index(name) + 1] if name in args and args.index(name) + 1 < len(args) else default


def emit(text, delay=0.0):
    for line in text.splitlines(True):
        sys.stdout.write(line)
        sys.stdout.flush()
        if delay:
            time.sleep(delay)


def write_file(path, data):
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "wb") as f:
        f.write(data)


if entry is not None:
    time.sleep(entry.get("sleep", 0))
    for rel, text in entry.get("write", {}).items():
        write_file(os.path.join(WS, rel), text.encode("utf-8"))
    emit(entry.get("stdout", ""))
    sys.stderr.write(entry.get("stderr", ""))
    sys.exit(entry.get("exit", 0))
if TOOL == "ltx-movie" and sub == "--help":
    with open(os.path.join(FIXTURES, "ltx-movie-help-current.txt"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
        emit(f.read())
    sys.exit(0)
if TOOL == "ltx-movie" and sub == "run":
    sdir = os.path.join(WS, "generated", "stories", opt("--story-id", "story"))
    panels = int(opt("--panels", "2"))
    if "--story-only" in args:
        emit("=== Phase 1: story ===\n", 0.05)
        text = "".join("## Panel %d -- Shot %d\nImage: a quiet road.\nMotion: the camera drifts.\n\n" % (i, i)
                       for i in range(1, panels + 1))
        write_file(os.path.join(sdir, "story.md"), text.encode("utf-8"))
        emit("story.md written: %s\n" % os.path.join(sdir, "story.md"))
        sys.exit(0)
    for phase in ("=== Phase 1: story ===", "=== Phase 1b: release the story server ===", "=== Phase 2: stills ==="):
        emit(phase + "\n", 0.05)
    write_file(os.path.join(sdir, "images", "panel_01.png"), b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n")
    emit("=== Phase 3: manifest + dry-run ===\npanels: %d\n=== Phase 4: render ===\n" % panels, 0.05)
    for i in range(1, panels + 1):
        emit("=== panel %d/%d (panel-%d) ===\n" % (i, panels, i), 0.1)
        write_file(os.path.join(sdir, "clips", "panel_%02d.mp4" % i), b"\x00\x00\x00\x18ftypmp42")
        emit("panel %d ok: %s (0.1s)\n" % (i, os.path.join(sdir, "clips", "panel_%02d.mp4" % i)), 0.05)
    write_file(os.path.join(sdir, "movie.mp4"), b"\x00\x00\x00\x18ftypmp42")
    emit("=== render summary (ok) ===\n")
    sys.exit(0)
if TOOL == "ltx-mlx-render" and sub == "run":
    clips = opt("--clips-dir")
    emit("=== panel 1/1 (panel-1) ===\n", 0.05)
    write_file(os.path.join(clips, "panel_01.mp4"), b"\x00\x00\x00\x18ftypmp42")
    emit("panel 1 ok: %s (0.1s)\n" % os.path.join(clips, "panel_01.mp4"))
    sys.exit(0)
if TOOL == "lora-catalog" and sub == "list":
    emit(json.dumps({"catalog": os.path.join(WS, "generated", "loras", "catalog.json"), "entries": [],
                     "characters": []}) + "\n")
    sys.exit(0)
if TOOL == "lora-catalog" and sub == "verify":
    for entry_id in args[1:]:
        emit("OK %s\n" % entry_id)
    emit("verified %d entries: 0 problem(s)\n" % len(args[1:]))
    sys.exit(0)
if TOOL == "character" and sub == "list":
    emit("no characters in %s\n" % os.path.join(WS, "generated", "characters"))
    sys.exit(0)
if TOOL == "story-server" and sub == "status":
    emit("story-server status  2026-10-19T00:00:00Z\n  state:          SERVING vision\n")
    sys.exit(0)
sys.exit(0)
'''


def _write_exec(path, text):
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(text)
    os.chmod(path, os.stat(path).st_mode | stat.S_IXUSR | stat.S_IXGRP | stat.S_IXOTH)


def make_stub_workspace(tmp):
    """<tmp>/ws with executable stubs of ltx-movie, lora-catalog, character, ltx-mlx-render (Python)
    and story-server (sh, running bin/.story-server.py); returns the workspace realpath.

    Each stub appends {"tool", "sub", "argv", "cwd", "env"} as one JSON line to
    <ws>/stub_calls.jsonl and obeys <ws>/stub_config.json: {key: {"exit", "stdout", "stderr",
    "sleep", "write": {relpath: text}}}, where the first key found of "<tool> <every argument>",
    "<tool> <sub>" and "<tool>" applies. sub is the first argument, or for ltx-movie and
    ltx-mlx-render "--help", "--dry-run" or "run". Without a
    config entry: ltx-movie --help prints the current help fixture; ltx-movie runs write story.md
    (--story-only) or stills, clips and movie.mp4 with the real progress markers; lora-catalog list
    prints an empty catalog; story-server status reports SERVING vision; everything else exits 0.
    """
    ws = os.path.join(str(tmp), "ws")
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(ws, "bin"), exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(ws, "generated", "stories"), exist_ok=True)
    body = STUB_SOURCE.replace("__FIXTURES__", repr(FIXTURES))
    for tool in STUB_TOOLS:
        source = "#!%s\nTOOL = %r\n%s" % (sys.executable, tool, body)
        if tool == "story-server":
            _write_exec(os.path.join(ws, "bin", ".story-server.py"), source)
            _write_exec(os.path.join(ws, "bin", "story-server"),
                        "#!/bin/sh\nexec '%s' \"$(dirname \"$0\")/.story-server.py\" \"$@\"\n" % sys.executable)
        else:
            _write_exec(os.path.join(ws, "bin", tool), source)
    return os.path.realpath(ws)


def stub_config(ws, config):
    with open(os.path.join(ws, "stub_config.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(config, f)


def stub_calls(ws):
    try:
        with open(os.path.join(ws, "stub_calls.jsonl"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return [json.loads(line) for line in f if line.strip()]
    except OSError:
        return []


def fields(ws, **overrides):
    """A valid MU36 field object for ws (action story) with overrides applied."""
    out = movie_ui_lib.compose_fields({"ws": ws, "story_id": "ronin-rescue",
                                       "narrative": "A ronin rescues a child from a burning village."})
    out.update(overrides)
    return out
```

```bash
import sys

DROP = ("--story-only", "--story-legacy", "--no-auto-lora", "--judge", "--goal", "--gemma")
out, skipping = [], False
with open(sys.argv[1], encoding="utf-8") as f:

for line in lines:
    if line.startswith("  -"):
        head = re.split(r"\s{2,}", line[2:].rstrip("\n"), maxsplit=1)[0]
        skipping = any(flag in re.findall(r"--[a-z][a-z0-9-]*", head) for flag in DROP)

EOF
```

```python
"""movie-ui command builder and version-aware form tests (movie-ui spec MUT1, MUT3, MUT25, MUT26).
Run from the workspace root: python3 -m pytest tests/test_movie_ui_builder.py -v"""

import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

import movie_ui_testutil as mt
import movie_ui_lib as lib

PY = "/py"
WS = "/w"
STATE = "/s"
LTX = "/w/bin/ltx-movie"


def _f(**overrides):
    fields = lib.compose_fields({"story_id": "ronin", "narrative": "  A ronin walks.  "})
    fields.update(overrides)
    return fields


def _lora(kind, path, strength=0.8, lora_id=None):
    return {"id": lora_id, "path": path, "strength": strength, "kind": kind}


def test_mut1_golden_argv():
    base = [PY, LTX, "A ronin walks.", "--story-id", "ronin"]
    defaults = ["--fps", "24", "--frames", "145", "--model", lib.DEFAULT_MODEL, "--gemma", lib.DEFAULT_GEMMA]
    cases = [
        (_f(), base + ["--panels", "15"] + defaults + ["--story-only", "--no-review"]),
        (_f(action="regenerate"), base + ["--panels", "15"] + defaults + ["--force-story", "--story-only", "--no-review"]),
        (_f(action="movie", panels=6), base + ["--panels", "6"] + defaults + ["--no-review"]),
        (_f(action="movie", panels=None, length=30), base + ["--length", "30.0"] + defaults + ["--no-review"]),
        (_f(action="movie", seed_upload="ab.png", width=None),
         base + ["--panels", "15", "--fps", "24", "--frames", "145", "--seed-image", "/s/uploads/ab.png",
                 "--model", lib.DEFAULT_MODEL, "--gemma", lib.DEFAULT_GEMMA, "--no-review"]),
        (_f(action="movie", width=768, height=512),
         base + ["--panels", "15", "--fps", "24", "--frames", "145", "--video-width", "768", "--video-height", "512",
                 "--model", lib.DEFAULT_MODEL, "--gemma", lib.DEFAULT_GEMMA, "--no-review"]),
        (_f(action="redo", mode="shots", redo=[7, 3, 3]),
         base + ["--panels", "15"] + defaults + ["--shots", "--redo", "3,7", "--no-review"]),
        (_f(action="movie", mode="no_stills"), base + ["--panels", "15"] + defaults + ["--no-stills", "--no-review"]),
        (_f(action="movie", video_loras=[_lora("style", "/l/style.safetensors", 1.0, "ink"),
                                         _lora("motion", "/l/pan.safetensors", 0.75, "pan"),
                                         _lora(None, "/l/free.safetensors", 0.5),
                                         _lora("physics", "/l/water.safetensors", 1.5, "water")]),
         base + ["--panels", "15"] + defaults + ["--lora", "/l/pan.safetensors:0.75", "--lora", "/l/water.safetensors:1.5",
                                                 "--lora", "/l/style.safetensors:1.0", "--lora", "/l/free.safetensors:0.5",
                                                 "--no-review"]),
        (_f(action="movie", stills_loras=[_lora(None, "/l/s.safetensors", 2), _lora("style", "/l/t.safetensors", 1, "t")]),
         base + ["--panels", "15"] + defaults + ["--stills-lora", "/l/s.safetensors:2.0",
                                                 "--stills-lora", "/l/t.safetensors:1.0", "--no-review"]),
        (_f(action="movie", characters=["kyra", "ronin"]),
         base + ["--panels", "15"] + defaults + ["--character", "kyra", "--character", "ronin", "--no-review"]),
        (_f(action="movie", model=None, gemma=None, story_model="qwen38", goal=" Make it tense. ", auto_lora=False,
            judge=True, no_low_ram=True, panel_timeout=900, overwrite_movie=True),
         base + ["--panels", "15", "--fps", "24", "--frames", "145", "--story-model", "qwen38",
                 "--goal", "Make it tense", "--no-auto-lora", "--judge", "--no-low-ram", "--panel-timeout", "900",
                 "--force", "--no-review"]),
        (_f(story_legacy=True), base + ["--panels", "15"] + defaults + ["--story-legacy", "--story-only", "--no-review"]),
    ]
    for fields, expected in cases:
        assert lib.build_argv(fields, WS, STATE, py=PY) == expected
        assert lib.build_argv(fields, WS, STATE, py=PY)[-1] == "--no-review"


def test_mut1_character_argv():
    c = [PY, "/w/bin/character"]
    create = {"name": "kyra", "phrase": "the young woman", "seed_upload": "ab.jpg", "descriptor": None,
              "class_noun": None, "trigger": None, "seed": None, "force": False}
    assert lib.character_argv("character-create", create, WS, STATE, py=PY) == c + [
        "create", "kyra", "--phrase", "the young woman", "--seed-image", "/s/uploads/ab.jpg"]
    create.update(seed_upload=None, descriptor="a tall woman", class_noun="woman", trigger="kyrax", seed=0, force=True)
    assert lib.character_argv("character-create", create, WS, STATE, py=PY) == c + [
        "create", "kyra", "--phrase", "the young woman", "--descriptor", "a tall woman", "--class", "woman",
        "--trigger", "kyrax", "--seed", "0", "--force"]
    assert lib.character_argv("character-regenerate", {"name": "kyra"}, WS, STATE, py=PY) == c + [
        "create", "kyra", "--regenerate"]
    assert lib.character_argv("character-train", {"name": "kyra", "video": True, "stills": False, "force": False},
                              WS, STATE, py=PY) == c + ["train", "kyra", "--video"]
    assert lib.character_argv("character-train", {"name": "kyra", "video": True, "stills": True, "force": True},
                              WS, STATE, py=PY) == c + ["train", "kyra", "--video", "--stills", "--force"]


def test_mu36_compose_fields_defaults_and_unknown_keys():
    fields = lib.compose_fields({"story_id": "x", "bogus": 1, "video_loras": [{"id": None}]})
    assert set(fields) == set(lib.DEFAULT_FIELDS)
    assert fields["story_id"] == "x" and fields["panels"] == 15 and fields["mode"] == "chained"
    assert fields["model"] == lib.DEFAULT_MODEL and fields["redo"] == []
    assert lib.ordered([_lora(None, "/a"), _lora("motion", "/b")]) == [_lora("motion", "/b"), _lora(None, "/a")]


ALL_MU37_FLAGS = {"--story-id", "--panels", "--length", "--fps", "--frames", "--video-width", "--video-height",
                  "--seed-image", "--model", "--gemma", "--story-model", "--goal", "--shots", "--no-stills",
                  "--lora", "--stills-lora", "--character", "--no-auto-lora", "--story-legacy", "--judge",
                  "--no-low-ram", "--panel-timeout", "--force", "--force-story", "--redo", "--story-only",
                  "--no-review"}


def test_mut3_help_flags_fixtures_real_help_and_cache(tmp_path):
    with open(os.path.join(mt.FIXTURES, "ltx-movie-help-current.txt"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
        current = lib.parse_help_flags(f.read())
    with open(os.path.join(mt.FIXTURES, "ltx-movie-help-old.txt"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
        old = lib.parse_help_flags(f.read())
    assert ALL_MU37_FLAGS <= current
    assert {"--story-server-stop-after-story", "--no-story-server-stop-after-story", "--help"} <= current
    assert current - old == {"--story-only", "--story-legacy", "--no-auto-lora", "--judge", "--goal", "--gemma"}
    real, error = lib.help_flags(mt.WS)
    assert error is None and ALL_MU37_FLAGS <= real
    supported = lib.supported_map(old)
    assert supported["judge"] is False and supported["story_only"] is False and supported["panels"] is True
    assert set(supported) == {k for k, _flags in lib.FIELD_FLAGS} and len(supported) == 26
    assert lib.supported_map(None)["panels"] is False
    ws = mt.make_stub_workspace(tmp_path)
    flags, error = lib.help_flags(ws)
    assert error is None and flags == frozenset(current)
    mt.stub_config(ws, {"ltx-movie --help": {"stdout": "  --panels N   x\n"}})
    assert lib.help_flags(ws)[0] == frozenset(current)
    os.utime(os.path.join(ws, "bin", "ltx-movie"), ns=(10 ** 9, 10 ** 9))
    assert lib.help_flags(ws)[0] == frozenset({"--panels"})
    mt.stub_config(ws, {"ltx-movie --help": {"exit": 2, "stderr": "Traceback\nImportError: no torch\n"}})
    os.utime(os.path.join(ws, "bin", "ltx-movie"), ns=(2 * 10 ** 9, 2 * 10 ** 9))
    assert lib.help_flags(ws) == (None, "cannot read bin/ltx-movie --help (exit 2): ImportError: no torch")
    assert [c["sub"] for c in mt.stub_calls(ws)] == ["--help", "--help", "--help"]
```
