**3.1.** **Append** to `movie_ui_lib.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Compose fields, the command builder and light subprocesses (MU36-MU41, MU59)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

DEFAULT_MODEL = "MLXBits/ltx-2.3-10eros-v1.2-dmd-mlx-q8"     # bin/ltx-movie:318
DEFAULT_GEMMA = "mlx-community/gemma-3-12b-it-4bit"          # bin/ltx-movie:319-323
STILLS_BASE_MODEL = "Tongyi-MAI/Z-Image-Turbo"               # lora_catalog.py:29
ZIMAGE_TEXT_ENCODER = "BennyDaBall/Qwen3-4b-Z-Image-Turbo-AbliteratedV1"   # z_image_skill.py:53
ACTIONS = ("story", "regenerate", "movie", "redo")
MODES = ("chained", "shots", "no_stills")
LORA_KINDS = ("style", "motion", "physics")
OPTION_DEFAULTS = {"panels": 15, "fps": 24, "frames": 145, "model": DEFAULT_MODEL,
                   "gemma": DEFAULT_GEMMA, "panel_timeout": 7200}   # MU47
DEFAULT_FIELDS = {
    "ws": None, "action": "story",
    "story_id": "", "narrative": "", "goal": None,
    "panels": 15, "length": None, "fps": 24, "frames": 145,
    "width": None, "height": None, "seed_upload": None,
    "mode": "chained",
    "model": DEFAULT_MODEL, "gemma": DEFAULT_GEMMA,
    "story_model": None,
    "auto_lora": True,
    "video_loras": [], "stills_loras": [],
    "characters": [],
    "story_legacy": False, "judge": False, "no_low_ram": False, "panel_timeout": None,
    "overwrite_movie": False, "redo": [],
}
LIGHT_TIMEOUT = 60
# MU37: the flags each compose field or action needs; "supported" (MU47) is all-present.
FIELD_FLAGS = (
    ("story_id", ("--story-id",)), ("panels", ("--panels",)), ("length", ("--length",)),
    ("fps", ("--fps",)), ("frames", ("--frames",)),
    ("resolution", ("--video-width", "--video-height")), ("seed_upload", ("--seed-image",)),
    ("model", ("--model",)), ("gemma", ("--gemma",)), ("story_model", ("--story-model",)),
    ("goal", ("--goal",)), ("shots", ("--shots",)), ("no_stills", ("--no-stills",)),
    ("video_loras", ("--lora",)), ("stills_loras", ("--stills-lora",)),
    ("characters", ("--character",)), ("auto_lora", ("--no-auto-lora",)),
    ("story_legacy", ("--story-legacy",)), ("judge", ("--judge",)), ("no_low_ram", ("--no-low-ram",)),
    ("panel_timeout", ("--panel-timeout",)), ("overwrite_movie", ("--force",)),
    ("regenerate", ("--force-story",)), ("redo", ("--redo",)), ("story_only", ("--story-only",)),
    ("no_review", ("--no-review",)),
)


def compose_fields(body):
    """The MU36 field object: every DEFAULT_FIELDS key, taken from body when present (unknown
    keys are ignored). Values are not validated here (V1 does that)."""
    fields = {}
    for key, default in DEFAULT_FIELDS.items():
        value = body.get(key, default) if isinstance(body, dict) else default
        fields[key] = list(value) if isinstance(value, list) else value
    return fields


def goal_text(goal):
    """--goal's text as bin/ltx-movie's _parse_goal leaves it: stripped, one trailing "." removed
    and right-stripped (V1 rule 3)."""
    text = goal.strip()
    if text.endswith("."):
        text = text[:-1].rstrip()
    return text


def ordered(loras):
    """motion and physics items first, then every other item, each in submitted order (MU39)."""
    return ([item for item in loras if item.get("kind") in ("motion", "physics")]
            + [item for item in loras if item.get("kind") not in ("motion", "physics")])


def lora_arg(item):
    """PATH:STRENGTH for one LoRA item, the strength as repr(float(s)) (MU40 step 8)."""
    return "%s:%s" % (item["path"], repr(float(item["strength"])))


def build_argv(fields, ws_real, state, py=sys.executable):
    """The bin/ltx-movie argv for actions story, regenerate, movie and redo (MU40). Pure; the
    fields have passed V1."""
    action = fields["action"]
    argv = [py, os.path.join(ws_real, "bin", "ltx-movie"), fields["narrative"].strip(),
            "--story-id", fields["story_id"]]
    if fields["panels"] is not None:
        argv += ["--panels", str(fields["panels"])]
    else:
        argv += ["--length", repr(float(fields["length"]))]
    argv += ["--fps", str(fields["fps"]), "--frames", str(fields["frames"])]
    if fields["seed_upload"]:
        argv += ["--seed-image", os.path.join(state, "uploads", fields["seed_upload"])]
    elif fields["width"] is not None and fields["height"] is not None:
        argv += ["--video-width", str(fields["width"]), "--video-height", str(fields["height"])]
    for key, flag in (("model", "--model"), ("gemma", "--gemma"), ("story_model", "--story-model")):
        if fields[key] is not None:
            argv += [flag, fields[key]]
    if fields["goal"] is not None:
        argv += ["--goal", goal_text(fields["goal"])]
    if fields["mode"] == "shots":
        argv.append("--shots")
    elif fields["mode"] == "no_stills":
        argv.append("--no-stills")
    for item in ordered(fields["video_loras"]):
        argv += ["--lora", lora_arg(item)]
    for item in ordered(fields["stills_loras"]):
        argv += ["--stills-lora", lora_arg(item)]
    for name in fields["characters"]:
        argv += ["--character", name]
    if fields["auto_lora"] is False:
        argv.append("--no-auto-lora")
    for key, flag in (("story_legacy", "--story-legacy"), ("judge", "--judge"), ("no_low_ram", "--no-low-ram")):
        if fields[key]:
            argv.append(flag)
    if fields["panel_timeout"] is not None:
        argv += ["--panel-timeout", str(fields["panel_timeout"])]
    if fields["overwrite_movie"]:
        argv.append("--force")
    if action == "regenerate":
        argv.append("--force-story")
    if action == "redo":
        argv += ["--redo", ",".join(str(n) for n in sorted(set(fields["redo"])))]
    if action in ("story", "regenerate"):
        argv.append("--story-only")
    argv.append("--no-review")
    return argv


def character_argv(kind, fields, ws_real, state, py=sys.executable):
    """The bin/character argv of a character-create, -regenerate or -train job (MU41)."""
    argv = [py, os.path.join(ws_real, "bin", "character")]
    if kind == "character-regenerate":
        return argv + ["create", fields["name"], "--regenerate"]
    if kind == "character-train":
        return (argv + ["train", fields["name"]] + (["--video"] if fields.get("video") else [])
                + (["--stills"] if fields.get("stills") else []) + (["--force"] if fields.get("force") else []))
    argv += ["create", fields["name"], "--phrase", fields["phrase"]]
    if fields.get("seed_upload"):
        argv += ["--seed-image", os.path.join(state, "uploads", fields["seed_upload"])]
    else:
        argv += ["--descriptor", fields["descriptor"]]
    if fields.get("class_noun"):
        argv += ["--class", fields["class_noun"]]
    if fields.get("trigger"):
        argv += ["--trigger", fields["trigger"]]
    if fields.get("seed") is not None:
        argv += ["--seed", str(fields["seed"])]
    if fields.get("force"):
        argv.append("--force")
    return argv


def run_light(argv, cwd, env=None, timeout=None):
    """One light subprocess (MU59): {"argv", "exit_code", "stdout", "stderr", "timed_out"}."""
    limit = LIGHT_TIMEOUT if timeout is None else timeout
    result = {"argv": list(argv), "exit_code": None, "stdout": "", "stderr": "", "timed_out": False}
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(argv, cwd=cwd, env=env or dict(os.environ), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                              capture_output=True, timeout=limit)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
        result["timed_out"] = True
        result["stdout"] = (e.stdout or b"").decode("utf-8", "replace")
        result["stderr"] = (e.stderr or b"").decode("utf-8", "replace")
        return result
    except OSError as e:
        result["stderr"] = "%s: %s" % (type(e).__name__, e.strerror or e)
        return result
    result["exit_code"] = proc.returncode
    result["stdout"] = proc.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace")
    result["stderr"] = proc.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace")
    return result


def tool_argv(ws_real, tool, *args):
    """[sys.executable, <ws>/bin/<tool>, args...] (MU59)."""
    return [sys.executable, os.path.join(ws_real, "bin", tool)] + list(args)


def last_line(text):
    """The last non-empty line of text, stripped, or ""."""
    lines = [line.strip() for line in (text or "").splitlines() if line.strip()]
    return lines[-1] if lines else ""


_HELP_CACHE = {}
_HELP_LOCK = threading.Lock()


def parse_help_flags(text):
    """Every --flag on the option lines (starting with two spaces and "-") of argparse --help
    output (MU37)."""
    flags = set()
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("  -"):
            head = re.split(r"\s{2,}", line[2:], maxsplit=1)[0]
            flags.update(re.findall(r"--[a-z][a-z0-9-]*", head))
    return flags


def help_flags(ws_real):
    """(flags, error) for <ws>/bin/ltx-movie --help, cached by (ws, mtime_ns of bin/ltx-movie)
    (MU37). flags is a frozenset, or None with error "cannot read bin/ltx-movie --help (exit N):
    <last stderr line>"."""
    path = os.path.join(ws_real, "bin", "ltx-movie")
    try:
        key = (ws_real, os.stat(path).st_mtime_ns)
    except OSError as e:
        return None, "cannot read bin/ltx-movie --help (exit none): %s" % (e.strerror or e)
    with _HELP_LOCK:
        if key in _HELP_CACHE:
            return _HELP_CACHE[key]
    run = run_light(tool_argv(ws_real, "ltx-movie", "--help"), ws_real)
    if run["exit_code"] == 0:
        value = (frozenset(parse_help_flags(run["stdout"])), None)
    else:
        code = "none" if run["exit_code"] is None else str(run["exit_code"])
        value = (None, "cannot read bin/ltx-movie --help (exit %s): %s" % (code, last_line(run["stderr"])))
    with _HELP_LOCK:
        _HELP_CACHE[key] = value
    return value


def supported_map(flags):
    """{field or action: bool} for MU47's "supported" (all False when flags is None)."""
    return dict((key, flags is not None and all(f in flags for f in needed)) for key, needed in FIELD_FLAGS)
```

### Task 6: Check records, V1 field rules and V2 host checks

Implements MU38, MU42, the `render_needed`/`stills_needed`/`story_needed` sets of MU43, V1 rules 1-18 (smoke's subset arrives with Task 17), and V2's four records with their probes (reused by MU60). Tests: MUT4, MUT3's rule-18 half, MUT9's library half.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/test_movie_ui_checks.py`
- Modify: `movie_ui_lib.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5's `build_argv`, `help_flags`, `goal_text`, `run_light`, `last_line`, `ACTIONS`, `MODES`, `LORA_KINDS`; Task 2's `UPLOAD_ID_RE`, `STORY_ID_RE`, `CHARACTER_NAME_RE`, `SMOKE_STORY_ID`; testutil `make_stub_workspace`, `stub_config`, `fields`.
- Produces: `DETAIL_MAX = 20000`, `NARRATIVE_MAX`, `LORA_ID_RE`, `STATE_LINE_RE`, `STILLS_PROBE`, `STILLS_PROBE_TIMEOUT = 60`; `check(check_id, name, status, detail="") -> dict`; `checks_pass(checks) -> bool`; `story_dir(ws_real, story_id)`; `valid_story_id(story_id) -> bool`; `resolved_panels(fields) -> int or None`; `validate_fields(fields, ws_real, state) -> [str]`; `ltx_bin() -> str`; `render_check() -> (ok, detail)`; `stills_check(ws_real, py=sys.executable) -> (ok, detail)`; `story_server_check(ws_real) -> (ok, detail)`; `judge_key_check() -> (ok, detail)`; `not_needed(check_id, name)`; `render_needed(fields)`, `stills_needed(fields)`, `story_needed(fields, ws_real)`; `host_checks(fields, ws_real, py=sys.executable) -> [4 records]`; private `_from_pair`, `_rule_models`, `_rule_loras`, `_rule_flags`, `_rule_action` (Task 17 calls the four rule helpers).

**1.1.** **Create** `tests/test_movie_ui_checks.py` with exactly this content:

```python
"""movie-ui validation checks V1-V6 (movie-ui spec MUT3 rule 18, MUT4-MUT9).
Run from the workspace root: python3 -m pytest tests/test_movie_ui_checks.py -v
Every test runs under a temporary HOME against a stub workspace."""

import json
import os
import shutil
import stat
import sys

import pytest

import movie_ui_testutil as mt
import movie_ui_lib as lib


@pytest.fixture
def env(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    state = mt.set_home(monkeypatch, tmp_path)
    ws = mt.make_stub_workspace(tmp_path)
    return {"state": state, "ws": ws, "tmp": str(tmp_path)}


def _errors(env, **overrides):
    return lib.validate_fields(mt.fields(env["ws"], **overrides), env["ws"], env["state"])


def _story(env, story_id="ronin-rescue", files=("story.md",)):
    sdir = os.path.join(env["ws"], "generated", "stories", story_id)
    os.makedirs(sdir, exist_ok=True)
    for name in files:
        with open(os.path.join(sdir, name), "w") as f:
            f.write("x")
    return sdir


def test_mut4_every_v1_error_alone(env):
    upload = os.path.join(env["state"], "uploads", "a" * 64 + ".png")
    with open(upload, "wb") as f:
        f.write(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n")
    seed = "a" * 64 + ".png"
    assert _errors(env) == []
    table = [
        ({"story_id": "../x"}, "story id must match ^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,79}$ and must not contain '..'"),
        ({"story_id": "movie-ui-smoke"}, "story id movie-ui-smoke is reserved for smoke renders"),
        ({"narrative": "   "}, "narrative is required"),
        ({"narrative": None}, "narrative is required"),
        ({"narrative": "x" * 20001}, "narrative must be at most 20000 characters"),
        ({"narrative": " -x"}, "narrative must not start with '-'"),
        ({"goal": " . "}, "goal must be a single line of 1 to 300 characters"),
        ({"goal": "a\nb"}, "goal must be a single line of 1 to 300 characters"),
        ({"goal": "g" * 301}, "goal must be a single line of 1 to 300 characters"),
        ({"length": 30}, "give exactly one of panels or length"),
        ({"panels": None}, "give exactly one of panels or length"),
        ({"panels": 0}, "panels must be an integer from 1 to 200"),
        ({"panels": True}, "panels must be an integer from 1 to 200"),
        ({"panels": None, "length": 3601}, "length must be a number of seconds in (0, 3600]"),
        ({"panels": None, "length": False}, "length must be a number of seconds in (0, 3600]"),
        ({"panels": None, "length": 6}, "length resolves to 1 panel(s); the minimum is 2"),
        ({"fps": 121}, "fps must be an integer from 1 to 120"),
        ({"frames": 144}, "frames must satisfy (n-1)%8==0 and n>=9"),
        ({"frames": 1}, "frames must satisfy (n-1)%8==0 and n>=9"),
        ({"width": 700, "height": 448}, "width and height must both be multiples of 64 (at least 64)"),
        ({"width": 704}, "width and height must both be multiples of 64 (at least 64)"),
        ({"seed_upload": "b" * 64 + ".png"}, "seed image upload %s not found" % ("b" * 64 + ".png")),
        ({"mode": "fast"}, "mode must be chained, shots or no_stills"),
        ({"seed_upload": seed, "mode": "no_stills"}, "a seed image cannot be used with no-stills mode"),
        ({"seed_upload": seed, "mode": "shots"}, "a seed image cannot be used with shots mode"),
        ({"model": "-x"}, "model must be a model name or an absolute path"),
        ({"gemma": ""}, "gemma must be a model name or an absolute path"),
        ({"story_model": "a\tb"}, "story_model must be a model name or an absolute path"),
        ({"video_loras": [{"id": None, "path": "rel.safetensors", "strength": 1, "kind": None}]},
         "video LoRA 1: path must be absolute"),
        ({"stills_loras": [{"id": None, "path": "/a.safetensors", "strength": 0, "kind": None}]},
         "stills LoRA 1: strength must be in (0, 2]"),
        ({"video_loras": [{"id": "Bad_ID", "path": "/a.safetensors", "strength": 1, "kind": "style"}]},
         "video LoRA 1: bad catalog id"),
        ({"video_loras": [{"id": "pan", "path": "/a.safetensors", "strength": 1, "kind": None}]},
         "video LoRA 1: bad kind"),
        ({"video_loras": [{"id": None, "path": "/a.safetensors", "strength": 1, "kind": None},
                          {"id": None, "path": "/b/../a.safetensors", "strength": 1, "kind": None}]},
         "video LoRA /b/../a.safetensors is listed twice"),
        ({"characters": ["kyra", "kyra"]}, "characters must be distinct character names"),
        ({"characters": ["Kyra"]}, "characters must be distinct character names"),
        ({"judge": "yes"}, "judge must be true or false"),
        ({"panel_timeout": 59}, "panel_timeout must be an integer from 60 to 86400"),
        ({"action": "render"}, "unknown action"),
        ({"story_legacy": True, "judge": True}, "--story-legacy cannot be combined with --judge, --no-auto-lora or --goal"),
        ({"story_legacy": True, "goal": "tense"}, "--story-legacy cannot be combined with --judge, --no-auto-lora or --goal"),
    ]
    for overrides, message in table:
        assert _errors(env, **overrides) == [message], overrides
    assert _errors(env, seed_upload=seed, width=704, height=448) == ["give a seed image or a resolution, not both"]
    assert _errors(env, action="redo", mode="chained", redo=[1]) == ["redo needs shots mode", "redo needs an existing story.md"]
    _story(env)
    assert _errors(env, action="redo", mode="shots", redo=[]) == ["redo needs at least one panel number"]
    assert _errors(env, action="redo", mode="shots", redo=[2, 16]) == ["redo panel 16 is out of range (1..15)"]
    assert _errors(env, action="redo", mode="shots", redo=[2]) == []
    _story(env, files=("movie.mp4",))
    assert _errors(env, action="movie") == ['movie.mp4 already exists; tick "Overwrite movie.mp4" (--force)']
    assert _errors(env, action="movie", overwrite_movie=True) == []
    for bad in ("../x", "a/../b", "a..b", ".x", "x" * 81, "-x", "a b"):
        assert _errors(env, story_id=bad)[0].startswith("story id must match"), bad
    assert _errors(env, story_id="a.b-c_9") == []
    assert _errors(env, story_id="x" * 80) == []


def test_mut3_v1_rule_18_against_an_old_workspace(env):
    with open(os.path.join(mt.FIXTURES, "ltx-movie-help-old.txt"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
        mt.stub_config(env["ws"], {"ltx-movie --help": {"stdout": f.read()}})
    assert _errors(env, judge=True) == [
        "this workspace's bin/ltx-movie does not support --gemma",
        "this workspace's bin/ltx-movie does not support --judge",
        "this workspace's bin/ltx-movie does not support --story-only"]
    assert _errors(env, gemma=None, action="movie") == []


def test_mut9_v2_host_checks(env, monkeypatch):
    tmp = env["tmp"]
    binary = os.path.join(tmp, "ltx-2-mlx")
    tools = os.path.join(tmp, "tools")
    os.makedirs(tools)
    for name in ("ffmpeg", "ffprobe"):
        path = os.path.join(tools, name)
        with open(path, "w") as f:
            f.write("#!/bin/sh\n")
        os.chmod(path, 0o755)
    monkeypatch.setenv("PATH", tools)
    monkeypatch.setenv("LTX2_MLX_BIN", binary)
    assert lib.render_check() == (False, "ltx-2-mlx binary not found or not executable: %s (set LTX2_MLX_BIN)" % binary)
    with open(binary, "w") as f:
        f.write("#!/bin/sh\n")
    os.chmod(binary, 0o755)
    assert lib.render_check() == (True, binary)
    monkeypatch.setenv("PATH", os.path.join(tmp, "empty"))
    assert lib.render_check() == (False, "ffmpeg not found on PATH; ffprobe not found on PATH")
    monkeypatch.delenv("LTX2_MLX_BIN")
    monkeypatch.setenv("LTX2_MLX_DIR", os.path.join(tmp, "ltxdir"))
    assert lib.ltx_bin() == os.path.join(tmp, "ltxdir", ".venv", "bin", "ltx-2-mlx")
    good_py = os.path.join(tmp, "good-python")
    bad_py = os.path.join(tmp, "bad-python")
    with open(good_py, "w") as f:
        f.write("#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n")
    with open(bad_py, "w") as f:
        f.write("#!/bin/sh\necho 'Traceback' >&2\necho \"ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'torch'\" >&2\nexit 1\n")
    os.chmod(good_py, 0o755)
    os.chmod(bad_py, 0o755)
    assert lib.stills_check(env["ws"], good_py) == (True, "stills dependencies importable")
    assert lib.stills_check(env["ws"], bad_py) == (
        False, "stills dependencies not importable: ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'torch'")
    monkeypatch.setenv("PATH", "/usr/bin:/bin")
    assert lib.story_server_check(env["ws"]) == (True, "story server: SERVING vision")
    mt.stub_config(env["ws"], {"story-server status": {"stdout": "  state:          STOPPED\n"}})
    assert lib.story_server_check(env["ws"]) == (
        False, "story server is STOPPED; start it with bin/story-server vision (or text)")
    mt.stub_config(env["ws"], {"story-server status": {"exit": 4, "stderr": "story-server: /usr/sbin/lsof missing\n"}})
    assert lib.story_server_check(env["ws"]) == (
        False, "bin/story-server status exited 4: story-server: /usr/sbin/lsof missing")
    assert lib.judge_key_check() == (False, "--judge needs ANTHROPIC_API_KEY in movie-ui's environment")
    monkeypatch.setenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "test-not-a-key")
    assert lib.judge_key_check() == (True, "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is set")
    records = lib.host_checks(mt.fields(env["ws"]), env["ws"], good_py)
    assert [(r["name"], r["status"]) for r in records] == [
        ("render", "skip"), ("stills", "skip"), ("story server", "fail"), ("judge key", "skip")]
    assert records[0]["detail"] == "not needed for this action"
    mt.stub_config(env["ws"], {})
    _story(env)
    records = lib.host_checks(mt.fields(env["ws"], action="movie", judge=True, mode="no_stills"), env["ws"], good_py)
    assert [(r["name"], r["status"]) for r in records] == [
        ("render", "fail"), ("stills", "skip"), ("story server", "skip"), ("judge key", "pass")]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

**3.1.** **Append** to `movie_ui_lib.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Check records, V1 fields and V2 host (MU42, MU43, V1, V2)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

DETAIL_MAX = 20000
NARRATIVE_MAX = 20000
LORA_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,31}$")
STATE_LINE_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*state:\s*(.+?)\s*$")
STILLS_PROBE = ("import torch; from diffusers import ZImagePipeline, ZImageTransformer2DModel; "
                "from transformers import Qwen3Model")
STILLS_PROBE_TIMEOUT = 60


def check(check_id, name, status, detail=""):
    """One check record (MU42); detail keeps its last DETAIL_MAX characters."""
    return {"id": check_id, "name": name, "status": status, "detail": (detail or "")[-DETAIL_MAX:]}


def checks_pass(checks):
    return not any(c["status"] == "fail" for c in checks)


def _is_int(value):
    return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)


def _is_number(value):
    return isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool)


def story_dir(ws_real, story_id):
    return os.path.join(ws_real, "generated", "stories", story_id)


def valid_story_id(story_id):
    """MU38: STORY_ID_RE and no "..". (movie-ui-smoke passes this; callers reserve it.)"""
    return isinstance(story_id, str) and STORY_ID_RE.fullmatch(story_id) is not None and ".." not in story_id


def resolved_panels(fields):
    """P: panels, or round(length / (frames / fps)) when length is given; None when not computable."""
    if _is_int(fields.get("panels")):
        return fields["panels"]
    length, frames, fps = fields.get("length"), fields.get("frames"), fields.get("fps")
    if _is_number(length) and _is_int(frames) and _is_int(fps) and frames > 0 and fps > 0:
        return int(round(length / (frames / float(fps))))
    return None


def _rule_models(fields):
    """V1 rule 9."""
    errors = []
    for key in ("model", "gemma", "story_model"):
        value = fields.get(key)
        if value is None:
            continue
        if not (isinstance(value, str) and 1 <= len(value) <= 512 and not value.startswith("-")
                and all(ord(ch) >= 0x20 for ch in value)):
            errors.append("%s must be a model name or an absolute path" % key)
    return errors


def _rule_loras(fields, which):
    """V1 rule 11 for one list (which is "video" or "stills")."""
    value = fields.get(which + "_loras")
    if not isinstance(value, list) or len(value) > 8:
        return ["%s LoRAs must be a list of at most 8 items" % which]
    errors, seen = [], set()
    for k, item in enumerate(value, 1):
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            errors.append("%s LoRA %d: must be an object" % (which, k))
            continue
        path, strength, lora_id, kind = item.get("path"), item.get("strength"), item.get("id"), item.get("kind")
        if not (isinstance(path, str) and os.path.isabs(path)):
            errors.append("%s LoRA %d: path must be absolute" % (which, k))
        else:
            real = os.path.realpath(path)
            if real in seen:
                errors.append("%s LoRA %s is listed twice" % (which, path))
            seen.add(real)
        if not (_is_number(strength) and 0 < strength <= 2):
            errors.append("%s LoRA %d: strength must be in (0, 2]" % (which, k))
        if lora_id is not None and not (isinstance(lora_id, str) and LORA_ID_RE.fullmatch(lora_id)):
            errors.append("%s LoRA %d: bad catalog id" % (which, k))
        if not (kind is None or kind in LORA_KINDS) or ((kind is None) != (lora_id is None)):
            errors.append("%s LoRA %d: bad kind" % (which, k))
    return errors


def _rule_flags(fields):
    """V1 rule 13."""
    errors = []
    for key in ("auto_lora", "story_legacy", "judge", "no_low_ram", "overwrite_movie"):
        if not isinstance(fields.get(key), bool):
            errors.append("%s must be true or false" % key)
    timeout = fields.get("panel_timeout")
    if timeout is not None and not (_is_int(timeout) and 60 <= timeout <= 86400):
        errors.append("panel_timeout must be an integer from 60 to 86400")
    return errors


def _rule_action(fields):
    """V1 rule 14."""
    return [] if fields.get("action") in ACTIONS else ["unknown action"]


def validate_fields(fields, ws_real, state):
    """V1: every failure line, in rule order; [] when the fields pass."""
    errors = []
    story_id = fields.get("story_id")
    sid_ok = valid_story_id(story_id)
    if not sid_ok:                                                           # 1
        errors.append("story id must match ^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,79}$ and must not contain '..'")
    elif story_id == SMOKE_STORY_ID:
        sid_ok = False
        errors.append("story id movie-ui-smoke is reserved for smoke renders")
    narrative = fields.get("narrative")                                      # 2
    if not isinstance(narrative, str) or not narrative.strip():
        errors.append("narrative is required")
    elif len(narrative) > NARRATIVE_MAX:
        errors.append("narrative must be at most 20000 characters")
    elif narrative.strip().startswith("-"):
        errors.append("narrative must not start with '-'")
    goal = fields.get("goal")                                                # 3
    if goal is not None:
        text = goal_text(goal) if isinstance(goal, str) else ""
        if not text or "\n" in text or "\r" in text or len(text) > 300:
            errors.append("goal must be a single line of 1 to 300 characters")
    panels, length = fields.get("panels"), fields.get("length")              # 4
    fps, frames = fields.get("fps"), fields.get("frames")
    fps_ok = _is_int(fps) and 1 <= fps <= 120
    frames_ok = _is_int(frames) and (frames - 1) % 8 == 0 and frames >= 9
    if (panels is None) == (length is None):
        errors.append("give exactly one of panels or length")
    elif panels is not None:
        if not (_is_int(panels) and 1 <= panels <= 200):
            errors.append("panels must be an integer from 1 to 200")
    elif not (_is_number(length) and 0 < length <= 3600):
        errors.append("length must be a number of seconds in (0, 3600]")
    elif fps_ok and frames_ok:
        n = int(round(length / (frames / float(fps))))
        if n < 2:
            errors.append("length resolves to %d panel(s); the minimum is 2" % n)
    if not fps_ok:                                                           # 5
        errors.append("fps must be an integer from 1 to 120")
    if not frames_ok:                                                        # 6
        errors.append("frames must satisfy (n-1)%8==0 and n>=9")
    seed, width, height = fields.get("seed_upload"), fields.get("width"), fields.get("height")   # 7
    if seed is not None and (width is not None or height is not None):
        errors.append("give a seed image or a resolution, not both")
    if not ((width is None and height is None)
            or (_is_int(width) and _is_int(height) and width % 64 == 0 and height % 64 == 0
                and width >= 64 and height >= 64)):
        errors.append("width and height must both be multiples of 64 (at least 64)")
    if seed is not None and not (isinstance(seed, str) and UPLOAD_ID_RE.fullmatch(seed)
                                 and os.path.isfile(os.path.join(state, "uploads", seed))):
        errors.append("seed image upload %s not found" % seed)
    mode = fields.get("mode")                                                # 8
    if mode not in MODES:
        errors.append("mode must be chained, shots or no_stills")
    elif seed is not None and mode == "no_stills":
        errors.append("a seed image cannot be used with no-stills mode")
    elif seed is not None and mode == "shots":
        errors.append("a seed image cannot be used with shots mode")
    errors += _rule_models(fields)                                           # 9
    errors += _rule_loras(fields, "video") + _rule_loras(fields, "stills")  # 11
    chars = fields.get("characters")                                         # 12
    if not (isinstance(chars, list) and len(chars) <= 8 and len(set(map(repr, chars))) == len(chars)
            and all(isinstance(c, str) and CHARACTER_NAME_RE.fullmatch(c) for c in chars)):
        errors.append("characters must be distinct character names")
    errors += _rule_flags(fields)                                            # 13
    errors += _rule_action(fields)                                           # 14
    if fields.get("story_legacy") is True and (fields.get("judge") is True or fields.get("auto_lora") is False
                                               or goal is not None):     # 15
        errors.append("--story-legacy cannot be combined with --judge, --no-auto-lora or --goal")
    action = fields.get("action")
    sdir = story_dir(ws_real, story_id) if sid_ok else None
    if action == "redo":                                                     # 16
        redo = fields.get("redo")
        if mode != "shots":
            errors.append("redo needs shots mode")
        if not (isinstance(redo, list) and redo and all(_is_int(n) for n in redo)):
            errors.append("redo needs at least one panel number")
        else:
            total = resolved_panels(fields)
            if total is not None:
                for n in sorted(set(redo)):
                    if not 1 <= n <= total:
                        errors.append("redo panel %d is out of range (1..%d)" % (n, total))
        if sdir is None or not os.path.isfile(os.path.join(sdir, "story.md")):
            errors.append("redo needs an existing story.md")
    if (action == "movie" and sdir is not None and fields.get("overwrite_movie") is False
            and os.path.exists(os.path.join(sdir, "movie.mp4"))):           # 17
        errors.append('movie.mp4 already exists; tick "Overwrite movie.mp4" (--force)')
    if errors:
        return errors
    flags, error = help_flags(ws_real)                                       # 18
    if flags is None:
        return [error]
    return ["this workspace's bin/ltx-movie does not support %s" % a
            for a in build_argv(fields, ws_real, state) if a.startswith("--") and a not in flags]


def ltx_bin():
    """The ltx-2-mlx binary bin/ltx-mlx-render would run (ltx2_mlx_video_skill.py:44-46, :130-139)."""
    path = os.environ.get("LTX2_MLX_BIN") or os.path.join(
        os.environ.get("LTX2_MLX_DIR") or os.path.expanduser("~/ltx-2-mlx"), ".venv", "bin", "ltx-2-mlx")
    if os.sep not in path:
        path = shutil.which(path) or path
    return path


def render_check():
    """(ok, detail) for V2 "render"."""
    path = ltx_bin()
    problems = []
    if not (os.path.isfile(path) and os.access(path, os.X_OK)):
        problems.append("ltx-2-mlx binary not found or not executable: %s (set LTX2_MLX_BIN)" % path)
    for tool in ("ffmpeg", "ffprobe"):
        if shutil.which(tool) is None:
            problems.append("%s not found on PATH" % tool)
    return (False, "; ".join(problems)) if problems else (True, path)


def stills_check(ws_real, py=sys.executable):
    """(ok, detail) for V2 "stills": the imports of z_image_skill.py:46-48 in a child."""
    run = run_light([py, "-c", STILLS_PROBE], ws_real, timeout=STILLS_PROBE_TIMEOUT)
    if run["timed_out"]:
        return False, "stills dependency probe timed out after %d s" % STILLS_PROBE_TIMEOUT
    if run["exit_code"] == 0:
        return True, "stills dependencies importable"
    return False, "stills dependencies not importable: %s" % last_line(run["stderr"])


def story_server_check(ws_real):
    """(ok, detail) for V2 "story server": bin/story-server status (bash, run directly)."""
    run = run_light([os.path.join(ws_real, "bin", "story-server"), "status"], ws_real)
    state = None
    for line in run["stdout"].splitlines():
        match = STATE_LINE_RE.match(line)
        if match:
            state = match.group(1)
            break
    if run["exit_code"] != 0 or state is None:
        code = "none" if run["exit_code"] is None else str(run["exit_code"])
        return False, "bin/story-server status exited %s: %s" % (code, last_line(run["stderr"]) or "-")
    if state.startswith("SERVING "):
        return True, "story server: %s" % state
    return False, "story server is %s; start it with bin/story-server vision (or text)" % state


def judge_key_check():
    """(ok, detail) for V2 "judge key"."""
    if os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"):
        return True, "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is set"
    return False, "--judge needs ANTHROPIC_API_KEY in movie-ui's environment"


def _from_pair(check_id, name, pair):
    return check(check_id, name, "pass" if pair[0] else "fail", pair[1])


def not_needed(check_id, name):
    return check(check_id, name, "skip", "not needed for this action")


def render_needed(fields):
    return fields["action"] in ("movie", "redo", "smoke")


def stills_needed(fields):
    return fields["action"] in ("movie", "redo") and fields["mode"] != "no_stills"


def story_needed(fields, ws_real):
    if fields["action"] in ("story", "regenerate"):
        return True
    return fields["action"] == "movie" and not os.path.isfile(
        os.path.join(story_dir(ws_real, fields["story_id"]), "story.md"))


def host_checks(fields, ws_real, py=sys.executable):
    """V2's four records."""
    out = [_from_pair("V2", "render", render_check()) if render_needed(fields) else not_needed("V2", "render"),
           _from_pair("V2", "stills", stills_check(ws_real, py)) if stills_needed(fields)
           else not_needed("V2", "stills"),
           _from_pair("V2", "story server", story_server_check(ws_real)) if story_needed(fields, ws_real)
           else not_needed("V2", "story server"),
           _from_pair("V2", "judge key", judge_key_check()) if fields["judge"] is True
           else not_needed("V2", "judge key")]
    return out
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: `ltx_hub()`, `zimage_hub()`, `hf_hub()`, `repo_dir_name(repo_id)`, `zimage_hub_for(repo_id)`, `choose_snapshot(repo_dir) -> (path or None, reason or None)`, `intact_problems(snapshot, repo_dir=None) -> [str]`, `model_check(model_id, hub) -> (ok, detail)`, `model_checks(fields, ws_real) -> [4 records]`, `_tree_bytes(path) -> int` (regular files by `lstat`, no symlink following; Task 11 reuses it for `data_bytes`), `list_models() -> {"hubs", "models"}`; testutil `make_hf_cache(root, repo, files, refs=True, incomplete=0, broken=())`.

**1.1.** **Append** to `tests/movie_ui_testutil.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# HF caches
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_hf_cache(root, repo, files, refs=True, incomplete=0, broken=()):
    """<root>/hub/models--org--name/{blobs, snapshots/<rev>, refs/main} with every snapshot file a
    symlink into blobs. files maps relpath -> bytes; broken lists relpaths whose blob is missing.
    Returns the repo directory."""
    rev = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567"
    repo_dir = os.path.join(str(root), "hub", "models--" + repo.replace("/", "--"))
    blobs = os.path.join(repo_dir, "blobs")
    snap = os.path.join(repo_dir, "snapshots", rev)
    os.makedirs(blobs, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(snap, exist_ok=True)
    for i, (rel, data) in enumerate(sorted(files.items())):
        blob = os.path.join(blobs, "blob%04d" % i)
        if rel not in broken:
            with open(blob, "wb") as f:
                f.write(data)
        link = os.path.join(snap, rel)
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(link), exist_ok=True)
        os.symlink(os.path.relpath(blob, os.path.dirname(link)), link)
    for i in range(incomplete):
        with open(os.path.join(blobs, "partial%d.incomplete" % i), "wb") as f:
            f.write(b"x")
    if refs:
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(repo_dir, "refs"), exist_ok=True)
        with open(os.path.join(repo_dir, "refs", "main"), "w") as f:
            f.write(rev + "\n")
    return repo_dir
```

def test_mut5_v3_models_present_and_intact(env, monkeypatch):

**3.1.** **Append** to `movie_ui_lib.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# V3: models present and intact, and the model list of MU63
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def ltx_hub():
    """<LTX2_MLX_HF_HOME or <LTX2_MLX_DIR or ~/ltx-2-mlx>/hf_cache>/hub (ltx2_mlx_video_skill.py:47-48)."""
    home = os.environ.get("LTX2_MLX_HF_HOME") or os.path.join(
        os.environ.get("LTX2_MLX_DIR") or os.path.expanduser("~/ltx-2-mlx"), "hf_cache")
    return os.path.join(home, "hub")


def zimage_hub():
    """<Z_IMAGE_HF_HOME or ~/hf_home>/hub (z_image_skill.py:56-59)."""
    return os.path.join(os.environ.get("Z_IMAGE_HF_HOME") or os.path.expanduser("~/hf_home"), "hub")


def hf_hub():
    """<HF_HOME or ~/.cache/huggingface>/hub, the default HF cache."""
    return os.path.join(os.environ.get("HF_HOME") or os.path.expanduser("~/.cache/huggingface"), "hub")


def repo_dir_name(repo_id):
    return "models--" + repo_id.replace("/", "--")


def zimage_hub_for(repo_id):
    """The scoped Z-Image hub when it holds the repo, else the default HF hub (z_image_skill.py:62-69)."""
    scoped = zimage_hub()
    return scoped if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(scoped, repo_dir_name(repo_id))) else hf_hub()


def choose_snapshot(repo_dir):
    """(snapshot path, None) by refs/main, else the only snapshot; (None, reason) otherwise."""
    try:
        with open(os.path.join(repo_dir, "refs", "main"), "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            rev = f.read().strip()
        return os.path.join(repo_dir, "snapshots", rev), None
    except OSError:
        pass
    try:
        snaps = sorted(n for n in os.listdir(os.path.join(repo_dir, "snapshots"))
                       if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(repo_dir, "snapshots", n)))
    except OSError:
        snaps = []
    if len(snaps) == 1:
        return os.path.join(repo_dir, "snapshots", snaps[0]), None
    return None, "no refs/main"


def _nonempty_file(path):
    real = os.path.realpath(path)
    return os.path.isfile(real) and os.path.getsize(real) > 0


def intact_problems(snapshot, repo_dir=None):
    """V3's intact rules (b)-(e) on one snapshot (or an absolute model directory when repo_dir is
    None). The config.json clause of the brief is not applied (D3, Q3)."""
    problems = []
    if repo_dir is not None:
        try:
            partial = [n for n in os.listdir(os.path.join(repo_dir, "blobs")) if n.endswith(".incomplete")]
        except OSError:
            partial = []
        if partial:
            problems.append("download incomplete: %d partial blob(s)" % len(partial))
    safetensors, indexes = 0, []
    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(snapshot, followlinks=False):
        dirnames.sort()
        for name in sorted(filenames):
            full = os.path.join(dirpath, name)
            rel = os.path.relpath(full, snapshot)
            real = os.path.realpath(full)
            if not os.path.isfile(real):
                problems.append("broken file: %s" % rel)
                continue
            if name.endswith(".safetensors"):
                safetensors += 1
                if os.path.getsize(real) == 0:
                    problems.append("empty file: %s" % rel)
            if name.endswith(".safetensors.index.json"):
                indexes.append(full)
    if safetensors == 0:
        problems.append("no .safetensors file")
    for index in indexes:
        rel = os.path.relpath(index, snapshot)
        data = read_json(index)
        if not (isinstance(data, dict) and isinstance(data.get("weight_map"), dict)):
            problems.append("unreadable index: %s" % rel)
            continue
        for shard in sorted(set(v for v in data["weight_map"].values() if isinstance(v, str))):
            path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(index), shard)
            if not _nonempty_file(path):
                problems.append("missing shard: %s" % os.path.relpath(path, snapshot))
    return problems


def model_check(model_id, hub):
    """(ok, detail) for one V3 record: an absolute path is checked in place, an HF ID in hub."""
    if os.path.isabs(model_id):
        if not os.path.isdir(model_id):
            return False, "%s is not a directory" % model_id
        problems = intact_problems(model_id)
        return (False, "; ".join(problems)) if problems else (True, model_id)
    repo_dir = os.path.join(hub, repo_dir_name(model_id))
    if not os.path.isdir(repo_dir):
        return False, "%s is not in %s" % (model_id, hub)
    snapshot, reason = choose_snapshot(repo_dir)
    if snapshot is None:
        return False, "cannot choose a snapshot of %s (%s)" % (model_id, reason)
    problems = intact_problems(snapshot, repo_dir)
    return (False, "; ".join(problems)) if problems else (True, snapshot)


def model_checks(fields, ws_real):
    """V3's four records."""
    m = fields["model"] or DEFAULT_MODEL
    g = fields["gemma"] or DEFAULT_GEMMA
    out = []
    if render_needed(fields):
        out.append(_from_pair("V3", "video model %s" % m, model_check(m, ltx_hub())))
        out.append(_from_pair("V3", "gemma %s" % g, model_check(g, ltx_hub())))
    else:
        out += [not_needed("V3", "video model %s" % m), not_needed("V3", "gemma %s" % g)]
    for name, repo in (("Z-Image %s" % STILLS_BASE_MODEL, STILLS_BASE_MODEL),
                       ("Z-Image text encoder %s" % ZIMAGE_TEXT_ENCODER, ZIMAGE_TEXT_ENCODER)):
        if stills_needed(fields):
            out.append(_from_pair("V3", name, model_check(repo, zimage_hub_for(repo))))
        else:
            out.append(not_needed("V3", name))
    return out


def _tree_bytes(path):
    total = 0
    for dirpath, _dirnames, filenames in os.walk(path, followlinks=False):
        for name in filenames:
            try:
                info = os.lstat(os.path.join(dirpath, name))
            except OSError:
                continue
            if info.st_mode & 0o170000 == 0o100000:
                total += info.st_size
    return total


def list_models():
    """MU63: {"hubs": {"ltx", "zimage", "hf"}, "models": [...]} sorted by (hub, repo)."""
    hubs = {"ltx": ltx_hub(), "zimage": zimage_hub(), "hf": hf_hub()}
    models = []
    for hub_name, hub in hubs.items():
        try:
            names = os.listdir(hub)
        except OSError:
            continue
        for name in names:
            repo_dir = os.path.join(hub, name)
            if not (name.startswith("models--") and os.path.isdir(repo_dir)):
                continue
            org, _sep, rest = name[len("models--"):].partition("--")
            snapshot, reason = choose_snapshot(repo_dir)
            problems = (["cannot choose a snapshot (%s)" % reason] if snapshot is None
                        else intact_problems(snapshot, repo_dir))
            models.append({"repo": "%s/%s" % (org, rest), "hub": hub_name, "repo_dir": repo_dir,
                           "intact": not problems, "problems": problems,
                           "bytes": _tree_bytes(os.path.join(repo_dir, "blobs"))})
    models.sort(key=lambda m: (m["hub"], m["repo"]))
    return {"hubs": hubs, "models": models}
```

### Task 8: V4 LoRAs intact, V5 compatible, V6 dry run, and `run_checks`

Implements V4 (catalog verify over chosen and auto-pickable IDs, the stdlib safetensors header rule), V5 (catalog compatibility, the cast characters, the video-base-model `skip` of D2/Q2), V6 (dry run with `STORY_PIPELINE_LOGGED=1`, D17) and MU43's `run_checks`. Tests: MUT6, MUT7, MUT8 and the MU43 order.

**Files:**
- Modify: `tests/movie_ui_testutil.py`
- Modify: `tests/test_movie_ui_checks.py`
- Modify: `movie_ui_lib.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 5-7 (`build_argv`, `run_light`, `tool_argv`, `last_line`, `check`, `not_needed`, `host_checks`, `model_checks`, `validate_fields`); testutil `stub_config`, `stub_calls`, `fields`.
- Produces: `SAFETENSORS_HEADER_MAX = 104857600`, `DRY_RUN_TIMEOUT = 60` (tests monkeypatch it), `V_GROUP_NAMES`; `catalog_list(ws_real, model, cache) -> dict` (raises `_ToolError`); `safetensors_problem(path) -> str or None`; `lora_checks(fields, ws_real, cache, smoke=False) -> [records]`; `compat_checks(fields, ws_real, cache, smoke=False) -> [records]`; `dry_run_check(fields, ws_real, state) -> record`; `run_checks(fields, ws_real, state, py=sys.executable) -> [records]`. The `smoke=True` paths are exercised by Task 17. Testutil: `fake_safetensors(path, header=None, n=None)`.

**1.1.** **Append** to `tests/movie_ui_testutil.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Safetensors files
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def fake_safetensors(path, header=None, n=None):
    """A minimal safetensors file: 8-byte little-endian header length, then the JSON header."""
    raw = json.dumps(header if header is not None else {"__metadata__": {}}).encode("utf-8")
    with open(path, "wb") as f:
        f.write((len(raw) if n is None else n).to_bytes(8, "little") + raw)
    return path
```

**1.2.** **Append** to `tests/test_movie_ui_checks.py`:

```python
def _entry(entry_id, target="video", kind="motion", path=None, rng=(0.5, 1.0)):
    return {"id": entry_id, "kind": kind, "path": path or "/loras/%s.safetensors" % entry_id, "target": target,
            "strength_range": list(rng), "base_models": []}


def _catalog(env, video=(), stills=(), verify=None, extra=None):
    """Stub `lora-catalog list --model M --json` for the video model and the stills base model."""
    config = dict(extra or {})
    for model, entries in ((lib.DEFAULT_MODEL, video), (lib.STILLS_BASE_MODEL, stills)):
        config["lora-catalog list --model %s --json" % model] = {
            "stdout": json.dumps({"catalog": "/c", "entries": list(entries), "characters": []})}
    if verify is not None:
        config["lora-catalog verify"] = verify
    mt.stub_config(env["ws"], config)
    return config


def _item(entry, strength=0.8):
    return {"id": entry["id"], "path": entry["path"], "strength": strength, "kind": entry["kind"]}


def test_mut6_v4_loras_intact(env):
    ws = env["ws"]
    pan, ink = _entry("pan"), _entry("ink", target="stills", kind="style")
    _catalog(env, video=[pan], stills=[ink])
    records = lib.lora_checks(mt.fields(ws), ws, {})
    assert records == [lib.check("V4", "catalog verify", "pass", "OK ink\nOK pan\nverified 2 entries: 0 problem(s)\n")]
    verify_calls = [c["argv"] for c in mt.stub_calls(ws) if c["sub"] == "verify"]
    assert verify_calls == [["verify", "ink", "pan"]]
    records = lib.lora_checks(mt.fields(ws, mode="no_stills"), ws, {})
    assert mt.stub_calls(ws)[-1]["argv"] == ["verify", "pan"]
    records = lib.lora_checks(mt.fields(ws, story_legacy=True), ws, {})
    assert records == [lib.check("V4", "catalog verify", "skip", "no catalog LoRA chosen or pickable")]
    _catalog(env, video=[pan], stills=[ink],
             verify={"exit": 1, "stdout": "FAIL pan: sha256 changed since it was cataloged\nverified 1 entry: 1 problem(s)\n"})
    records = lib.lora_checks(mt.fields(ws, auto_lora=False, video_loras=[_item(pan)]), ws, {})
    assert records[0]["status"] == "fail" and "FAIL pan: sha256 changed" in records[0]["detail"]
    tmp = env["tmp"]
    good = mt.fake_safetensors(os.path.join(tmp, "good.safetensors"))
    short = os.path.join(tmp, "short.safetensors")
    with open(short, "wb") as f:
        f.write(b"\x10\x00")
    zero = mt.fake_safetensors(os.path.join(tmp, "zero.safetensors"), n=0)
    trunc = mt.fake_safetensors(os.path.join(tmp, "trunc.safetensors"), n=500)
    notjson = os.path.join(tmp, "notjson.safetensors")
    with open(notjson, "wb") as f:
        f.write((4).to_bytes(8, "little") + b"[1] ")
    missing = os.path.join(tmp, "missing.safetensors")
    free = [{"id": None, "path": p, "strength": 1.0, "kind": None} for p in (good, short, zero, trunc, notjson, missing)]
    records = lib.lora_checks(mt.fields(ws, auto_lora=False, video_loras=free[:3], stills_loras=free[3:]), ws, {})
    assert [(r["name"], r["status"], r["detail"]) for r in records[1:]] == [
        ("LoRA file " + good, "pass", good + ": safetensors header ok"),
        ("LoRA file " + short, "fail", short + ": not a safetensors file"),
        ("LoRA file " + zero, "fail", zero + ": not a safetensors file"),
        ("LoRA file " + trunc, "fail", trunc + ": not a safetensors file"),
        ("LoRA file " + notjson, "fail", notjson + ": not a safetensors file"),
        ("LoRA file " + missing, "fail", missing + " is not a readable file")]


def _character(name, status="trained", video_base="/ltx/models/ltx-2.3-mlx-q8-dev",
               stills_base=lib.STILLS_BASE_MODEL, exit_code=0):
    """{"character show <name>": stub entry} printing the JSON then the files: block (D6)."""
    data = {"name": name, "status": status,
            "loras": {"video": {"base_model": video_base} if video_base else None,
                      "stills": {"base_model": stills_base} if stills_base else None}}
    text = json.dumps(data, indent=2) + "\nfiles:\n  ok       /lib/%s/seed.png\n" % name
    entry = {"stdout": text} if exit_code == 0 else {"exit": exit_code, "stderr": "Error: no character %s\n" % name}
    return {"character show %s" % name: entry}


def test_mut7_v5_compatible(env):
    ws = env["ws"]
    pan, ink, ink2 = _entry("pan"), _entry("ink", target="stills", kind="style"), _entry("ink2", target="stills",
                                                                                          kind="style")
    _catalog(env, video=[pan], stills=[ink, ink2])
    ok = lib.compat_checks(mt.fields(ws, video_loras=[_item(pan)], stills_loras=[_item(ink)]), ws, {})
    assert ok[0] == lib.check("V5", "catalog compatibility", "pass", "every catalog LoRA is compatible")
    assert ok[1] == lib.check("V5", "characters", "skip", "no character cast")
    cases = [
        ({"video_loras": [_item(ink)]}, "ink is not a video LoRA compatible with %s" % lib.DEFAULT_MODEL),
        ({"stills_loras": [_item(pan)]}, "pan is not a stills LoRA compatible with %s" % lib.STILLS_BASE_MODEL),
        ({"video_loras": [dict(_item(pan), path="/elsewhere.safetensors")]},
         "pan: path or kind differs from the catalog; reload the LoRA list"),
        ({"video_loras": [dict(_item(pan), kind="physics")]}, "pan: path or kind differs from the catalog; reload the LoRA list"),
        ({"video_loras": [_item(pan, 1.5)]}, "pan: strength 1.5 is outside 0.5-1.0"),
        ({"stills_loras": [_item(ink), _item(ink2)]}, "at most one style LoRA among the stills LoRAs"),
    ]
    for overrides, message in cases:
        record = lib.compat_checks(mt.fields(ws, **overrides), ws, {})[0]
        assert (record["status"], record["detail"]) == ("fail", message), overrides
    assert lib.compat_checks(mt.fields(ws), ws, {})[0] == lib.check("V5", "catalog compatibility", "skip",
                                                                    "no catalog LoRA chosen")
    extra = {}
    for spec in (_character("kyra"), _character("ronin", status="untrained", video_base=None),
                 _character("mei", stills_base="other/model"), _character("hana", video_base=lib.DEFAULT_MODEL),
                 _character("ghost", exit_code=2)):
        extra.update(spec)
    _catalog(env, video=[pan], stills=[ink, ink2], extra=extra)
    records = lib.compat_checks(mt.fields(ws, characters=["kyra", "ronin", "mei", "hana", "ghost"]), ws, {})
    assert [(r["name"], r["status"], r["detail"]) for r in records[1:]] == [
        ("character kyra", "pass", "trained"),
        ("character kyra video base model", "skip",
         "video LoRA trained on /ltx/models/ltx-2.3-mlx-q8-dev; bin/ltx-movie does not check this against --model %s"
         % lib.DEFAULT_MODEL),
        ("character ronin", "fail", "character ronin is not trained (status untrained)"),
        ("character mei", "fail", "character mei's stills LoRA is for other/model, not %s" % lib.STILLS_BASE_MODEL),
        ("character mei video base model", "skip",
         "video LoRA trained on /ltx/models/ltx-2.3-mlx-q8-dev; bin/ltx-movie does not check this against --model %s"
         % lib.DEFAULT_MODEL),
        ("character hana", "pass", "trained"),
        ("character hana video base model", "pass", "trained on %s" % lib.DEFAULT_MODEL),
        ("character ghost", "fail", "Error: no character ghost")]


def test_mut8_v6_dry_run(env, monkeypatch):
    ws = env["ws"]
    fields = mt.fields(ws, action="movie")
    record = lib.dry_run_check(fields, ws, env["state"])
    assert record == lib.check("V6", "dry run", "pass", "")
    call = mt.stub_calls(ws)[-1]
    assert call["argv"][-2:] == ["--no-review", "--dry-run"] and call["env"]["STORY_PIPELINE_LOGGED"] == "1"
    assert call["cwd"] == ws
    mt.stub_config(ws, {"ltx-movie --dry-run": {"exit": 2, "stderr": "Error: --redo needs --shots\n"}})
    record = lib.dry_run_check(fields, ws, env["state"])
    assert (record["status"], record["detail"]) == ("fail", "Error: --redo needs --shots\n")
    monkeypatch.setattr(lib, "DRY_RUN_TIMEOUT", 1)
    mt.stub_config(ws, {"ltx-movie --dry-run": {"sleep": 3}})
    record = lib.dry_run_check(fields, ws, env["state"])
    assert (record["status"], record["detail"]) == ("fail", "bin/ltx-movie --dry-run timed out after 1 s")


def test_mu43_run_checks_order_and_v1_short_circuit(env, monkeypatch):
    ws = env["ws"]
    bad = lib.run_checks(mt.fields(ws, panels=0), ws, env["state"])
    assert [(r["id"], r["name"], r["status"]) for r in bad] == [
        ("V1", "fields", "fail"), ("V2", "host can run it", "skip"), ("V3", "models present and intact", "skip"),
        ("V4", "LoRAs intact", "skip"), ("V5", "compatible", "skip"), ("V6", "dry run", "skip")]
    assert bad[1]["detail"] == "skipped: V1 failed"
    good = lib.run_checks(mt.fields(ws), ws, env["state"])
    assert [(r["id"], r["name"], r["status"]) for r in good] == [
        ("V1", "fields", "pass"), ("V2", "render", "skip"), ("V2", "stills", "skip"),
        ("V2", "story server", "pass"), ("V2", "judge key", "skip"),
        ("V3", "video model %s" % lib.DEFAULT_MODEL, "skip"), ("V3", "gemma %s" % lib.DEFAULT_GEMMA, "skip"),
        ("V3", "Z-Image %s" % lib.STILLS_BASE_MODEL, "skip"),
        ("V3", "Z-Image text encoder %s" % lib.ZIMAGE_TEXT_ENCODER, "skip"),
        ("V4", "catalog verify", "skip"), ("V5", "catalog compatibility", "skip"), ("V5", "characters", "skip"),
        ("V6", "dry run", "pass")]
    assert lib.checks_pass(good) and not lib.checks_pass(bad)
    assert len(lib.check("V6", "dry run", "fail", "x" * 30000)["detail"]) == 20000
```

**3.1.** **Append** to `movie_ui_lib.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# V4 LoRAs intact, V5 compatible, V6 dry run, and run_checks (MU43, V4-V6)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

SAFETENSORS_HEADER_MAX = 104857600
DRY_RUN_TIMEOUT = 60


class _ToolError(Exception):
    """A light bin/ call that failed; str() is the check detail."""


def catalog_list(ws_real, model, cache):
    """The parsed `lora-catalog list --model M --json` object (cached in cache by model)."""
    if model not in cache:
        run = run_light(tool_argv(ws_real, "lora-catalog", "list", "--model", model, "--json"), ws_real)
        try:
            if run["exit_code"] != 0:
                raise ValueError
            value = json.loads(run["stdout"])
            if not (isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(value.get("entries"), list)):
                raise ValueError
            cache[model] = value
        except ValueError:
            cache[model] = _ToolError("bin/lora-catalog list --model %s --json %s: %s" % (
                model, "timed out" if run["timed_out"] else "exited %s" % run["exit_code"],
                last_line(run["stderr"]) or "unparsable output"))
    if isinstance(cache[model], _ToolError):
        raise cache[model]
    return cache[model]


def safetensors_problem(path):
    """None when path is a readable safetensors file with a JSON-object header, else the V4 detail."""
    try:
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            raise OSError
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            head = f.read(8)
            if len(head) != 8:
                return "%s: not a safetensors file" % path
            (n,) = struct.unpack("<Q", head)
            if not 0 < n <= SAFETENSORS_HEADER_MAX:
                return "%s: not a safetensors file" % path
            raw = f.read(n)
    except OSError:
        return "%s is not a readable file" % path
    try:
        header = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8")) if len(raw) == n else None
    except (ValueError, RecursionError):
        header = None
    return None if isinstance(header, dict) else "%s: not a safetensors file" % path


def lora_checks(fields, ws_real, cache, smoke=False):
    """V4's records: catalog verify, then one LoRA file record per free path."""
    lists = [fields["video_loras"]] + ([] if smoke else [fields["stills_loras"]])
    ids = set(item["id"] for items in lists for item in items if item.get("id") is not None)
    out = []
    try:
        if fields["auto_lora"] is True and fields["story_legacy"] is False and not smoke:
            m = fields["model"] or DEFAULT_MODEL
            ids.update(e["id"] for e in catalog_list(ws_real, m, cache)["entries"] if e.get("target") == "video")
            if fields["mode"] != "no_stills":
                ids.update(e["id"] for e in catalog_list(ws_real, STILLS_BASE_MODEL, cache)["entries"]
                           if e.get("target") == "stills")
    except _ToolError as e:
        out.append(check("V4", "catalog verify", "fail", str(e)))
    else:
        if not ids:
            out.append(check("V4", "catalog verify", "skip", "no catalog LoRA chosen or pickable"))
        else:
            run = run_light(tool_argv(ws_real, "lora-catalog", "verify", *sorted(ids)), ws_real)
            out.append(check("V4", "catalog verify", "pass" if run["exit_code"] == 0 else "fail",
                             run["stdout"] + run["stderr"]))
    for items in lists:
        for item in items:
            if item.get("id") is None:
                problem = safetensors_problem(item["path"])
                out.append(check("V4", "LoRA file %s" % item["path"], "fail" if problem else "pass",
                                 problem or "%s: safetensors header ok" % item["path"]))
    return out


def _compat_problems(items, which, entries, target, model):
    problems = []
    for item in items:
        if item.get("id") is None:
            continue
        entry = entries.get(item["id"])
        if entry is None or entry.get("target") != target:
            problems.append("%s is not a %s LoRA compatible with %s" % (item["id"], target, model))
            continue
        if item["path"] != entry.get("path") or item.get("kind") != entry.get("kind"):
            problems.append("%s: path or kind differs from the catalog; reload the LoRA list" % item["id"])
        rng = entry.get("strength_range")
        if isinstance(rng, list) and len(rng) == 2 and not rng[0] <= item["strength"] <= rng[1]:
            problems.append("%s: strength %s is outside %s-%s" % (item["id"], item["strength"], rng[0], rng[1]))
    if sum(1 for item in items if item.get("id") is not None and item.get("kind") == "style") > 1:
        problems.append("at most one style LoRA among the %s LoRAs" % which)
    return problems


def _same_model(a, b):
    return a == b or (isinstance(a, str) and isinstance(b, str) and os.path.isabs(a) and os.path.isabs(b)
                      and os.path.realpath(a) == os.path.realpath(b))


def compat_checks(fields, ws_real, cache, smoke=False):
    """V5's records: catalog compatibility, then per cast character its record and, when it has a
    video LoRA, its video base model record (a mismatch is skip, never fail: D2, Q2)."""
    m = fields["model"] or DEFAULT_MODEL
    video = [i for i in fields["video_loras"] if i.get("id") is not None]
    stills = [] if smoke else [i for i in fields["stills_loras"] if i.get("id") is not None]
    out = []
    if not video and not stills:
        out.append(check("V5", "catalog compatibility", "skip", "no catalog LoRA chosen"))
    else:
        try:
            problems = []
            if video:
                entries = dict((e["id"], e) for e in catalog_list(ws_real, m, cache)["entries"])
                problems += _compat_problems(video, "video", entries, "video", m)
            if stills:
                entries = dict((e["id"], e) for e in catalog_list(ws_real, STILLS_BASE_MODEL, cache)["entries"])
                problems += _compat_problems(stills, "stills", entries, "stills", STILLS_BASE_MODEL)
        except _ToolError as e:
            problems = [str(e)]
        out.append(check("V5", "catalog compatibility", "fail" if problems else "pass",
                         "; ".join(problems) or "every catalog LoRA is compatible"))
    if smoke:
        return out + [not_needed("V5", "characters")]
    if not fields["characters"]:
        out.append(check("V5", "characters", "skip", "no character cast"))
    for name in fields["characters"]:
        run = run_light(tool_argv(ws_real, "character", "show", name), ws_real)
        if run["exit_code"] != 0:
            out.append(check("V5", "character %s" % name, "fail", run["stderr"].strip() or run["stdout"].strip()))
            continue
        try:
            data, _end = json.JSONDecoder().raw_decode(run["stdout"].lstrip())
        except ValueError:
            data = None
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            out.append(check("V5", "character %s" % name, "fail", "cannot parse bin/character show %s" % name))
            continue
        loras = data.get("loras") if isinstance(data.get("loras"), dict) else {}
        stills_lora = loras.get("stills") if isinstance(loras.get("stills"), dict) else None
        if data.get("status") != "trained":
            out.append(check("V5", "character %s" % name, "fail",
                             "character %s is not trained (status %s)" % (name, data.get("status"))))
        elif stills_lora is not None and stills_lora.get("base_model") != STILLS_BASE_MODEL:
            out.append(check("V5", "character %s" % name, "fail",
                             "character %s's stills LoRA is for %s, not %s"
                             % (name, stills_lora.get("base_model"), STILLS_BASE_MODEL)))
        else:
            out.append(check("V5", "character %s" % name, "pass", "trained"))
        video_lora = loras.get("video") if isinstance(loras.get("video"), dict) else None
        if video_lora is not None:
            base = video_lora.get("base_model")
            if _same_model(base, m):
                out.append(check("V5", "character %s video base model" % name, "pass", "trained on %s" % base))
            else:
                out.append(check("V5", "character %s video base model" % name, "skip",
                                 "video LoRA trained on %s; bin/ltx-movie does not check this against --model %s"
                                 % (base, m)))
    return out


def dry_run_check(fields, ws_real, state):
    """V6: build_argv(fields) + ["--dry-run"] with STORY_PIPELINE_LOGGED=1 (D17)."""
    argv = build_argv(fields, ws_real, state) + ["--dry-run"]
    run = run_light(argv, ws_real, env=dict(os.environ, STORY_PIPELINE_LOGGED="1"), timeout=DRY_RUN_TIMEOUT)
    if run["timed_out"]:
        return check("V6", "dry run", "fail", "bin/ltx-movie --dry-run timed out after %d s" % DRY_RUN_TIMEOUT)
    return check("V6", "dry run", "pass" if run["exit_code"] == 0 else "fail", run["stdout"] + run["stderr"])


V_GROUP_NAMES = (("V2", "host can run it"), ("V3", "models present and intact"), ("V4", "LoRAs intact"),
                 ("V5", "compatible"), ("V6", "dry run"))


def run_checks(fields, ws_real, state, py=sys.executable):
    """MU43: V1; on a V1 failure one skip per V2-V6; otherwise V2-V6 in order, no short circuit."""
    errors = validate_fields(fields, ws_real, state)
    v1 = check("V1", "fields", "fail" if errors else "pass", "\n".join(errors))
    if errors:
        return [v1] + [check(i, n, "skip", "skipped: V1 failed") for i, n in V_GROUP_NAMES]
    cache = {}
    return ([v1] + host_checks(fields, ws_real, py) + model_checks(fields, ws_real)
            + lora_checks(fields, ws_real, cache) + compat_checks(fields, ws_real, cache)
            + [dry_run_check(fields, ws_real, state)])
```

### Task 9: The incremental progress parser

Implements MU55 (byte-incremental feed, the CR collapse, the nine regexes, `last_line` with the incomplete tail) and MU56 (the progress objects of story, movie and character jobs; the smoke object arrives with Task 17), and writes the four canned logs of spec 14.2. Tests: MUT16.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/test_movie_ui_progress.py`
- Modify: `movie_ui_lib.py`
- Create (generated by a Run step): `tests/fixtures/movie_ui/render-chained.log`
- Create (generated by a Run step): `tests/fixtures/movie_ui/render-shots-redo.log`
- Create (generated by a Run step): `tests/fixtures/movie_ui/story-only.log`
- Create (generated by a Run step): `tests/fixtures/movie_ui/character-face.log`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's `parse_utc`; Task 6's `story_dir`, `resolved_panels`.
- Produces: `LINE_CLIP = 300`, `FEED_MAX = 1048576`, the regexes `PHASE_RE`, `REDO_RE`, `TOTAL_RE`, `PANEL_HDR_RE`, `OK_RE`, `REUSE_RE`, `FAILED_RE`, `SUMMARY_RE`, `FACE_RE`, `STILL_NAME_RE`; `collapse(line) -> str`; `job_phases(kind, fields) -> [str]`; class `ProgressParser(kind, fields)` with attributes `offset`, `tail`, `phase`, `phase_title`, `rewrite`, `redo`, `panels_total`, `panels`, `wrap_up`, `face_problem`, `last_line` and methods `feed(data)`, `current_last_line()`, `snapshot(job, status, state, now=None) -> dict`.

**1.2.** **Create** `tests/test_movie_ui_progress.py` with exactly this content:

```python
"""movie-ui progress parser (movie-ui spec MUT16, MU55, MU56).
Run from the workspace root: python3 -m pytest tests/test_movie_ui_progress.py -v"""

import os

import movie_ui_testutil as mt
import movie_ui_lib as lib


def _read(name):
    with open(os.path.join(mt.FIXTURES, name), "rb") as f:
        return f.read()


def _job(tmp_path, kind, fields, story_id="demo"):
    return {"id": "20261019-142233-a1b2", "kind": kind, "workspace": str(tmp_path), "story_id": story_id,
            "fields": fields}


def _parse(name, kind, fields, slices=None):
    parser = lib.ProgressParser(kind, fields)
    data = _read(name)
    if slices is None:
        parser.feed(data)
    else:
        for i in range(0, len(data), slices):
            parser.feed(data[i:i + slices])
    return parser


def test_mut16_render_chained(tmp_path):
    fields = {"action": "movie", "mode": "chained", "panels": 3, "seed_upload": None}
    parser = _parse("render-chained.log", "movie", fields)
    progress = parser.snapshot(_job(tmp_path, "movie", fields), {"started": lib.utc_now()}, str(tmp_path))
    assert progress == {
        "phase": "4", "phase_title": "render", "rewrite": None, "phases": ["1", "1b", "2", "3", "4"], "redo": None,
        "panels_total": 3,
        "panels": {"1": {"state": "ok", "reused": False}, "2": {"state": "ok", "reused": False},
                   "3": {"state": "running"}},
        "panels_done": 2, "wrap_up": False, "stills": None, "last_line": "ltx-2-mlx: 60%"}
    sliced = _parse("render-chained.log", "movie", fields, slices=7)
    assert sliced.snapshot(_job(tmp_path, "movie", fields), None, str(tmp_path)) == progress
    assert parser.offset == len(_read("render-chained.log"))


def test_mut16_shots_redo_failed_then_summary(tmp_path):
    fields = {"action": "redo", "mode": "shots", "panels": 4, "seed_upload": None, "redo": [2, 4]}
    progress = _parse("render-shots-redo.log", "movie", fields).snapshot(
        _job(tmp_path, "movie", fields), None, str(tmp_path))
    assert progress["phases"] == ["1", "1b", "redo", "2", "3", "4"]
    assert progress["redo"] == "2,4" and progress["phase"] == "4"
    assert progress["panels"] == {
        "1": {"state": "ok", "reused": True}, "2": {"state": "ok", "reused": False},
        "3": {"state": "ok", "reused": True},
        "4": {"state": "failed", "reason": "timeout, rc=-9", "detail": "ltx-2-mlx exceeded --panel-timeout 7200s"}}
    assert progress["panels_done"] == 3 and progress["panels_total"] == 4 and progress["wrap_up"] is True
    assert progress["last_line"] == "Error: 1 panel(s) failed"


def test_mut16_story_only_rewrite_and_phases(tmp_path):
    fields = {"action": "story", "mode": "chained", "panels": 6, "seed_upload": "a" * 64 + ".png"}
    progress = _parse("story-only.log", "story", fields).snapshot(_job(tmp_path, "story", fields), None, str(tmp_path))
    assert progress["phases"] == ["0", "1"]
    assert (progress["phase"], progress["phase_title"], progress["rewrite"]) == ("1", "story", [1, 2])
    assert progress["panels"] == {} and progress["panels_total"] is None and progress["panels_done"] == 0
    assert progress["last_line"] == "story.md written: /ws/generated/stories/demo/story.md"
    assert lib.job_phases("movie", {"action": "movie", "mode": "no_stills"}) == ["1", "1b", "3", "4"]
    assert lib.job_phases("movie", {"action": "movie", "mode": "chained", "seed_upload": "x.png"}) == [
        "0", "1", "1b", "2", "3", "4"]


def test_mut16_stills_count_during_phase_2(tmp_path):
    images = os.path.join(str(tmp_path), "generated", "stories", "demo", "images")
    os.makedirs(images)
    for name in ("panel_01.png", "panel_02.png", "panel_3.png", "seed.png"):
        open(os.path.join(images, name), "wb").close()
    for mode, expected in (("chained", 1), ("shots", 5)):
        fields = {"action": "movie", "mode": mode, "panels": 5, "seed_upload": None}
        parser = lib.ProgressParser("movie", fields)
        parser.feed(b"=== Phase 1: story ===\n=== Phase 2: stills ===\n")
        assert parser.snapshot(_job(tmp_path, "movie", fields), None, str(tmp_path))["stills"] == {
            "expected": expected, "count": 2}


def test_mut16_cr_collapse_and_character_face(tmp_path):
    assert lib.collapse("a\rb\rc\r") == "c"
    assert lib.collapse("plain") == "plain"
    parser = lib.ProgressParser("movie", {"action": "movie", "mode": "chained"})
    parser.feed(b"=== panel 1/2 (x) ===\nstep 1\rstep 2")
    assert parser.current_last_line() == "step 2"
    parser.feed(b"\rpanel 1 ok: /c.mp4 (1.0s)\n")
    assert parser.panels == {"1": {"state": "ok", "reused": False}}
    parser.feed(("x" * 400 + "\n").encode())
    assert len(parser.current_last_line()) == 300
    fields = {"name": "kyra"}
    face = _parse("character-face.log", "character-create", fields)
    snap = face.snapshot(_job(tmp_path, "character-create", fields, story_id=None),
                         {"started": "2026-10-19T10:00:00Z"}, str(tmp_path), now=lib.parse_utc("2026-10-19T10:00:42Z"))
    assert snap == {"kind": "character-create", "elapsed_s": 42, "last_line": "[movie-ui] exit 2 (failed)",
                    "face_problem": True}
    other = lib.ProgressParser("character-train", fields)
    other.feed(b"Error: the vision model found no face in the seed image; pass --force to proceed anyway.\n")
    assert other.face_problem is True
    assert lib.ProgressParser("character-train", fields).snapshot(
        _job(tmp_path, "character-train", fields), None, str(tmp_path))["elapsed_s"] is None
```

**3.1.** **Append** to `movie_ui_lib.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The progress parser (MU55, MU56)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

LINE_CLIP = 300
FEED_MAX = 1048576
PHASE_RE = re.compile(r"^=== Phase (\d+b?): (.+?)(?: \(rewrite (\d+) of (\d+)\))? ===$")
REDO_RE = re.compile(r"^=== Redo: panels (.+) ===$")
TOTAL_RE = re.compile(r"^panels: (\d+)$")
PANEL_HDR_RE = re.compile(r"^=== panel (\d+)/(\d+) \(")
OK_RE = re.compile(r"^panel (\d+) ok: ")
REUSE_RE = re.compile(r"^panel (\d+): reusing ")
FAILED_RE = re.compile(r"^panel (\d+) FAILED \(([^)]*)\):? ?(.*)$")
SUMMARY_RE = re.compile(r"^=== render summary \(")
FACE_RE = re.compile(
    r"^Error: (?:the vision model found no face in the seed image; pass --force to proceed anyway\."
    r"|the main character's face fills only \d+% of the seed image's height \(minimum 15%\); a small "
    r"face makes a weak identity reference\. Use a closer crop of the character, or pass --force to "
    r"proceed anyway\.)$")
STILL_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"^panel_\d{2}\.png$")


def collapse(line):
    """The text after the last carriage return of a line (pipeline_log.collapse_cr's rule)."""
    return line.rstrip("\r").rsplit("\r", 1)[-1]


def job_phases(kind, fields):
    """MU56's "phases", mirroring bin/ltx-movie's _phase_sequence."""
    seed = ["0"] if fields.get("seed_upload") else []
    if kind == "story":
        return seed + ["1"]
    return (seed + ["1", "1b"] + (["redo"] if fields.get("action") == "redo" else [])
            + ([] if fields.get("mode") == "no_stills" else ["2"]) + ["3", "4"])


class ProgressParser(object):
    """Incremental log.txt parser for one job (MU55). feed() takes raw bytes; a line split across
    feeds is parsed once, whole."""

    def __init__(self, kind, fields):
        self.kind, self.fields = kind, fields
        self.offset = 0
        self.tail = b""
        self.phase = self.phase_title = self.rewrite = self.redo = self.panels_total = None
        self.panels = {}
        self.wrap_up = False
        self.face_problem = False
        self.last_line = ""

    def feed(self, data):
        self.offset += len(data)
        lines = (self.tail + data).split(b"\n")
        self.tail = lines.pop()
        for raw in lines:
            self._line(collapse(raw.decode("utf-8", "replace")))

    def current_last_line(self):
        tail = collapse(self.tail.decode("utf-8", "replace"))
        return tail[:LINE_CLIP] if tail.strip() else self.last_line

    def _line(self, line):
        if line.strip():
            self.last_line = line[:LINE_CLIP]
        match = PHASE_RE.match(line)
        if match:
            self.phase, self.phase_title = match.group(1), match.group(2)
            self.rewrite = [int(match.group(3)), int(match.group(4))] if match.group(3) else None
            return
        match = REDO_RE.match(line)
        if match:
            self.phase, self.redo = "redo", match.group(1)
            return
        match = TOTAL_RE.match(line)
        if match:
            self.panels_total = int(match.group(1))
            return
        match = PANEL_HDR_RE.match(line)
        if match:
            self.panels_total = int(match.group(2))
            self.panels[match.group(1)] = {"state": "running"}
            return
        match = OK_RE.match(line)
        if match:
            self.panels[match.group(1)] = {"state": "ok", "reused": False}
            return
        match = REUSE_RE.match(line)
        if match:
            self.panels[match.group(1)] = {"state": "ok", "reused": True}
            return
        match = FAILED_RE.match(line)
        if match:
            self.panels[match.group(1)] = {"state": "failed", "reason": match.group(2),
                                           "detail": match.group(3)[:LINE_CLIP]}
            return
        if SUMMARY_RE.match(line):
            self.wrap_up = True
            return
        if FACE_RE.match(line):
            self.face_problem = True

    def snapshot(self, job, status, state, now=None):
        """The MU56 progress object for job (its job.json) and status (its status.json or None)."""
        started = parse_utc((status or {}).get("started"))
        current = time.time() if now is None else now
        elapsed = None if started is None else int(current - started)
        if self.kind not in ("story", "movie"):
            return {"kind": self.kind, "elapsed_s": elapsed, "last_line": self.current_last_line(),
                    "face_problem": self.face_problem}
        stills = None
        if self.phase == "2":
            images = os.path.join(story_dir(job["workspace"], job["story_id"]), "images")
            try:
                count = sum(1 for n in os.listdir(images) if STILL_NAME_RE.fullmatch(n))
            except OSError:
                count = 0
            expected = resolved_panels(self.fields) if self.fields.get("mode") == "shots" else 1
            stills = {"expected": expected, "count": count}
        return {"phase": self.phase, "phase_title": self.phase_title, "rewrite": self.rewrite,
                "phases": job_phases(self.kind, self.fields), "redo": self.redo,
                "panels_total": self.panels_total,
                "panels": dict((k, dict(v)) for k, v in sorted(self.panels.items(), key=lambda kv: int(kv[0]))),
                "panels_done": sum(1 for v in self.panels.values() if v["state"] == "ok"),
                "wrap_up": self.wrap_up, "stills": stills, "last_line": self.current_last_line()}
```
