### Task 10: `movie_ui_server`: the hardened loopback HTTP layer and the route table

Implements MU4 (`make_server`, `MovieUIServer`), MU5's in-memory token, MU7's request-log rule, MU9, MU10 (Host, Origin, static, token, route, body, dispatch), MU11 (all 35 routes; their handlers arrive in Tasks 11-15, so until then a matched route with a valid token answers 500), MU12's header set, MU13 and MU14. Tests: MUT18 and MU13.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/test_movie_ui_http.py`
- Create: `movie_ui_server.py`
- Modify: `tests/movie_ui_testutil.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `movie_ui_lib` as `lib` (Tasks 2-9: `render_check`, `stills_check`, `story_server_check`, `utc_now`, `read_job`, `job_dir`, `ProgressParser`, `FEED_MAX`, `valid_story_id`); `fleet_lib` (imported; used from Task 11).
- Produces, in `movie_ui_server`: `UI_WS`, `STORE_LOCK` (a `threading.Lock`), `MAX_BODY`, `UPLOAD_MAX`, `LOG_CHUNK`, `HISTORY_MAX`, `FILE_CHUNK`, `TOKEN_HEADER = "X-Movie-UI-Token"`, `PAGE_CSP`, `STATIC`, `OFFSET_RE`, `N_RE`, `RANGE_RE`, `UPLOAD_TYPES`, `FILE_TYPES`, `ROUTES` (35 `(method, regex, handler name, sample path)`); `ApiError(status, message, **extra)` (`.status`, `.body`); `percent_decode(text)`; `parse_query(qs) -> dict` (raises `ValueError`); `resolve_ws(value) -> realpath` (raises `ApiError(400)`); `MovieUIServer(port, handler, state, ui_ws, token)` with `.port`, `.state`, `.ui_ws`, `.token`, `.probe_ws`, `.stills_py`, `.capabilities`, `refresh_capabilities()`, `capability_snapshot()`, `job(job_id)`, `progress(job_id, job, status)`, `.parsers`, `.parser_lock`, `.job_cache`; `make_server(port, state, ui_ws, token)`; `MovieUIHandler` with `send_json(status, obj, headers=())`, `ws(value)`, `story_id(value)` and the handler protocol: a handler is `h_<name>(self, groups, query, body)` returning `(status, obj)`, or `None` after writing its own response, or raising `ApiError`.
- Produces, in testutil: `TOKEN`, `Running(state, ui_ws=WS)` (`.server`, `.port`, `close()`), `api(server, method, path, body=None, token=TOKEN, headers=None, raw=None) -> (status, headers dict, JSON or bytes)` (a header value of `None` omits that header).

**1.1.** **Append** to `tests/movie_ui_testutil.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# In-process HTTP
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

TOKEN = "test-token-not-a-secret"


class Running(object):
    """A movie-ui server on 127.0.0.1:<free port>, serving on a thread."""

    def __init__(self, state, ui_ws=WS):
        import movie_ui_server
        self.server = movie_ui_server.make_server(0, state, ui_ws, TOKEN)
        self.port = self.server.port
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
        self.thread.start()

    def close(self):
        self.server.shutdown()
        self.server.server_close()


def api(server, method, path, body=None, token=TOKEN, headers=None, raw=None):
    """One http.client request to a Running server; returns (status, headers dict, parsed JSON
    or bytes). body is sent as JSON; raw is sent as-is."""
    conn = http.client.HTTPConnection("127.0.0.1", server.port, timeout=60)
    hdrs = {"Host": "127.0.0.1:%d" % server.port}
    data = None
    if body is not None:
        data = json.dumps(body).encode("utf-8")
        hdrs["Content-Type"] = "application/json"
    if raw is not None:
        data = raw
    if token is not None:
        hdrs["X-Movie-UI-Token"] = token
    hdrs.update(headers or {})
    try:
        conn.putrequest(method, path, skip_host=True, skip_accept_encoding=True)
        for key, value in hdrs.items():
            if value is not None:
                conn.putheader(key, value)
        if data is not None and "Content-Length" not in hdrs:
            conn.putheader("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
        conn.endheaders()
        if data is not None:
            conn.send(data)
        resp = conn.getresponse()
        payload = resp.read()
        result_headers = dict((k.lower(), v) for k, v in resp.getheaders())
        if result_headers.get("content-type", "").startswith("application/json"):
            payload = json.loads(payload.decode("utf-8"))
        return resp.status, result_headers, payload
    finally:
        conn.close()
```

**1.2.** **Create** `tests/test_movie_ui_http.py` with exactly this content:

```python
"""movie-ui HTTP layer and endpoint tests (movie-ui spec MUT2, MUT9, MUT13, MUT14, MUT17-MUT21,
MUT23, MUT24). Run from the workspace root: python3 -m pytest tests/test_movie_ui_http.py -v
Every server binds 127.0.0.1 on a free port under a temporary HOME; requests use http.client."""

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import shlex
import shutil
import signal
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time

import pytest

import movie_ui_testutil as mt
import movie_ui_lib as lib
import movie_ui_server as srv


@pytest.fixture
def env(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    state = mt.set_home(monkeypatch, tmp_path)
    ws = mt.make_stub_workspace(tmp_path)
    running = mt.Running(state)
    yield {"state": state, "ws": ws, "tmp": str(tmp_path), "srv": running, "home": os.environ["HOME"]}
    running.close()
    mt.kill_everything(state)


def test_mu13_query_parsing():
    assert srv.parse_query("a=1&b=x+y&c=%2Fp%20q&a=2&&d") == {"a": "2", "b": "x y", "c": "/p q", "d": ""}
    assert srv.parse_query("p=%C3%A9%e2%9c%93") == {"p": "é✓"}
    assert srv.parse_query("p=100%") == {"p": "100%"}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        srv.parse_query("p=%ff")


def test_mut18_every_api_route_needs_the_token(env):
    s = env["srv"]
    assert len(srv.ROUTES) == 35
    for method, regex, name, sample in srv.ROUTES:
        assert re.fullmatch(regex, sample), sample
        body = {} if method in ("POST", "PUT") else None
        for token in (None, "wrong-token"):
            status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, method, sample, body=body, token=token)
            assert (status, payload) == (401, {"error": "missing or invalid token"}), (method, sample, token)
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/x", token=None)[0] == 401
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/x")[2] == {"error": "not found"}
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/x")[0] == 404
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/nothing")[0] == 404
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "DELETE", "/api/host")
    assert (status, payload) == (405, {"error": "method not allowed"})
    assert mt.api(s, "PUT", "/api/jobs", body={})[0] == 405


def test_mut18_host_origin_and_body_checks(env):
    s = env["srv"]
    port = s.port
    status, headers, payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/host", headers={"Host": "evil:%d" % port})
    assert (status, payload) == (403, {"error": "forbidden host"})
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/host", headers={"Host": "127.0.0.1"})[0] == 403
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/host", headers={"Host": "[::1]:%d" % port})[0] == 403
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/x", headers={"Host": "localhost:%d" % port})[0] == 404
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/x", headers={"Origin": "http://evil"})
    assert (status, payload) == (403, {"error": "forbidden origin"})
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/x", headers={"Origin": "http://localhost:%d" % port})[0] == 404
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/x", headers={"Origin": "http://127.0.0.1:%d" % port})[0] == 404
    status, headers, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/preview", raw=b"",
                                      headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "Content-Length": None})
    assert (status, payload) == (411, {"error": "Content-Length required"})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/preview", raw=b"",
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "Content-Length": str(26214401)})
    assert (status, payload) == (413, {"error": "request body too large"})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/preview", raw=b"{}", headers={"Content-Type": "text/plain"})
    assert (status, payload) == (415, {"error": "unsupported content type"})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/preview", raw=b"{nope",
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8"})
    assert (status, payload) == (400, {"error": "invalid JSON body"})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/preview", raw=b"[1]", headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    assert (status, payload) == (400, {"error": "body must be a JSON object"})
    status, headers, payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/x?q=%ff")
    assert (status, payload) == (404, {"error": "not found"})
    assert headers["content-type"] == "application/json; charset=utf-8"
    assert headers["cache-control"] == "no-store" and headers["x-content-type-options"] == "nosniff"
    assert not any(k.startswith("access-control-") for k in headers)
    with pytest.raises(srv.ApiError) as e:
        srv.resolve_ws(None)
    assert (e.value.status, e.value.body) == (400, {"error": "missing ws"})
    for value, message in (("relative", "ws must be an absolute path"),
                           ("/nonexistent", "not a harness workspace (no bin/ltx-movie): /nonexistent")):
        with pytest.raises(srv.ApiError) as e:
            srv.resolve_ws(value)
        assert e.value.body == {"error": message}
    assert srv.resolve_ws(env["ws"] + "/bin/..") == env["ws"]


def test_mu7_no_request_log_below_500_and_a_500_line(env, capfd):
    s = env["srv"]
    mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/x?t=secret")
    mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/x", token=None)

    def boom(self, groups, query, body):
        raise RuntimeError("kaput")
    srv.MovieUIHandler.h_boom = boom
    srv.ROUTES.append(("GET", r"/api/boom", "h_boom", "/api/boom"))
    try:
        status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/boom?t=secret")
    finally:
        srv.ROUTES.pop()
        del srv.MovieUIHandler.h_boom
    assert (status, payload) == (500, {"error": "internal error"})
    err = capfd.readouterr().err
    assert err == "movie-ui: GET /api/boom -> 500 (RuntimeError: kaput)\n"
```

**3.1.** **Create** `movie_ui_server.py` with exactly this content:

```python
"""movie_ui_server -- the HTTP side of movie-ui: the hardened loopback server, the route table,
the JSON API, /files, the dispatcher and capability-probe threads, the trace proxy and main().

See docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-movie-ui-design.md (the movie-ui spec, piece F).
Standard library only, plus movie_ui_lib and this workspace's own fleet_lib (read-only names
only, GC7). Outbound HTTP goes only through fleet_lib's http.client helpers (GC3); query strings
are parsed by the local parse_query. Valid on Python 3.9 and 3.13 (GC1).
"""

import argparse
import errno
import fcntl
import hashlib
import hmac
import http.server
import json
import os
import platform
import re
import secrets
import shlex
import signal
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time

import fleet_lib
import movie_ui_lib as lib

UI_WS = os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(__file__))
STORE_LOCK = threading.Lock()   # MU18: guards queue.json, server-side status writes, dispatch
MAX_BODY = 26214400             # 25 MiB (MU10)
UPLOAD_MAX = 20971520           # 20 MiB (MU53)
LOG_CHUNK = 262144              # MU58
HISTORY_MAX = 200               # MU67
FILE_CHUNK = 65536              # MU54
TOKEN_HEADER = "X-Movie-UI-Token"
PAGE_CSP = ("default-src 'self'; img-src 'self' blob:; media-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; "
            "base-uri 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'; form-action 'self'")
STATIC = {"/": ("index.html", "text/html; charset=utf-8"),
          "/static/app.js": ("app.js", "text/javascript; charset=utf-8"),
          "/static/app.css": ("app.css", "text/css; charset=utf-8")}
OFFSET_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9]{1,15}$")
N_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9]{1,3}$")
RANGE_RE = re.compile(r"^bytes=(\d*)-(\d*)$")
UPLOAD_TYPES = {"image/png": ("png", b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"), "image/jpeg": ("jpg", b"\xff\xd8\xff"),
                "image/webp": ("webp", None)}
FILE_TYPES = {".png": "image/png", ".jpg": "image/jpeg", ".jpeg": "image/jpeg", ".webp": "image/webp",
              ".mp4": "video/mp4", ".json": "application/json", ".md": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
              ".txt": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", ".log": "text/plain; charset=utf-8"}

_ID = r"([0-9]{8}-[0-9]{6}-[0-9a-f]{4})"
_STORY = r"([A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,79})"
_NAME = r"([a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,23})"
_TRACE = r"([0-9a-f]{32})"
_SAMPLE_ID = "20261019-142233-a1b2"
_SAMPLE_TRACE = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef"

# MU11: (method, regex, handler name, sample path), matched with re.fullmatch in this order.
ROUTES = [
    ("GET", r"/api/host", "h_host", "/api/host"),
    ("GET", r"/api/workspaces", "h_workspaces", "/api/workspaces"),
    ("GET", r"/api/options", "h_options", "/api/options"),
    ("GET", r"/api/loras", "h_loras", "/api/loras"),
    ("POST", r"/api/loras", "h_lora_add", "/api/loras"),
    ("POST", r"/api/loras/remove", "h_lora_remove", "/api/loras/remove"),
    ("POST", r"/api/loras/verify", "h_lora_verify", "/api/loras/verify"),
    ("GET", r"/api/models", "h_models", "/api/models"),
    ("GET", r"/api/characters", "h_characters", "/api/characters"),
    ("POST", r"/api/characters", "h_character_create", "/api/characters"),
    ("GET", r"/api/characters/" + _NAME, "h_character", "/api/characters/kyra"),
    ("POST", r"/api/characters/" + _NAME + r"/train", "h_character_train", "/api/characters/kyra/train"),
    ("POST", r"/api/characters/" + _NAME + r"/regenerate", "h_character_regenerate",
     "/api/characters/kyra/regenerate"),
    ("POST", r"/api/uploads", "h_upload", "/api/uploads"),
    ("POST", r"/api/preview", "h_preview", "/api/preview"),
    ("POST", r"/api/validate", "h_validate", "/api/validate"),
    ("GET", r"/api/jobs", "h_jobs", "/api/jobs"),
    ("POST", r"/api/jobs", "h_job_create", "/api/jobs"),
    ("GET", r"/api/jobs/" + _ID, "h_job", "/api/jobs/" + _SAMPLE_ID),
    ("DELETE", r"/api/jobs/" + _ID, "h_job_delete", "/api/jobs/" + _SAMPLE_ID),
    ("GET", r"/api/jobs/" + _ID + r"/log", "h_job_log", "/api/jobs/" + _SAMPLE_ID + "/log"),
    ("POST", r"/api/jobs/" + _ID + r"/cancel", "h_job_cancel", "/api/jobs/" + _SAMPLE_ID + "/cancel"),
    ("POST", r"/api/jobs/" + _ID + r"/kill-orphan", "h_job_kill_orphan",
     "/api/jobs/" + _SAMPLE_ID + "/kill-orphan"),
    ("POST", r"/api/queue/move", "h_queue_move", "/api/queue/move"),
    ("GET", r"/api/stories", "h_stories", "/api/stories"),
    ("GET", r"/api/stories/" + _STORY, "h_story", "/api/stories/ronin-rescue"),
    ("PUT", r"/api/stories/" + _STORY + r"/story\.md", "h_story_save", "/api/stories/ronin-rescue/story.md"),
    ("GET", r"/api/traces/agents", "h_trace_agents", "/api/traces/agents"),
    ("GET", r"/api/traces/recent", "h_traces_recent", "/api/traces/recent"),
    ("GET", r"/api/traces/saved", "h_traces_saved", "/api/traces/saved"),
    ("GET", r"/api/traces/saved/" + _TRACE, "h_trace_saved", "/api/traces/saved/" + _SAMPLE_TRACE),
    ("DELETE", r"/api/traces/saved/" + _TRACE, "h_trace_saved_delete", "/api/traces/saved/" + _SAMPLE_TRACE),
    ("GET", r"/api/traces/" + _TRACE, "h_trace", "/api/traces/" + _SAMPLE_TRACE),
    ("POST", r"/api/traces/" + _TRACE + r"/save", "h_trace_save", "/api/traces/" + _SAMPLE_TRACE + "/save"),
    ("GET", r"/files", "h_files", "/files"),
]


class ApiError(Exception):
    """Raised by a handler to answer status with {"error": message, **extra}."""

    def __init__(self, status, message, **extra):
        Exception.__init__(self, message)
        self.status, self.body = status, dict({"error": message}, **extra)


def percent_decode(text):
    """+ becomes a space and %XX a byte; the bytes are decoded UTF-8 strictly (MU13). Raises
    ValueError on invalid UTF-8."""
    raw = text.replace("+", " ").encode("latin-1", "strict")
    out, i = bytearray(), 0
    while i < len(raw):
        if raw[i:i + 1] == b"%" and re.fullmatch(rb"[0-9A-Fa-f]{2}", raw[i + 1:i + 3]):
            out.append(int(raw[i + 1:i + 3], 16))
            i += 3
        else:
            out.append(raw[i])
            i += 1
    return bytes(out).decode("utf-8", "strict")


def parse_query(qs):
    """{key: value} from a query string; a repeated key keeps the last value (MU13). Raises
    ValueError on a decode error."""
    out = {}
    for item in qs.split("&"):
        if not item:
            continue
        key, _sep, value = item.partition("=")
        out[percent_decode(key)] = percent_decode(value)
    return out


def resolve_ws(value):
    """The realpath of a harness workspace, or ApiError(400) (MU14)."""
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        raise ApiError(400, "missing ws")
    if not os.path.isabs(value):
        raise ApiError(400, "ws must be an absolute path")
    real = os.path.realpath(value)
    if not (os.path.isdir(real) and os.path.isfile(os.path.join(real, "bin", "ltx-movie"))):
        raise ApiError(400, "not a harness workspace (no bin/ltx-movie): %s" % value)
    return real


class MovieUIServer(http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer):
    """The movie-ui server bound to 127.0.0.1 (MU4). probe_ws and stills_py are the workspace and
    interpreter the capability probe uses (the UI workspace and sys.executable; tests change them)."""

    daemon_threads = True
    allow_reuse_address = True

    def __init__(self, port, handler, state, ui_ws, token):
        http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer.__init__(self, ("127.0.0.1", port), handler)
        self.port = self.server_address[1]
        self.state = state
        self.ui_ws = os.path.realpath(ui_ws)
        self.token = token
        self.probe_ws = self.ui_ws
        self.stills_py = sys.executable
        self.cap_lock = threading.Lock()
        self.capabilities = dict((k, {"ok": None, "reason": "probing", "checked": None})
                                 for k in ("render", "stills", "story_server"))
        self.parser_lock = threading.Lock()
        self.parsers = {}
        self.job_cache = {}

    def refresh_capabilities(self):
        """Re-run MU60's three probes against probe_ws and store the results."""
        results = {"render": lib.render_check(), "stills": lib.stills_check(self.probe_ws, self.stills_py),
                   "story_server": lib.story_server_check(self.probe_ws)}
        checked = lib.utc_now()
        with self.cap_lock:
            for key, (ok, detail) in results.items():
                reason = detail if (key == "story_server" or not ok) else None
                self.capabilities[key] = {"ok": ok, "reason": reason, "checked": checked}

    def capability_snapshot(self):
        with self.cap_lock:
            return dict((k, dict(v)) for k, v in self.capabilities.items())

    def job(self, job_id):
        """job.json of job_id (immutable, cached by ID; MU32), or None."""
        if job_id not in self.job_cache:
            job = lib.read_job(lib.job_dir(self.state, job_id))
            if job is None:
                return None
            self.job_cache[job_id] = job
        return self.job_cache[job_id]

    def progress(self, job_id, job, status):
        """The MU56 object, feeding the job's parser the new bytes of log.txt (at most 1 MiB)."""
        with self.parser_lock:
            parser = self.parsers.get(job_id)
            if parser is None:
                parser = self.parsers[job_id] = lib.ProgressParser(job["kind"], job["fields"])
            try:
                with open(os.path.join(lib.job_dir(self.state, job_id), "log.txt"), "rb") as f:
                    f.seek(parser.offset)
                    parser.feed(f.read(lib.FEED_MAX))
            except OSError:
                pass
            return parser.snapshot(job, status, self.state)


def make_server(port, state, ui_ws, token):
    """A MovieUIServer on ("127.0.0.1", port); port 0 picks a free port (MU4). Raises OSError."""
    return MovieUIServer(port, MovieUIHandler, state, ui_ws, token)


class MovieUIHandler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    """MU9-MU14: Host, Origin, static, token, route, body, dispatch."""

    protocol_version = "HTTP/1.0"
    timeout = 30
    server_version = "movie-ui"

    def do_GET(self):
        self._handle()

    def do_POST(self):
        self._handle()

    def do_PUT(self):
        self._handle()

    def do_DELETE(self):
        self._handle()

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        """Nothing: the default request log would print ?t=<token> (MU7)."""

    def log_request(self, code="-", size="-"):
        if getattr(self, "_logged_500", False):
            return
        try:
            status = int(code)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return
        if status >= 500:
            sys.stderr.write("movie-ui: %s %s -> %d\n" % (self.command, self.path.partition("?")[0], status))

    # -- responses --------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _write(self, data):
        try:
            self.wfile.write(data)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
            pass

    def send_json(self, status, obj, headers=()):
        body = json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.send_header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
        for key, value in headers:
            self.send_header(key, value)
        self.end_headers()
        self._write(body)

    def _send_static(self, name, content_type):
        try:
            with open(os.path.join(self.server.ui_ws, "static", "movie-ui", name), "rb") as f:
                body = f.read()
        except OSError:
            self.send_json(404, {"error": "not found"})
            return
        self.send_response(200)
        for key, value in (("Content-Type", content_type), ("Content-Length", str(len(body))),
                           ("Content-Security-Policy", PAGE_CSP), ("X-Frame-Options", "DENY"),
                           ("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff"), ("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer"),
                           ("Cache-Control", "no-store")):
            self.send_header(key, value)
        self.end_headers()
        self._write(body)

    # -- the check order (MU10) -------------------------------------------------------------

    def _token_ok(self, value):
        return isinstance(value, str) and hmac.compare_digest(value.encode("utf-8"),
                                                              self.server.token.encode("utf-8"))

    def _handle(self):
        path, _sep, qs = self.path.partition("?")
        port = self.server.port
        if self.headers.get("Host") not in ("127.0.0.1:%d" % port, "localhost:%d" % port):
            self.send_json(403, {"error": "forbidden host"})
            return
        origin = self.headers.get("Origin")
        if origin is not None and origin not in ("http://127.0.0.1:%d" % port, "http://localhost:%d" % port):
            self.send_json(403, {"error": "forbidden origin"})
            return
        if self.command == "GET" and path in STATIC:
            self._send_static(*STATIC[path])
            return
        if path.startswith("/api/") or path == "/files":
            ok = self._token_ok(self.headers.get(TOKEN_HEADER))
            if not ok and path == "/files":
                try:
                    ok = self._token_ok(parse_query(qs).get("t"))
                except ValueError:
                    ok = False
            if not ok:
                self.send_json(401, {"error": "missing or invalid token"})
                return
        matched, route = False, None
        for method, regex, name, _sample in ROUTES:
            m = re.fullmatch(regex, path)
            if m:
                matched = True
                if method == self.command:
                    route = (name, m.groups())
                    break
        if route is None:
            self.send_json(405 if matched else 404, {"error": "method not allowed" if matched else "not found"})
            return
        body = None
        if self.command in ("POST", "PUT"):
            body = self._read_body(path)
            if body is None:
                return
        try:
            query = parse_query(qs)
        except ValueError:
            self.send_json(400, {"error": "invalid query string"})
            return
        try:
            result = getattr(self, route[0])(route[1], query, body)
        except ApiError as e:
            self.send_json(e.status, e.body)
            return
        except Exception as e:
            self._logged_500 = True
            sys.stderr.write("movie-ui: %s %s -> 500 (%s: %s)\n" % (self.command, path, type(e).__name__, e))
            self.send_json(500, {"error": "internal error"})
            return
        if result is not None:
            self.send_json(result[0], result[1])

    def _read_body(self, path):
        """The parsed JSON object (or the raw bytes for POST /api/uploads), or None after an error
        response (MU10 step 6)."""
        length = self.headers.get("Content-Length")
        if length is None:
            self.send_json(411, {"error": "Content-Length required"})
            return None
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]{1,15}", length.strip()):
            self.send_json(400, {"error": "invalid Content-Length"})
            return None
        n = int(length.strip())
        if n > MAX_BODY:
            self.send_json(413, {"error": "request body too large"})
            return None
        media = (self.headers.get("Content-Type") or "").split(";")[0].strip().lower()
        if self.command == "POST" and path == "/api/uploads":
            return self.rfile.read(n) if media in UPLOAD_TYPES and n <= UPLOAD_MAX else self._upload_refusal(media, n)
        if media != "application/json":
            self.send_json(415, {"error": "unsupported content type"})
            return None
        try:
            obj = json.loads(self.rfile.read(n).decode("utf-8"))
        except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError, RecursionError):
            self.send_json(400, {"error": "invalid JSON body"})
            return None
        if not isinstance(obj, dict):
            self.send_json(400, {"error": "body must be a JSON object"})
            return None
        return obj

    def _upload_refusal(self, media, n):
        if media not in UPLOAD_TYPES:
            self.send_json(415, {"error": "unsupported content type"})
        else:
            self.send_json(413, {"error": "upload larger than 20 MiB"})
        return None

    # -- helpers ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def ws(self, value):
        return resolve_ws(value)

    def story_id(self, value):
        """A URL story ID that also passes MU38's ".." rule, else ApiError(404)."""
        if not lib.valid_story_id(value):
            raise ApiError(404, "not found")
        return value
```

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Agents, registries and a copy of the UI
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def registry(host_ids, port):
    """A valid fleet registry naming host_ids, every host at port."""
    hosts = {}
    for host_id in host_ids:
        hosts[host_id] = {"role": "agent", "address": "127.0.0.1", "port": port,
                          "model_server": {"type": "vllm", "url": "http://127.0.0.1:9/v1"},
                          "model": "m", "skills": []}
    return {"protocol": 1, "fleet_id": "0123456789abcdef", "hosts": hosts}


def make_agents_home(home, agents):
    """With HOME already set to home, build ~/agents/<id> for each agents item
    {id: {"current": tag or None, "releases": {tag: {"data": bool, "bytes": n}}, "state": dict or None,
    "port": int or None, "ports": [int, ...] (one registry file each), "fleet_env": bool (an empty
    fleet.env, so an agent without current is on disk), "default": bool (the ~/.local/bin/qwen-agent
    wrapper names it)}}. Returns {id: deploy root}."""
    roots = {}
    for agent, spec in agents.items():
        a = os.path.join(str(home), "agents", agent)
        roots[agent] = a
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(a, "releases"), exist_ok=True)
        for tag, rel in spec.get("releases", {}).items():
            ws = os.path.join(a, "releases", tag, fleet_lib.WS_SUBDIR)
            os.makedirs(os.path.join(ws, "bin"), exist_ok=True)
            with open(os.path.join(ws, "bin", "ltx-movie"), "w") as f:
                f.write("#!/bin/sh\n")
            if rel.get("data"):
                story = os.path.join(ws, "generated", "stories", "old-story")
                os.makedirs(story, exist_ok=True)
                with open(os.path.join(story, "story.md"), "wb") as f:
                    f.write(b"x" * rel.get("bytes", 10))
            fleets = os.path.join(ws, "fleets")
            os.makedirs(fleets, exist_ok=True)
            ports = spec.get("ports") or ([spec["port"]] if spec.get("port") else [])
            for i, port in enumerate(ports):
                with open(os.path.join(fleets, "f%d.json" % i), "w") as f:
                    json.dump(registry([agent], port), f)
        if spec.get("current"):
            os.symlink(os.path.join("releases", spec["current"]), os.path.join(a, "current"))
        if spec.get("fleet_env"):
            open(os.path.join(a, "fleet.env"), "w").close()
        if spec.get("state") is not None:
            with open(os.path.join(a, "update-state.json"), "w") as f:
                json.dump(spec["state"], f)
        if spec.get("default"):
            bindir = os.path.join(str(home), ".local", "bin")
            os.makedirs(bindir, exist_ok=True)
            with open(os.path.join(bindir, "qwen-agent"), "wb") as f:
                f.write(fleet_lib.wrapper_bytes(agent, spec.get("port") or fleet_lib.DEFAULT_PORT))
    return roots


def free_port():
    import socket
    s = socket.socket()
    s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
    port = s.getsockname()[1]
    s.close()
    return port


def copy_ui(tmp):
    """A copy of the movie-ui files (bin/movie-ui, bin/movie-ui-job, movie_ui_lib.py,
    movie_ui_server.py, fleet_lib.py, static/movie-ui) plus the stub tools, so a subprocess
    bin/movie-ui probes stubs instead of this checkout's real bin/story-server. Returns its root."""
    root = make_stub_workspace(os.path.join(str(tmp), "ui"))
    for rel in ("bin/movie-ui", "bin/movie-ui-job", "movie_ui_lib.py", "movie_ui_server.py", "fleet_lib.py"):
        shutil.copy2(os.path.join(WS, rel), os.path.join(root, rel))
    static = os.path.join(WS, "static", "movie-ui")
    if os.path.isdir(static):
        shutil.copytree(static, os.path.join(root, "static", "movie-ui"))
    return root
```

```python
def test_mu14_ws_parameter_errors_and_options(env):
    s = env["srv"]
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/options")[2] == {"error": "missing ws"}
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/options?ws=relative")[2] == {"error": "ws must be an absolute path"}
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/options?ws=%ff")[:3:2] == (400, {"error": "invalid query string"})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/options?ws=" + env["ws"])
    assert status == 200
    assert payload["workspace"] == env["ws"] and payload["help_ok"] is True and payload["help_error"] is None
    assert payload["flags"] == sorted(payload["flags"]) and "--judge" in payload["flags"]
    assert all(payload["supported"].values()) and len(payload["supported"]) == 26
    assert payload["defaults"] == {"panels": 15, "fps": 24, "frames": 145, "model": lib.DEFAULT_MODEL,
                                   "gemma": lib.DEFAULT_GEMMA, "panel_timeout": 7200}
    with open(os.path.join(mt.FIXTURES, "ltx-movie-help-old.txt"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
        mt.stub_config(env["ws"], {"ltx-movie --help": {"stdout": f.read()}})
    os.utime(os.path.join(env["ws"], "bin", "ltx-movie"), ns=(10 ** 9, 10 ** 9))
    payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/options?ws=" + env["ws"])[2]
    assert payload["supported"]["judge"] is False and payload["supported"]["goal"] is False
    assert payload["supported"]["panels"] is True


def test_mut9_host_and_refresh(env, monkeypatch):
    s = env["srv"]
    server = s.server
    server.probe_ws = env["ws"]
    good_py = os.path.join(env["tmp"], "good-python")
    with open(good_py, "w") as f:
        f.write("#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n")
    os.chmod(good_py, 0o755)
    server.stills_py = good_py
    monkeypatch.setenv("LTX2_MLX_BIN", os.path.join(env["tmp"], "missing-ltx"))
    payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/host")[2]
    assert payload["capabilities"]["render"] == {"ok": None, "reason": "probing", "checked": None}
    assert payload["port"] == s.port and payload["state_dir"] == env["state"]
    assert payload["ui_workspace"] == mt.WS and payload["default_workspace"] == mt.WS
    assert payload["default_agent"] is None and payload["api_key_present"] is False
    assert payload["running_job"] is None and payload["queue_length"] == 0
    assert set(payload) == {"host", "platform", "python", "port", "state_dir", "ui_workspace", "default_workspace",
                            "default_agent", "api_key_present", "capabilities", "running_job", "queue_length"}
    payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/host?refresh=1")[2]
    caps = payload["capabilities"]
    assert caps["render"]["ok"] is False and caps["render"]["reason"].startswith("ltx-2-mlx binary not found")
    assert caps["stills"] == {"ok": True, "reason": None, "checked": caps["stills"]["checked"]}
    assert caps["story_server"]["ok"] is True and caps["story_server"]["reason"] == "story server: SERVING vision"
    mt.stub_config(env["ws"], {"story-server status": {"stdout": "  state:          STOPPED\n"}})
    monkeypatch.setenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "test-not-a-key")
    payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/host?refresh=1")[2]
    assert payload["capabilities"]["story_server"]["ok"] is False and payload["api_key_present"] is True


def test_mut23_workspaces_labels_default_and_prune(env, monkeypatch):
    home = env["home"]
    state = {"from": "v2026.10.02", "to": "v2026.10.05", "status": "ok", "attempts": 0, "bad_tags": []}
    mt.make_agents_home(home, {
        "mac-local": {"current": "v2026.10.05", "default": True, "port": 8190, "state": state,
                      "releases": {"v2026.10.01": {"data": True, "bytes": 1234}, "v2026.10.02": {},
                                   "v2026.09.30": {}, "v2026.10.03": {}, "v2026.10.04": {}, "v2026.10.05": {},
                                   "notes": {}}},
        "spare": {"releases": {"v2026.10.01": {}}, "fleet_env": True},
        "ghost": {"releases": {"v2026.10.01": {}}}})
    s = env["srv"]
    payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/workspaces")[2]
    a = os.path.join(home, "agents", "mac-local")
    entries = payload["workspaces"]
    assert [e["label"] for e in entries] == [
        "mac-local current (v2026.10.05)", "mac-local v2026.10.04", "mac-local v2026.10.03", "mac-local v2026.10.02",
        "mac-local v2026.10.01", "mac-local v2026.09.30", "spare v2026.10.01",
        "this checkout (%s)" % entries[-1]["commit"] if entries[-1]["commit"] else "this checkout (unknown commit)"]
    by_tag = dict((e["label"], e) for e in entries)
    cur = by_tag["mac-local current (v2026.10.05)"]
    assert cur["path"] == os.path.join(a, "current", "qwen-agent-workspace") and cur["is_current"] is True
    assert cur["prune"] is None and cur["data_bytes"] is None and cur["agent"] == "mac-local"
    assert by_tag["mac-local v2026.10.04"]["prune"] == "kept"
    assert by_tag["mac-local v2026.10.02"]["prune"] == "kept"
    assert by_tag["mac-local v2026.10.01"]["prune"] == "kept_for_data"
    assert by_tag["mac-local v2026.10.01"]["data_bytes"] == 1234
    assert by_tag["mac-local v2026.10.01"]["stories"] == 1
    assert by_tag["mac-local v2026.09.30"]["prune"] == "would_prune"
    assert by_tag["mac-local v2026.09.30"]["data_bytes"] == 0 and by_tag["mac-local v2026.09.30"]["is_current"] is False
    assert entries[-1]["is_ui"] is True and entries[-1]["agent"] is None and entries[-1]["prune"] is None
    assert set(cur) == {"path", "realpath", "label", "agent", "tag", "commit", "is_current", "is_ui", "stories",
                        "prune", "data_bytes"}
    assert payload["default"] == mt.WS
    assert srv.default_agent() == "mac-local"
    ui_copy = os.path.join(a, "releases", "v2026.10.03", "qwen-agent-workspace")
    assert srv.default_workspace(ui_copy) == os.path.join(a, "current", "qwen-agent-workspace")
    assert srv.default_workspace("/somewhere/else") == "/somewhere/else"
    os.unlink(os.path.join(home, ".local", "bin", "qwen-agent"))
    assert srv.default_agent() is None
    typed = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/options?ws=" + os.path.join(env["tmp"]))
    assert typed[:3:2] == (400, {"error": "not a harness workspace (no bin/ltx-movie): %s" % env["tmp"]})
```

```python
def _start_ui(ui, home, port):
    env = dict(os.environ, HOME=home, PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1")
    env.pop("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", None)
    return subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, os.path.join(ui, "bin", "movie-ui"), "--port", str(port)],
                            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env=env, text=True)


def test_mut15_bin_movie_ui_lock_port_and_startup_lines(tmp_path):
    home = os.path.join(str(tmp_path), "home")
    os.makedirs(home)
    ui = mt.copy_ui(tmp_path)
    port = mt.free_port()
    first = _start_ui(ui, home, port)
    try:
        line1 = first.stdout.readline()
        line2 = first.stdout.readline()
        state = os.path.join(home, ".local", "share", "qwen-agent", "movie-ui")
        m = re.fullmatch(r"movie-ui: http://127\.0\.0\.1:%d/\?t=([A-Za-z0-9_-]{43})\n" % port, line1)
        assert m, line1
        assert line2 == "movie-ui: state %s; Ctrl-C stops the server (running jobs keep running)\n" % state
        token = m.group(1)
        for name in ("jobs", "uploads", "traces", "smoke"):
            assert stat.S_IMODE(os.stat(os.path.join(state, name)).st_mode) == 0o700
        conn = http.client.HTTPConnection("127.0.0.1", port, timeout=10)
        conn.request("GET", "/api/host", headers={"X-Movie-UI-Token": token})
        resp = conn.getresponse()
        body = json.loads(resp.read())
        conn.close()
        assert resp.status == 200 and body["port"] == port and body["ui_workspace"] == os.path.realpath(ui)
        second = _start_ui(ui, home, mt.free_port())
        out, err = second.communicate(timeout=30)
        assert second.returncode == 2 and out == ""
        assert err == ("movie-ui: another movie-ui is already running on this host (lock held: %s)\n"
                       % os.path.join(state, "server.lock"))
    finally:
        first.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)
        out, err = first.communicate(timeout=30)
    assert first.returncode == 0 and out == "movie-ui: stopped\n" and err == ""
    held = socket.socket()
    held.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
    held.listen(1)
    busy_port = held.getsockname()[1]
    try:
        busy = _start_ui(ui, home, busy_port)
        out, err = busy.communicate(timeout=30)
    finally:
        held.close()
    assert (busy.returncode, err) == (2, "movie-ui: port %d on 127.0.0.1 is in use; pass --port N\n" % busy_port)
    for bad in ("80", "65536", "x"):
        low = _start_ui(ui, home, bad)
        out, err = low.communicate(timeout=30)
        assert (low.returncode, err) == (2, "movie-ui: invalid --port %s (1024 to 65535)\n" % bad)
```

**3.1.** **Append** to `movie_ui_server.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Workspaces, versions, host and options (MU33-MU35, MU45-MU47, MU60)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def default_agent():
    """MU35: the default command's wrapper agent when it is on disk, else the only agent, else None."""
    ids = fleet_lib.agent_ids_on_disk()
    kind, found = fleet_lib.classify_command_path(fleet_lib.default_command_path())
    if kind == "wrapper" and found in ids:
        return found
    return ids[0] if len(ids) == 1 else None


def _has_ltx_movie(ws_path):
    return os.path.isfile(os.path.join(ws_path, "bin", "ltx-movie"))


def default_workspace(ui_ws):
    """MU34: an installed UI defaults to its agent's current workspace, else the UI workspace."""
    ui = os.path.realpath(ui_ws)
    root = os.path.realpath(os.path.expanduser("~/agents"))
    parts = os.path.relpath(ui, root).split(os.sep)
    if len(parts) == 4 and parts[0] not in ("", "..") and parts[1] == "releases" and parts[3] == fleet_lib.WS_SUBDIR:
        try:
            current = os.path.join(fleet_lib.deploy_root(default_agent() or parts[0]), "current",
                                   fleet_lib.WS_SUBDIR)
        except ValueError:
            return ui
        if _has_ltx_movie(current):
            return current
    return ui


def _git_commit(path):
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", path, "rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD"], stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                              capture_output=True, timeout=5)
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return None
    text = proc.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip()
    return text if proc.returncode == 0 and text else None


def _story_count(real):
    try:
        names = os.listdir(os.path.join(real, "generated", "stories"))
    except OSError:
        return 0
    return sum(1 for n in names if lib.valid_story_id(n) and n != lib.SMOKE_STORY_ID
               and os.path.isdir(os.path.join(real, "generated", "stories", n)))


def list_workspaces(ui_ws):
    """MU33: installed current and release workspaces of every agent, then the UI's own."""
    entries, by_real = [], {}

    def add(entry):
        real = entry["realpath"]
        if real in by_real:
            by_real[real]["is_current"] = by_real[real]["is_current"] or entry["is_current"]
            by_real[real]["is_ui"] = by_real[real]["is_ui"] or entry["is_ui"]
            return
        by_real[real] = entry
        entries.append(entry)

    for agent in fleet_lib.agent_ids_on_disk():
        a = fleet_lib.deploy_root(agent)
        releases = os.path.join(a, "releases")
        current_real = None
        cur_ws = os.path.join(a, "current", fleet_lib.WS_SUBDIR)
        cur_tag = os.path.basename(os.path.realpath(os.path.join(a, "current")))
        if _has_ltx_movie(cur_ws):
            current_real = os.path.realpath(cur_ws)
            tag = cur_tag if fleet_lib.tag_key(cur_tag) is not None else None
            add({"path": cur_ws, "realpath": current_real, "agent": agent, "tag": tag, "commit": None,
                 "is_current": True, "is_ui": False, "kind": "current"})
        try:
            present = os.listdir(releases)
        except OSError:
            present = []
        state = fleet_lib.read_state(os.path.join(a, "update-state.json"))
        doomed = set(fleet_lib.releases_to_prune(present, cur_tag, state))
        for name in sorted((n for n in present if fleet_lib.tag_key(n) is not None), key=fleet_lib.tag_key,
                           reverse=True):
            rel_ws = os.path.join(releases, name, fleet_lib.WS_SUBDIR)
            if not _has_ltx_movie(rel_ws):
                continue
            real = os.path.realpath(rel_ws)
            if name in doomed:
                prune = ("kept_for_data" if fleet_lib.release_holds_data(os.path.join(releases, name))
                         else "would_prune")
            else:
                prune = "kept"
            add({"path": rel_ws, "realpath": real, "agent": agent, "tag": name, "commit": None,
                 "is_current": real == current_real, "is_ui": False, "kind": "release", "prune": prune,
                 "data_bytes": lib._tree_bytes(os.path.join(real, "generated"))})
    ui = os.path.realpath(ui_ws)
    if ui in by_real:
        by_real[ui]["is_ui"] = True
    else:
        add({"path": ui, "realpath": ui, "agent": None, "tag": None, "commit": _git_commit(ui),
             "is_current": False, "is_ui": True, "kind": "ui"})
    out = []
    for e in entries:
        kind = e.pop("kind")
        if kind == "current":
            label = "%s current (%s)" % (e["agent"], e["tag"] or "unknown tag")
        elif kind == "release":
            label = "%s %s" % (e["agent"], e["tag"])
        else:
            label = "this checkout (%s)" % (e["commit"] or "unknown commit")
        e["label"] = label
        e.setdefault("prune", None)
        e.setdefault("data_bytes", None)
        e["stories"] = _story_count(e["realpath"])
        out.append(dict((k, e[k]) for k in ("path", "realpath", "label", "agent", "tag", "commit",
                                            "is_current", "is_ui", "stories", "prune", "data_bytes")))
    return out


def _running_job(state):
    for job_id in lib.active_job_ids(state):
        return job_id
    return None


def _h_host(self, groups, query, body):
    if query.get("refresh") == "1":
        self.server.refresh_capabilities()
    state = self.server.state
    return 200, {"host": socket.gethostname(), "platform": sys.platform, "python": platform.python_version(),
                 "port": self.server.port, "state_dir": state, "ui_workspace": self.server.ui_ws,
                 "default_workspace": default_workspace(self.server.ui_ws), "default_agent": default_agent(),
                 "api_key_present": bool(os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")),
                 "capabilities": self.server.capability_snapshot(),
                 "running_job": _running_job(state), "queue_length": len(lib.read_queue(state))}


def _h_workspaces(self, groups, query, body):
    return 200, {"default": default_workspace(self.server.ui_ws), "workspaces": list_workspaces(self.server.ui_ws)}


def _h_options(self, groups, query, body):
    real = self.ws(query.get("ws"))
    flags, error = lib.help_flags(real)
    return 200, {"workspace": real, "help_ok": flags is not None, "help_error": error,
                 "flags": sorted(flags or ()), "supported": lib.supported_map(flags),
                 "defaults": dict(lib.OPTION_DEFAULTS)}


MovieUIHandler.h_host = _h_host
MovieUIHandler.h_workspaces = _h_workspaces
MovieUIHandler.h_options = _h_options


def _dispatcher_loop(server, stop):
    """MU30: one dispatch tick per second under STORE_LOCK; errors are logged, never fatal."""
    while not stop.wait(1.0):
        try:
            with STORE_LOCK:
                lib.dispatch_tick(server.state, server.ui_ws)
        except Exception as e:
            sys.stderr.write("movie-ui: dispatcher: %s: %s\n" % (type(e).__name__, e))


def _probe_once(server):
    try:
        server.refresh_capabilities()
    except Exception as e:
        sys.stderr.write("movie-ui: capability probe: %s: %s\n" % (type(e).__name__, e))


def start_threads(server):
    """Start the dispatcher (MU30) and capability-probe (MU60) threads, both daemon; returns the
    Event that stops the dispatcher."""
    stop = threading.Event()
    threading.Thread(target=_dispatcher_loop, args=(server, stop), name="dispatcher", daemon=True).start()
    threading.Thread(target=_probe_once, args=(server,), name="probe", daemon=True).start()
    return stop


_LOCK_FD = []


def _raise_interrupt(signum, frame):
    raise KeyboardInterrupt


def main(argv):
    """bin/movie-ui (MU1-MU7). Returns the exit code."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="movie-ui", description="The locally hosted movie page.")
    parser.add_argument("--port", default="8999", help="TCP port on 127.0.0.1 (default 8999)")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if not (re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]{1,5}", args.port) and 1024 <= int(args.port) <= 65535):
        sys.stderr.write("movie-ui: invalid --port %s (1024 to 65535)\n" % args.port)
        return 2
    port = int(args.port)
    state = lib.state_dir()
    lib.ensure_state(state)
    lock_path = os.path.join(state, "server.lock")
    fd = os.open(lock_path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o600)
    try:
        fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    except OSError:
        os.close(fd)
        sys.stderr.write("movie-ui: another movie-ui is already running on this host (lock held: %s)\n"
                         % lock_path)
        return 2
    _LOCK_FD.append(fd)
    token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    try:
        server = make_server(port, state, UI_WS, token)
    except OSError as e:
        if e.errno == errno.EADDRINUSE:
            sys.stderr.write("movie-ui: port %d on 127.0.0.1 is in use; pass --port N\n" % port)
        else:
            sys.stderr.write("movie-ui: cannot listen on 127.0.0.1:%d: %s\n" % (port, e.strerror or e))
        return 2
    with STORE_LOCK:
        lib.startup_recovery(state)
    stop = start_threads(server)
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, _raise_interrupt)
    sys.stdout.write("movie-ui: http://127.0.0.1:%d/?t=%s\n" % (server.port, token))
    sys.stdout.write("movie-ui: state %s; Ctrl-C stops the server (running jobs keep running)\n" % state)
    sys.stdout.flush()
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass
    stop.set()
    server.server_close()
    sys.stdout.write("movie-ui: stopped\n")
    sys.stdout.flush()
    return 0
```

**3.2.** **Create** `bin/movie-ui` with exactly this content:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""movie-ui -- the locally hosted movie page (movie-ui spec, piece F).

Usage: bin/movie-ui [--port N]

Serves http://127.0.0.1:<port>/ (default 8999) with a per-launch token, runs heavy jobs one at a
time as detached runners whose state lives in ~/.local/share/qwen-agent/movie-ui, and stops on
Ctrl-C without touching running jobs. See docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-movie-ui-design.md.
"""

import os
import sys

WS = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, WS)
import movie_ui_server  # noqa: E402

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(movie_ui_server.main(sys.argv[1:]))
```

### Task 12: Compose and jobs endpoints: preview, validate, submit, reads, logs and actions

Implements MU44, MU58, MU66, MU67 (summaries, the jobs list, the job detail) and MU68 over HTTP. Tests: MUT2, MUT13 and MUT14's HTTP halves, MUT17, Review Focus 4 and 5.

**Files:**
- Modify: `tests/test_movie_ui_http.py`
- Modify: `movie_ui_server.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 10's `STORE_LOCK`, `ApiError`, `resolve_ws`, `MovieUIServer.job`/`.progress`; Tasks 4-9's `compose_fields`, `validate_fields`, `build_argv`, `run_checks`, `checks_pass`, `new_job_id`, `create_job`, `job_state`, `cancel_job`, `move_job`, `delete_job`, `orphan_info`, `kill_orphan`, `list_job_ids`, `read_queue`, `ACTIVE_STATES`, `FINISHED_STATES`, `JOB_ID_RE`.
- Produces: `COMPOSE_KINDS`; `job_summary(server, job_id, queue) -> dict or None` (MU67's 14 keys; `progress` only for starting/running jobs); `_preview(server, fields) -> (argv or None, errors)`; `create_compose_job(server, fields) -> job_id`; `utf8_cut(data) -> bytes`; handlers `h_preview`, `h_validate`, `h_job_create`, `h_jobs`, `h_job`, `h_job_log`, `h_job_cancel`, `h_queue_move`, `h_job_delete`, `h_job_kill_orphan`. `GET /api/jobs/<id>` returns `progress` for every job that has a `status.json` (Plan decision 6).

```python
def _compose(env, **overrides):
    return mt.fields(env["ws"], **overrides)


def test_mut2_preview_argv_is_the_job_argv(env):
    s = env["srv"]
    free = mt.fake_safetensors(os.path.join(env["tmp"], "free.safetensors"))
    sets = [_compose(env),
            _compose(env, action="regenerate", goal="Keep it tense."),
            _compose(env, panels=None, length=30, mode="shots", story_model="qwen38",
                     stills_loras=[{"id": None, "path": free, "strength": 0.7, "kind": None}])]
    for body in sets:
        status, _h, preview = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/preview", body=body)
        assert status == 200 and preview["errors"] == []
        assert preview["command"] == shlex.join(preview["argv"])
        status, _h, created = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/jobs", body=body)
        assert status == 201, created
        assert all(c["status"] != "fail" for c in created["checks"])
        job = lib.read_job(lib.job_dir(env["state"], created["job"]["id"]))
        assert job["argv"] == preview["argv"]
        assert job["fields"]["ws"] == env["ws"] and job["workspace"] == env["ws"]
        assert job["kind"] == "story" and job["story_id"] == "ronin-rescue" and job["character"] is None
        assert created["job"]["state"] == "queued" and created["job"]["progress"] is None
    status, _h, bad = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/preview", body=_compose(env, panels=0))
    assert (status, bad["argv"], bad["command"]) == (200, None, None)
    assert bad["errors"] == ["panels must be an integer from 1 to 200"]
    status, _h, refused = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/jobs", body=_compose(env, panels=0))
    assert status == 409 and refused["error"] == "validation failed" and refused["checks"][0]["status"] == "fail"
    status, _h, valid = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/validate", body=_compose(env))
    assert status == 200 and valid["ok"] is True and valid["argv"] == mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/preview",
                                                                             body=_compose(env))[2]["argv"]
    jobs = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs")[2]
    assert jobs["running"] is None and jobs["history"] == []
    assert [j["queue_position"] for j in jobs["queued"]] == [1, 2, 3]
    assert set(jobs["queued"][0]) == {"id", "kind", "state", "workspace", "story_id", "character", "created",
                                      "started", "ended", "exit_code", "error", "queue_position", "command",
                                      "progress"}


def test_mut13_mut14_job_actions_over_http(env):
    s, state, ws = env["srv"], env["state"], env["ws"]
    a, b = mt.make_job(state, ["true"], ws), mt.make_job(state, ["true"], ws)
    assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/queue/move", body={"id": b, "direction": "up"})[:3:2] == (200, {"queued": [b, a]})
    assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/queue/move", body={"id": b, "direction": "sideways"})[0] == 400
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/jobs/%s/cancel" % a, body={})
    assert (status, payload) == (200, {"state": "cancelled"})
    assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/jobs/%s/cancel" % a, body={})[:3:2] == (
        409, {"error": "job %s is not queued or running" % a})
    assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/queue/move", body={"id": a, "direction": "up"})[:3:2] == (
        409, {"error": "job %s is not queued" % a})
    assert mt.api(s, "DELETE", "/api/jobs/%s" % b)[:3:2] == (409, {"error": "job %s is not finished" % b})
    assert mt.api(s, "DELETE", "/api/jobs/%s" % a)[:3:2] == (200, {"deleted": a})
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs/%s" % a)[0] == 404
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs/20261019-000000-0000")[0] == 404
    detail = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs/%s" % b)[2]
    assert detail["status"] == {"state": "queued"} and detail["progress"] is None and detail["orphan"] is None
    assert detail["window"] == {"start": None, "end": None} and detail["job"]["id"] == b
    running = mt.make_job(state, ["true"], ws, status={"state": "running", "dispatched": lib.utc_now(),
                                                         "runner_pid": 1, "pgid": None, "started": lib.utc_now()})
    assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/jobs/%s/cancel" % running, body={})[:3:2] == (202, {"state": "cancelling"})
    assert os.path.exists(os.path.join(lib.job_dir(state, running), "cancel"))
    orphan = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-c", "import time; time.sleep(60)"], start_new_session=True)
    try:
        lost = mt.make_job(state, ["true"], ws, status={
            "state": "lost", "dispatched": lib.utc_now(), "runner_pid": 1, "pgid": orphan.pid,
            "started": lib.utc_now(), "exit_code": None, "ended": None, "detected": lib.utc_now()})
        assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs/%s" % lost)[2]["orphan"] == {"pgid": orphan.pid, "alive": True}
        assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/jobs/%s/kill-orphan" % lost, body={})[:3:2] == (
            400, {"error": "confirm must be true"})
        assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/jobs/%s/kill-orphan" % lost, body={"confirm": "yes"})[0] == 400
        assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/jobs/%s/kill-orphan" % lost, body={"confirm": True})[:3:2] == (
            200, {"killed": orphan.pid})
        orphan.wait(5)
        assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/jobs/%s/kill-orphan" % lost, body={"confirm": True})[:3:2] == (
            409, {"error": "job %s has no live orphan process group" % lost})
    finally:
        if orphan.poll() is None:
            orphan.kill()
    jobs = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs")[2]
    assert jobs["running"]["id"] == running and jobs["running"]["progress"]["phase"] is None
    assert [j["id"] for j in jobs["queued"]] == [b]
    assert [j["id"] for j in jobs["history"]] == [lost]


def test_mut17_log_offsets_chain_on_character_boundaries(env):
    s, state = env["srv"], env["state"]
    job_id = mt.make_job(state, ["true"], env["ws"])
    data = b"a" * 262143 + "€".encode("utf-8") + b"b" * (300 * 1024 - 262146)
    with open(os.path.join(lib.job_dir(state, job_id), "log.txt"), "wb") as f:
        f.write(data)
    first = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs/%s/log" % job_id)[2]
    assert (first["offset"], first["next_offset"], first["size"], first["eof"]) == (0, 262143, len(data), False)
    assert first["text"] == "a" * 262143
    text, offset, rounds = first["text"], first["next_offset"], 1
    while True:
        chunk = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs/%s/log?offset=%d" % (job_id, offset))[2]
        text += chunk["text"]
        offset = chunk["next_offset"]
        rounds += 1
        if chunk["eof"]:
            break
    assert text == data.decode("utf-8") and offset == len(data) and rounds == 2
    tail = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs/%s/log?offset=%d" % (job_id, len(data) + 50))[2]
    assert (tail["text"], tail["next_offset"], tail["eof"]) == ("", len(data), True)
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs/%s/log?offset=-1" % job_id)[:3:2] == (400, {"error": "invalid offset"})
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs/%s/log?offset=1234567890123456" % job_id)[0] == 400
    assert srv.utf8_cut("a€".encode()[:-1]) == b"a" and srv.utf8_cut("a€".encode()) == "a€".encode()
    assert srv.utf8_cut("😀".encode()[:3]) == b"" and srv.utf8_cut("😀".encode()) == "😀".encode()


def test_review_focus_4_two_tabs_submit_at_once(env):
    """Review Focus 4: two browser tabs (or a double click) submit jobs at the same moment."""
    s = env["srv"]
    results = []

    def submit(i):
        results.append(mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/jobs", body=_compose(env, story_id="story-%d" % i)))
    threads = [threading.Thread(target=submit, args=(i,)) for i in range(8)]
    for t in threads:
        t.start()
    for t in threads:
        t.join(60)
    assert sorted(r[0] for r in results) == [201] * 8
    ids = [r[2]["job"]["id"] for r in results]
    assert len(set(ids)) == 8
    assert sorted(lib.read_queue(env["state"])) == sorted(ids)
    assert sorted(lib.list_job_ids(env["state"])) == sorted(ids)


def test_review_focus_5_undecodable_bytes_in_a_running_log(env):
    """Review Focus 5: ffmpeg or a model prints bytes that are not UTF-8 while the page polls."""
    s, state = env["srv"], env["state"]
    job_id = mt.make_job(state, ["true"], env["ws"], status={"state": "running", "dispatched": lib.utc_now(),
                                                               "runner_pid": 1, "pgid": None, "started": lib.utc_now()})
    log = os.path.join(lib.job_dir(state, job_id), "log.txt")
    with open(log, "wb") as f:
        f.write(b"\xff\xfe\x00junk\n=== Phase 4: render ===\n=== panel 1/2 (a) ===\npanel 1 ok: caf\xc3")
    progress = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs/%s" % job_id)[2]["progress"]
    assert progress["phase"] == "4" and progress["panels"] == {"1": {"state": "running"}}
    assert progress["last_line"] == "panel 1 ok: caf�"
    with open(log, "ab") as f:
        f.write(b"\xa9.mp4 (1.0s)\n")
    progress = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs")[2]["running"]["progress"]
    assert progress["panels"] == {"1": {"state": "ok", "reused": False}}
    assert progress["last_line"] == "panel 1 ok: café.mp4 (1.0s)"
    chunk = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/jobs/%s/log" % job_id)[2]
    assert chunk["text"].startswith("��\x00junk\n") and chunk["eof"] is True
```

**3.1.** **Append** to `movie_ui_server.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Compose and jobs (MU44, MU58, MU66-MU68)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

COMPOSE_KINDS = {"story": "story", "regenerate": "story", "movie": "movie", "redo": "movie"}


def job_summary(server, job_id, queue):
    """MU67's summary object for one job, or None when job.json is unreadable."""
    job = server.job(job_id)
    if job is None:
        return None
    status = lib.read_status(lib.job_dir(server.state, job_id)) or {}
    state = lib.job_state(server.state, job_id, queue)
    return {"id": job_id, "kind": job["kind"], "state": state, "workspace": job["workspace"],
            "story_id": job["story_id"], "character": job["character"], "created": job["created"],
            "started": status.get("started"), "ended": status.get("ended"), "exit_code": status.get("exit_code"),
            "error": status.get("error"), "queue_position": queue.index(job_id) + 1 if state == "queued" else None,
            "command": shlex.join(job["argv"]),
            "progress": server.progress(job_id, job, status) if state in lib.ACTIVE_STATES else None}


def _fields_body(body):
    fields = lib.compose_fields(body)
    fields["ws"] = resolve_ws(body.get("ws"))
    return fields


def _preview(server, fields):
    errors = lib.validate_fields(fields, fields["ws"], server.state)
    if errors:
        return None, errors
    return lib.build_argv(fields, fields["ws"], server.state), errors


def _h_preview(self, groups, query, body):
    fields = _fields_body(body)
    argv, errors = _preview(self.server, fields)
    return 200, {"argv": argv, "command": shlex.join(argv) if argv else None, "errors": errors}


def _h_validate(self, groups, query, body):
    fields = _fields_body(body)
    checks = lib.run_checks(fields, fields["ws"], self.server.state)
    argv, _errors = _preview(self.server, fields)
    return 200, {"ok": lib.checks_pass(checks), "checks": checks, "argv": argv,
                 "command": shlex.join(argv) if argv else None}


def create_compose_job(server, fields):
    """Create the queued job of a validated compose submission; returns its ID."""
    state, real = server.state, fields["ws"]
    with STORE_LOCK:
        job_id = lib.new_job_id(state)
        argv = lib.build_argv(fields, real, state)
        return lib.create_job(state, job_id, COMPOSE_KINDS[fields["action"]], real, fields["story_id"], None,
                              argv, fields)["id"]


def _h_job_create(self, groups, query, body):
    fields = _fields_body(body)
    checks = lib.run_checks(fields, fields["ws"], self.server.state)
    if not lib.checks_pass(checks):
        raise ApiError(409, "validation failed", checks=checks)
    job_id = create_compose_job(self.server, fields)
    return 201, {"job": job_summary(self.server, job_id, lib.read_queue(self.server.state)), "checks": checks}


def _h_jobs(self, groups, query, body):
    server = self.server
    queue = lib.read_queue(server.state)
    running, history = None, []
    for job_id in sorted(lib.list_job_ids(server.state), reverse=True):
        summary = job_summary(server, job_id, queue)
        if summary is None:
            continue
        if summary["state"] in lib.ACTIVE_STATES and running is None:
            running = summary
        elif summary["state"] in lib.FINISHED_STATES and len(history) < HISTORY_MAX:
            history.append(summary)
    queued = [s for s in (job_summary(server, i, queue) for i in queue) if s is not None]
    return 200, {"running": running, "queued": queued, "history": history}


def _known_job(server, job_id):
    job = server.job(job_id)
    if job is None:
        raise ApiError(404, "unknown job %s" % job_id)
    return job


def _h_job(self, groups, query, body):
    server, job_id = self.server, groups[0]
    job = _known_job(server, job_id)
    status = lib.read_status(lib.job_dir(server.state, job_id))
    if status is None:
        shown = {"state": lib.job_state(server.state, job_id, lib.read_queue(server.state))}
    else:
        shown = status
    return 200, {"job": job, "status": shown,
                 "progress": server.progress(job_id, job, status) if status is not None else None,
                 "orphan": lib.orphan_info(status),
                 "window": {"start": (status or {}).get("started"), "end": (status or {}).get("ended")}}


def utf8_cut(data):
    """data without a trailing incomplete UTF-8 sequence (at most 3 bytes are dropped; MU58)."""
    for back in range(1, min(3, len(data)) + 1):
        byte = data[-back]
        if byte & 0xC0 == 0x80:
            continue
        need = 2 if byte & 0xE0 == 0xC0 else 3 if byte & 0xF0 == 0xE0 else 4 if byte & 0xF8 == 0xF0 else 1
        return data[:-back] if need > back else data
    return data


def _h_job_log(self, groups, query, body):
    server, job_id = self.server, groups[0]
    offset = query.get("offset", "0")
    if not OFFSET_RE.fullmatch(offset):
        raise ApiError(400, "invalid offset")
    _known_job(server, job_id)
    n = int(offset)
    path = os.path.join(lib.job_dir(server.state, job_id), "log.txt")
    try:
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            size = os.fstat(f.fileno()).st_size
            if n >= size:
                data = b""
            else:
                f.seek(n)
                data = utf8_cut(f.read(LOG_CHUNK))
    except OSError:
        raise ApiError(404, "unknown job %s" % job_id)
    nxt = size if n >= size else n + len(data)
    return 200, {"offset": n, "next_offset": nxt, "size": size, "eof": nxt >= size,
                 "text": data.decode("utf-8", "replace")}


def _h_job_cancel(self, groups, query, body):
    job_id = groups[0]
    _known_job(self.server, job_id)
    with STORE_LOCK:
        result = lib.cancel_job(self.server.state, job_id)
    if result == "cancelled":
        return 200, {"state": "cancelled"}
    if result == "cancelling":
        return 202, {"state": "cancelling"}
    raise ApiError(409, "job %s is not queued or running" % job_id)


def _h_queue_move(self, groups, query, body):
    job_id, direction = body.get("id"), body.get("direction")
    if direction not in ("up", "down"):
        raise ApiError(400, "direction must be up or down")
    if not (isinstance(job_id, str) and lib.JOB_ID_RE.fullmatch(job_id)):
        raise ApiError(409, "job %s is not queued" % (job_id,))
    with STORE_LOCK:
        queue = lib.move_job(self.server.state, job_id, direction)
    if queue is None:
        raise ApiError(409, "job %s is not queued" % job_id)
    return 200, {"queued": queue}


def _h_job_delete(self, groups, query, body):
    job_id = groups[0]
    _known_job(self.server, job_id)
    with STORE_LOCK:
        deleted = lib.delete_job(self.server.state, job_id)
    if not deleted:
        raise ApiError(409, "job %s is not finished" % job_id)
    self.server.job_cache.pop(job_id, None)
    with self.server.parser_lock:
        self.server.parsers.pop(job_id, None)
    return 200, {"deleted": job_id}


def _h_job_kill_orphan(self, groups, query, body):
    job_id = groups[0]
    if body.get("confirm") is not True:
        raise ApiError(400, "confirm must be true")
    _known_job(self.server, job_id)
    with STORE_LOCK:
        pgid = lib.kill_orphan(self.server.state, job_id)
    if pgid is None:
        raise ApiError(409, "job %s has no live orphan process group" % job_id)
    return 200, {"killed": pgid}


MovieUIHandler.h_preview = _h_preview
MovieUIHandler.h_validate = _h_validate
MovieUIHandler.h_job_create = _h_job_create
MovieUIHandler.h_jobs = _h_jobs
MovieUIHandler.h_job = _h_job
MovieUIHandler.h_job_log = _h_job_log
MovieUIHandler.h_job_cancel = _h_job_cancel
MovieUIHandler.h_queue_move = _h_queue_move
MovieUIHandler.h_job_delete = _h_job_delete
MovieUIHandler.h_job_kill_orphan = _h_job_kill_orphan
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 10's body reader (it returns the raw bytes for `POST /api/uploads` after the type and size checks), Task 12's `job_summary`; Tasks 2-9's `write_bytes_atomic`, `run_light`, `tool_argv`, `LIGHT_TIMEOUT`, `list_models`, `ProgressParser`, `new_job_id`, `create_job`, `character_argv`, `list_job_ids`, `UPLOAD_ID_RE`, `CHARACTER_NAME_RE`.

```python
JPEG = b"\xff\xd8\xff\xe0" + b"\x00" * 32
```

**1.2.** **Append** to `tests/test_movie_ui_http.py`:

```python
def _upload(s, data, media):
    return mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/uploads", raw=data, headers={"Content-Type": media})


def test_mut21_uploads(env):
    s, state = env["srv"], env["state"]
    for data, media, ext in ((mt.PNG, "image/png", "png"), (mt.JPEG, "image/jpeg", "jpg"), (mt.WEBP, "image/webp", "webp")):
        status, _h, payload = _upload(s, data, media)
        upload_id = "%s.%s" % (hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), ext)
        assert (status, payload) == (201, {"upload_id": upload_id, "path": os.path.join(state, "uploads", upload_id),
                                           "bytes": len(data)})
        with open(payload["path"], "rb") as f:
            assert f.read() == data
        assert _upload(s, data, media)[2]["upload_id"] == upload_id
    assert _upload(s, mt.JPEG, "image/png")[:3:2] == (400, {"error": "file content is not image/png"})
    assert _upload(s, mt.PNG, "image/webp")[:3:2] == (400, {"error": "file content is not image/webp"})
    assert _upload(s, mt.PNG, "image/gif")[:3:2] == (415, {"error": "unsupported content type"})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/uploads", raw=b"",
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "image/png", "Content-Length": str(20971521)})
    assert (status, payload) == (413, {"error": "upload larger than 20 MiB"})
    assert sorted(os.listdir(os.path.join(state, "uploads"))) == sorted(
        "%s.%s" % (hashlib.sha256(d).hexdigest(), e) for d, e in ((mt.PNG, "png"), (mt.JPEG, "jpg"), (mt.WEBP, "webp")))


def test_mu61_mu62_lora_endpoints(env):
    s, ws = env["srv"], env["ws"]
    entry = {"id": "pan", "kind": "motion", "target": "video", "path": "/l/pan.safetensors"}
    mt.stub_config(ws, {
        "lora-catalog list --json --model M": {"stdout": json.dumps({"catalog": "/c", "entries": [entry],
                                                                     "characters": []})},
        "lora-catalog add": {"exit": 2, "stderr": "Error: catalog entry pan already exists\n"},
        "lora-catalog verify": {"exit": 1, "stdout": "OK ink\nFAIL pan: sha256 changed\nFAIL pan: missing file\n"
                                                    "verified 2 entries: 2 problem(s)\n"}})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/loras?ws=%s&model=M" % ws)
    assert (status, payload["entries"], payload["model"]) == (200, [entry], "M")
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/loras?ws=%s" % ws)
    assert status == 200 and payload["entries"] == [] and payload["model"] is None
    mt.stub_config(ws, {"lora-catalog list": {"exit": 2, "stderr": "Error: invalid catalog\n"}})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/loras?ws=%s" % ws)
    assert status == 502 and payload["error"] == "lora-catalog exited 2"
    assert payload["run"]["stderr"] == "Error: invalid catalog\n"
    mt.stub_config(ws, {"lora-catalog add": {"exit": 2, "stderr": "Error: catalog entry pan already exists\n"},
                        "lora-catalog verify": {"exit": 1, "stdout": "OK ink\nFAIL pan: sha256 changed\n"
                                                                    "FAIL pan: missing file\n"}})
    add = {"ws": ws, "path": "/l/pan.safetensors", "id": "pan", "kind": "motion", "target": "video",
           "base_models": ["M1", "M2"], "triggers": ["panshot"], "description": "a pan", "use_when": "pans",
           "avoid_when": "", "default_strength": 0.8, "strength_range": [0.5, 1], "rank": 16, "alpha": None}
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/loras", body=add)
    assert status == 200 and payload["run"]["exit_code"] == 2
    assert mt.stub_calls(ws)[-1]["argv"] == [
        "add", "/l/pan.safetensors", "--id", "pan", "--kind", "motion", "--target", "video", "--base-model", "M1",
        "--base-model", "M2", "--trigger", "panshot", "--description", "a pan", "--use-when", "pans",
        "--default-strength", "0.8", "--strength-range", "0.5,1.0", "--rank", "16"]
    assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/loras", body=dict(add, triggers="panshot"))[:3:2] == (
        400, {"error": "triggers must be a list of strings"})
    assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/loras", body=dict(add, rank=1.5))[:3:2] == (
        400, {"error": "rank must be an integer or null"})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/loras/remove", body={"ws": ws, "id": "pan"})
    assert status == 200 and mt.stub_calls(ws)[-1]["argv"] == ["remove", "pan"]
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/loras/verify", body={"ws": ws, "ids": []})
    assert status == 200 and mt.stub_calls(ws)[-1]["argv"] == ["verify"]
    assert payload["results"] == [{"id": "ink", "ok": True, "problems": []},
                                  {"id": "pan", "ok": False, "problems": ["sha256 changed", "missing file"]}]


def test_mu63_models_endpoint(env, monkeypatch):
    for var, sub in (("LTX2_MLX_HF_HOME", "l"), ("Z_IMAGE_HF_HOME", "z"), ("HF_HOME", "h")):
        monkeypatch.setenv(var, os.path.join(env["tmp"], sub))
    mt.make_hf_cache(os.path.join(env["tmp"], "l"), "org/m", {"a.safetensors": b"\x01"})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(env["srv"], "GET", "/api/models?ws=" + env["ws"])
    assert status == 200 and [m["repo"] for m in payload["models"]] == ["org/m"]
    assert mt.api(env["srv"], "GET", "/api/models")[:3:2] == (400, {"error": "missing ws"})


def test_mu64_mu65_character_endpoints(env):
    s, ws, state = env["srv"], env["ws"], env["state"]
    sheet = os.path.join(env["tmp"], "lib", "kyra", "dataset", "contact_sheet.jpg")
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(sheet), "stills"))
    for name in ("char_02.png", "char_01.png", "other.png"):
        open(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(sheet), "stills", name), "wb").close()
    show = json.dumps({"name": "kyra", "status": "untrained", "dataset": {"contact_sheet": sheet},
                       "loras": {"video": None, "stills": None}}, indent=2)
    mt.stub_config(ws, {
        "character list": {"stdout": "NAME             TRIGGER          STATUS    VIDEO STILLS PHRASE\n"
                                     "kyra             kyrax            trained   yes   no     the young woman\n"
                                     "broken           (invalid: bad json)\n"},
        "character show kyra": {"stdout": show + "\nfiles:\n  ok       %s\n  MISSING  /lib/kyra/seed.png\n" % sheet}})
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/characters?ws=" + ws)[2] == {"characters": [
        {"name": "kyra", "trigger": "kyrax", "status": "trained", "video": True, "stills": False,
         "phrase": "the young woman", "invalid": None},
        {"name": "broken", "invalid": "bad json"}]}
    detail = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/characters/kyra?ws=" + ws)[2]
    assert detail["character"]["status"] == "untrained"
    assert detail["files"] == [{"status": "ok", "path": sheet}, {"status": "MISSING", "path": "/lib/kyra/seed.png"}]
    stills = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(sheet), "stills")
    assert detail["dataset"] == {"contact_sheet": sheet, "stills": [os.path.join(stills, "char_01.png"),
                                                                    os.path.join(stills, "char_02.png")]}
    assert detail["face_problem"] is False and detail["last_create_fields"] is None
    seed = _upload(s, mt.PNG, "image/png")[2]["upload_id"]
    create = {"ws": ws, "name": "kyra", "phrase": "the young woman", "class_noun": None, "descriptor": None,
              "trigger": None, "seed": None, "seed_upload": seed, "force": False}
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/characters", body=create)
    assert status == 201 and payload["job"]["kind"] == "character-create" and payload["job"]["character"] == "kyra"
    job = lib.read_job(lib.job_dir(state, payload["job"]["id"]))
    assert job["argv"][2:] == ["create", "kyra", "--phrase", "the young woman", "--seed-image",
                               os.path.join(state, "uploads", seed)]
    with open(os.path.join(lib.job_dir(state, job["id"]), "log.txt"), "wb") as f:
        f.write(b"Error: the vision model found no face in the seed image; pass --force to proceed anyway.\n")
    detail = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/characters/kyra?ws=" + ws)[2]
    assert detail["face_problem"] is True and detail["last_create_fields"]["seed_upload"] == seed
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/characters", body=dict(create, name="K", phrase="", seed_upload=None))
    assert (status, payload["error"]) == (400, "invalid fields")
    assert payload["errors"] == ["character name must match [a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,23}", "phrase is required",
                                 "give exactly one of a seed image or a descriptor"]
    assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/characters", body=dict(create, seed_upload=None, descriptor="tall"))[2]["errors"] == [
        "a descriptor needs a class noun"]
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/characters/kyra/train", body={"ws": ws})
    assert (status, payload) == (400, {"error": "invalid fields", "errors": ["choose video, stills or both"]})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/characters/kyra/train", body={"ws": ws, "video": True,
                                                                               "force": True})
    assert status == 201 and lib.read_job(lib.job_dir(state, payload["job"]["id"]))["argv"][2:] == [
        "train", "kyra", "--video", "--force"]
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/characters/kyra/regenerate", body={"ws": ws})
    assert status == 201 and lib.read_job(lib.job_dir(state, payload["job"]["id"]))["argv"][2:] == [
        "create", "kyra", "--regenerate"]
    assert payload["job"]["kind"] == "character-regenerate" and payload["job"]["queue_position"] == 3
    mt.stub_config(ws, {"character show kyra": {"exit": 2, "stderr": "Error: no character kyra\n"}})
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/characters/kyra?ws=" + ws)[:3:2][0] == 502
```
