- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

**3.1.** **Append** to `movie_ui_server.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Uploads, LoRAs, models and characters (MU53, MU59, MU61-MU65)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def tool_result(tool, run):
    """MU59: 504 on a timeout, 502 on a non-zero exit (ApiError), else run."""
    if run["timed_out"]:
        raise ApiError(504, "%s timed out after %d s" % (tool, lib.LIGHT_TIMEOUT), run=run)
    if run["exit_code"] != 0:
        raise ApiError(502, "%s exited %s" % (tool, run["exit_code"]), run=run)
    return run


def _h_upload(self, groups, query, body):
    media = (self.headers.get("Content-Type") or "").split(";")[0].strip().lower()
    ext, magic = UPLOAD_TYPES[media]
    ok = (body[:4] == b"RIFF" and body[8:12] == b"WEBP") if magic is None else body.startswith(magic)
    if not ok:
        raise ApiError(400, "file content is not %s" % media)
    upload_id = "%s.%s" % (hashlib.sha256(body).hexdigest(), ext)
    path = os.path.join(self.server.state, "uploads", upload_id)
    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        lib.write_bytes_atomic(path, body)
    return 201, {"upload_id": upload_id, "path": path, "bytes": len(body)}


def _h_loras(self, groups, query, body):
    real = self.ws(query.get("ws"))
    model = query.get("model")
    args = ["list", "--json"] + (["--model", model] if model else [])
    run = tool_result("lora-catalog", lib.run_light(lib.tool_argv(real, "lora-catalog", *args), real))
    try:
        obj = json.loads(run["stdout"])
        if not isinstance(obj, dict):
            raise ValueError
    except ValueError:
        raise ApiError(502, "lora-catalog printed unparsable output", run=run)
    obj["model"] = model or None
    return 200, obj


def _lora_action(real, args):
    run = lib.run_light(lib.tool_argv(real, "lora-catalog", *args), real)
    if run["timed_out"]:
        raise ApiError(504, "lora-catalog timed out after %d s" % lib.LIGHT_TIMEOUT, run=run)
    return run


def _typed(body, key, kind, name, optional=False):
    value = body.get(key)
    if value is None and optional:
        return None
    ok = {"str": isinstance(value, str),
          "str list": isinstance(value, list) and all(isinstance(v, str) for v in value),
          "number": isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool),
          "int": isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool),
          "pair": (isinstance(value, list) and len(value) == 2
                   and all(isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) for v in value))}[kind]
    if not ok:
        raise ApiError(400, "%s must be %s" % (key, name))
    return value


def _h_lora_add(self, groups, query, body):
    real = self.ws(body.get("ws"))
    path = _typed(body, "path", "str", "a string")
    entry_id = _typed(body, "id", "str", "a string")
    kind = _typed(body, "kind", "str", "a string")
    target = _typed(body, "target", "str", "a string")
    base_models = _typed(body, "base_models", "str list", "a list of strings")
    triggers = _typed(body, "triggers", "str list", "a list of strings")
    description = _typed(body, "description", "str", "a string")
    use_when = _typed(body, "use_when", "str", "a string")
    avoid_when = _typed(body, "avoid_when", "str", "a string", optional=True)
    strength = _typed(body, "default_strength", "number", "a number")
    lo, hi = _typed(body, "strength_range", "pair", "a list of two numbers")
    rank = _typed(body, "rank", "int", "an integer or null", optional=True)
    alpha = _typed(body, "alpha", "int", "an integer or null", optional=True)
    args = ["add", path, "--id", entry_id, "--kind", kind, "--target", target]
    for m in base_models:
        args += ["--base-model", m]
    for t in triggers:
        args += ["--trigger", t]
    args += ["--description", description, "--use-when", use_when]
    if avoid_when:
        args += ["--avoid-when", avoid_when]
    args += ["--default-strength", repr(float(strength)),
             "--strength-range", "%s,%s" % (repr(float(lo)), repr(float(hi)))]
    if rank is not None:
        args += ["--rank", str(rank)]
    if alpha is not None:
        args += ["--alpha", str(alpha)]
    return 200, {"run": _lora_action(real, args)}


def _h_lora_remove(self, groups, query, body):
    real = self.ws(body.get("ws"))
    return 200, {"run": _lora_action(real, ["remove", _typed(body, "id", "str", "a string")])}


VERIFY_LINE_RE = re.compile(r"^(OK|FAIL) (\S+?)(?:: (.*))?$")


def parse_verify(stdout):
    """[{"id", "ok", "problems"}] from lora-catalog verify's OK/FAIL lines, in first-seen order."""
    results, by_id = [], {}
    for line in stdout.splitlines():
        m = VERIFY_LINE_RE.match(line)
        if not m:
            continue
        entry = by_id.get(m.group(2))
        if entry is None:
            entry = by_id[m.group(2)] = {"id": m.group(2), "ok": True, "problems": []}
            results.append(entry)
        if m.group(1) == "FAIL":
            entry["ok"] = False
            entry["problems"].append(m.group(3) or "")
    return results


def _h_lora_verify(self, groups, query, body):
    real = self.ws(body.get("ws"))
    ids = body.get("ids") or []
    if not (isinstance(ids, list) and all(isinstance(i, str) for i in ids)):
        raise ApiError(400, "ids must be a list of strings")
    run = _lora_action(real, ["verify"] + ids)
    return 200, {"run": run, "results": parse_verify(run["stdout"])}


def _h_models(self, groups, query, body):
    self.ws(query.get("ws"))
    return 200, lib.list_models()


LIST_ROW_RE = re.compile(r"^(\S+)\s+(\S+)\s+(dataset|untrained|trained)\s+(yes|no)\s+(yes|no)\s+(.+)$")
INVALID_ROW_RE = re.compile(r"^(\S+)\s+\(invalid: (.*)\)$")
FILE_LINE_RE = re.compile(r"^  (ok|MISSING)\s+(.+)$")
STILL_FILE_RE = re.compile(r"^char_\d{2}\.png$")


def parse_character_list(stdout):
    """MU64: rows of bin/character list."""
    out = []
    for line in stdout.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("NAME ") or line.startswith("no characters in ") or not line.strip():
            continue
        m = INVALID_ROW_RE.match(line)
        if m:
            out.append({"name": m.group(1), "invalid": m.group(2)})
            continue
        m = LIST_ROW_RE.match(line)
        if m:
            out.append({"name": m.group(1), "trigger": m.group(2), "status": m.group(3),
                        "video": m.group(4) == "yes", "stills": m.group(5) == "yes", "phrase": m.group(6),
                        "invalid": None})
    return out


def _h_characters(self, groups, query, body):
    real = self.ws(query.get("ws"))
    run = tool_result("character", lib.run_light(lib.tool_argv(real, "character", "list"), real))
    return 200, {"characters": parse_character_list(run["stdout"])}


def newest_job(server, kinds, ws_real, key, value):
    """The newest job of one of kinds whose workspace is ws_real and job[key] == value, or None."""
    for job_id in sorted(lib.list_job_ids(server.state), reverse=True):
        job = server.job(job_id)
        if job is not None and job["kind"] in kinds and job["workspace"] == ws_real and job.get(key) == value:
            return job
    return None


def _h_character(self, groups, query, body):
    real, name = self.ws(query.get("ws")), groups[0]
    run = tool_result("character", lib.run_light(lib.tool_argv(real, "character", "show", name), real))
    try:
        data, end = json.JSONDecoder().raw_decode(run["stdout"].lstrip())
    except ValueError:
        raise ApiError(502, "character printed unparsable output", run=run)
    rest = run["stdout"].lstrip()[end:]
    files = [{"status": m.group(1), "path": m.group(2)}
             for m in (FILE_LINE_RE.match(line) for line in rest.splitlines()) if m]
    dataset = None
    sheet = (data.get("dataset") or {}).get("contact_sheet") if isinstance(data, dict) else None
    if isinstance(sheet, str):
        stills_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(sheet), "stills")
        try:
            stills = sorted(os.path.join(stills_dir, n) for n in os.listdir(stills_dir) if STILL_FILE_RE.fullmatch(n))
        except OSError:
            stills = []
        dataset = {"contact_sheet": sheet, "stills": stills}
    job = newest_job(self.server, ("character-create",), real, "character", name)
    face = False
    if job is not None:
        parser = lib.ProgressParser(job["kind"], job["fields"])
        try:
            with open(os.path.join(lib.job_dir(self.server.state, job["id"]), "log.txt"), "rb") as f:
                parser.feed(f.read() + b"\n")
        except OSError:
            pass
        face = parser.face_problem
    return 200, {"character": data, "files": files, "dataset": dataset, "face_problem": face,
                 "last_create_fields": job["fields"] if job is not None else None}


PHRASE_MAX = 200


def character_create_errors(body, state):
    """MU65's field rules for POST /api/characters; [] when they pass."""
    errors = []
    name, phrase = body.get("name"), body.get("phrase")
    seed, descriptor, class_noun = body.get("seed_upload"), body.get("descriptor"), body.get("class_noun")
    trigger, seed_n, force = body.get("trigger"), body.get("seed"), body.get("force", False)
    if not (isinstance(name, str) and lib.CHARACTER_NAME_RE.fullmatch(name)):
        errors.append("character name must match [a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,23}")
    if not (isinstance(phrase, str) and 1 <= len(phrase.strip()) and len(phrase) <= PHRASE_MAX
            and "\n" not in phrase and "\r" not in phrase):
        errors.append("phrase is required")
    if (seed is None) == (descriptor is None or descriptor == ""):
        errors.append("give exactly one of a seed image or a descriptor")
    if descriptor not in (None, "") and not class_noun:
        errors.append("a descriptor needs a class noun")
    if descriptor not in (None, "") and not isinstance(descriptor, str):
        errors.append("descriptor must be a string")
    if class_noun not in (None, "") and not (isinstance(class_noun, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[a-z]{3,12}", class_noun)):
        errors.append("class noun must match [a-z]{3,12}")
    if trigger not in (None, "") and not (isinstance(trigger, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[a-z][a-z0-9]{3,15}", trigger)):
        errors.append("trigger must match [a-z][a-z0-9]{3,15}")
    if seed_n is not None and not (isinstance(seed_n, int) and not isinstance(seed_n, bool) and seed_n >= 0):
        errors.append("seed must be an integer >= 0")
    if seed is not None and not (isinstance(seed, str) and lib.UPLOAD_ID_RE.fullmatch(seed)
                                 and os.path.isfile(os.path.join(state, "uploads", seed))):
        errors.append("seed image upload %s not found" % (seed,))
    if not isinstance(force, bool):
        errors.append("force must be true or false")
    return errors


def create_character_job(server, kind, real, fields):
    with STORE_LOCK:
        job_id = lib.new_job_id(server.state)
        argv = lib.character_argv(kind, fields, real, server.state)
        lib.create_job(server.state, job_id, kind, real, None, fields["name"], argv, fields)
    return 201, {"job": job_summary(server, job_id, lib.read_queue(server.state))}


def _h_character_create(self, groups, query, body):
    real = self.ws(body.get("ws"))
    errors = character_create_errors(body, self.server.state)
    if errors:
        raise ApiError(400, "invalid fields", errors=errors)
    fields = dict((k, body.get(k)) for k in ("name", "phrase", "class_noun", "descriptor", "trigger", "seed",
                                              "seed_upload"))
    for key in ("class_noun", "descriptor", "trigger"):
        if fields[key] == "":
            fields[key] = None
    fields["force"] = body.get("force", False)
    fields["ws"] = real
    return create_character_job(self.server, "character-create", real, fields)


def _h_character_regenerate(self, groups, query, body):
    real = self.ws(body.get("ws"))
    return create_character_job(self.server, "character-regenerate", real, {"ws": real, "name": groups[0]})


def _h_character_train(self, groups, query, body):
    real = self.ws(body.get("ws"))
    fields = {"ws": real, "name": groups[0]}
    errors = []
    for key in ("video", "stills", "force"):
        value = body.get(key, False)
        if not isinstance(value, bool):
            errors.append("%s must be true or false" % key)
        fields[key] = value
    if not errors and not (fields["video"] or fields["stills"]):
        errors.append("choose video, stills or both")
    if errors:
        raise ApiError(400, "invalid fields", errors=errors)
    return create_character_job(self.server, "character-train", real, fields)


MovieUIHandler.h_upload = _h_upload
MovieUIHandler.h_loras = _h_loras
MovieUIHandler.h_lora_add = _h_lora_add
MovieUIHandler.h_lora_remove = _h_lora_remove
MovieUIHandler.h_lora_verify = _h_lora_verify
MovieUIHandler.h_models = _h_models
MovieUIHandler.h_characters = _h_characters
MovieUIHandler.h_character = _h_character
MovieUIHandler.h_character_create = _h_character_create
MovieUIHandler.h_character_regenerate = _h_character_regenerate
MovieUIHandler.h_character_train = _h_character_train
```

```python
def _make_story(ws, story_id, text=None, files=()):
    sdir = os.path.join(ws, "generated", "stories", story_id)
    os.makedirs(sdir, exist_ok=True)
    if text is not None:
        with open(os.path.join(sdir, "story.md"), "wb") as f:
            f.write(text.encode("utf-8"))
    for rel, data in files:
        path = os.path.join(sdir, rel)
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
        with open(path, "wb") as f:
            f.write(data)
    return sdir


def test_mu69_story_list_and_detail(env):
    s, ws = env["srv"], env["ws"]
    old = _make_story(ws, "old-one", "## Panel 1\n")
    os.utime(old, (1000, 1000))
    checks = {"source": "robust", "hard": [{"id": "N1"}], "advisory": [], "verdict": "ok", "panels": 3}
    sdir = _make_story(ws, "ronin-rescue", "## Panel 1\n", files=[
        ("story_checks.json", json.dumps(checks).encode()), ("plan.json", json.dumps({"mode": "shots", "loras": {
            "global": []}}).encode()), ("images/panel_01.png", b"p"), ("images/panel_02.png", b"p"),
        ("images/seed.png", b"p"), ("clips/panel_01.mp4", b"m"), ("movie.mp4", b"m"),
        ("runs/20261019-a/story_summary.json", b'{"run": "a"}'), ("runs/20261019-b/story_summary.json", b'{"run": "b"}')])
    _make_story(ws, "movie-ui-smoke", "x")
    _make_story(ws, "a..b", "x")
    payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/stories?ws=" + ws)[2]
    assert payload["stories_dir"] == os.path.join(ws, "generated", "stories")
    assert [st["id"] for st in payload["stories"]] == ["ronin-rescue", "old-one"]
    first = payload["stories"][0]
    assert first == {"id": "ronin-rescue", "story_md": True,
                     "checks": {"source": "robust", "hard": 1, "advisory": 0, "verdict": "ok"},
                     "stills": 2, "clips": 1, "movie": True, "mtime": first["mtime"]}
    assert payload["stories"][1]["mtime"] == "1970-01-01T00:16:40Z" and payload["stories"][1]["checks"] is None
    detail = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/stories/ronin-rescue?ws=" + ws)[2]
    assert detail["dir"] == sdir and detail["story_md"] == {"text": "## Panel 1\n",
                                                            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(b"## Panel 1\n").hexdigest()}
    assert detail["files"] == ["clips", "images", "movie.mp4", "plan.json", "runs", "story.md", "story_checks.json"]
    assert detail["checks"] == checks and detail["plan_mode"] == "shots" and detail["plan_loras"] == {"global": []}
    assert detail["stills"] == [os.path.join(sdir, "images", "panel_01.png"), os.path.join(sdir, "images", "panel_02.png")]
    assert detail["clips"] == [os.path.join(sdir, "clips", "panel_01.mp4")]
    assert detail["movie"] == os.path.join(sdir, "movie.mp4") and detail["summary"] == {"run": "b"}
    assert detail["catalog_loras"] is None and detail["last_fields"] is None
    created = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/jobs", body=_compose(env, goal="Keep it tense."))[2]
    job = lib.read_job(lib.job_dir(env["state"], created["job"]["id"]))
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/stories/ronin-rescue?ws=" + ws)[2]["last_fields"] == job["fields"]
    assert job["fields"]["goal"] == "Keep it tense."
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/stories/nope?ws=" + ws)[0] == 404
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/stories/a..b?ws=" + ws)[:3:2] == (404, {"error": "not found"})


def test_mut20_story_md_save_backup_and_conflicts(env, monkeypatch):
    s, ws, state = env["srv"], env["ws"], env["state"]
    sdir = _make_story(ws, "ronin-rescue", "v1\n")
    sha1 = hashlib.sha256(b"v1\n").hexdigest()
    put = "/api/stories/ronin-rescue/story.md"
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "PUT", put, body={"ws": ws, "text": "v2\r\n", "base_sha256": sha1})
    assert status == 200 and payload["sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(b"v2\r\n").hexdigest()
    backup = payload["backup"]
    assert re.fullmatch(re.escape(os.path.join(sdir, "story.md.bak-")) + r"[0-9]{8}T[0-9]{6}Z", backup)
    with open(backup, "rb") as f:
        assert f.read() == b"v1\n"
    with open(os.path.join(sdir, "story.md"), "rb") as f:
        assert f.read() == b"v2\r\n"
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "PUT", put, body={"ws": ws, "text": "v3", "base_sha256": sha1})
    assert (status, payload) == (409, {"error": "story.md changed since it was loaded; reload it",
                                       "sha256": hashlib.sha256(b"v2\r\n").hexdigest()})
    sha2 = hashlib.sha256(b"v2\r\n").hexdigest()
    stamp = backup[len(os.path.join(sdir, "story.md.bak-")):]
    original = time.strftime

    def frozen(fmt, *args):
        return stamp if fmt == "%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ" else original(fmt, *args)
    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.time, "strftime", frozen)
    payload = mt.api(s, "PUT", put, body={"ws": ws, "text": "v3", "base_sha256": sha2})[2]
    assert payload["backup"] == backup + "-2"
    payload = mt.api(s, "PUT", put, body={"ws": ws, "text": "v4", "base_sha256": hashlib.sha256(b"v3").hexdigest()})[2]
    assert payload["backup"] == backup + "-3"
    monkeypatch.setattr(srv.time, "strftime", original)
    fresh = _make_story(ws, "fresh")
    assert mt.api(s, "PUT", "/api/stories/fresh/story.md", body={"ws": ws, "text": "x", "base_sha256": sha1})[0] == 409
    payload = mt.api(s, "PUT", "/api/stories/fresh/story.md", body={"ws": ws, "text": "x", "base_sha256": None})[2]
    assert payload["backup"] is None and os.path.isfile(os.path.join(fresh, "story.md"))
    assert mt.api(s, "PUT", "/api/stories/missing/story.md", body={"ws": ws, "text": "x", "base_sha256": None})[0] == 404
    busy = mt.make_job(state, ["true"], ws, story_id="ronin-rescue", status={
        "state": "running", "dispatched": lib.utc_now(), "runner_pid": 1, "pgid": None, "started": lib.utc_now()})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "PUT", put, body={"ws": ws, "text": "v5",
                                                      "base_sha256": hashlib.sha256(b"v4").hexdigest()})
    assert (status, payload) == (409, {"error": "story ronin-rescue is in use by job %s" % busy})


def test_mut19_files_roots_ranges_and_token(env):
    s, ws, state = env["srv"], env["ws"], env["state"]
    data = bytes(range(100))
    sdir = _make_story(ws, "ronin-rescue", files=[("clips/panel_01.mp4", data)])
    clip = os.path.join(sdir, "clips", "panel_01.mp4")
    outside = os.path.join(env["tmp"], "secret.txt")
    with open(outside, "w") as f:
        f.write("secret")
    os.symlink(outside, os.path.join(sdir, "link.txt"))

    def get(path, token=mt.TOKEN, headers=None, t=None):
        url = "/files?ws=%s&path=%s" % (ws, path) + ("&t=%s" % t if t else "")
        return mt.api(s, "GET", url, token=token, headers=headers)
    status, headers, body = get(clip)
    assert (status, body) == (200, data)
    assert headers["content-type"] == "video/mp4" and headers["accept-ranges"] == "bytes"
    assert headers["x-content-type-options"] == "nosniff" and headers["cache-control"] == "no-store"
    assert get(clip, token=None)[0] == 401
    assert get(clip, token=None, t="wrong")[0] == 401
    assert get(clip, token=None, t=mt.TOKEN)[:3:2] == (200, data)
    assert get(os.path.join(sdir, "..", "..", "..", "..", "secret.txt"))[:3:2] == (403, {"error": "path not allowed"})
    assert get(os.path.join(sdir, "link.txt"))[:3:2] == (403, {"error": "path not allowed"})
    assert get(os.path.join(ws, "generated"))[0] == 403
    assert get(os.path.join(sdir, "nope.mp4"))[0] == 404
    for rng, start, end in (("bytes=0-9", 0, 9), ("bytes=10-", 10, 99), ("bytes=-5", 95, 99), ("bytes=90-500", 90, 99)):
        status, headers, body = get(clip, headers={"Range": rng})
        assert (status, body) == (206, data[start:end + 1]), rng
        assert headers["content-range"] == "bytes %d-%d/100" % (start, end)
        assert headers["content-length"] == str(end - start + 1)
    for rng in ("bytes=100-", "bytes=9-3", "bytes=-0"):
        status, headers, body = get(clip, headers={"Range": rng})
        assert (status, headers["content-range"], body) == (416, "bytes */100", b""), rng
    for rng in ("items=0-1", "bytes=0-1,5-6", "bytes=-"):
        assert get(clip, headers={"Range": rng})[:3:2] == (200, data), rng
    upload = _upload(s, mt.PNG, "image/png")[2]["path"]
    status, headers, body = get(upload)
    assert (status, headers["content-type"], body) == (200, "image/png", mt.PNG)


def test_review_focus_1_non_ascii_story_round_trip(env):
    """Review Focus 1: a narrative and a story.md edit with accents, CJK and emoji."""
    s, ws = env["srv"], env["ws"]
    narrative = "Ein Rōnin rettet ein Kind — 子供を救う 🔥"
    preview = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/preview", body=_compose(env, narrative=narrative))[2]
    assert preview["argv"][2] == narrative
    sdir = _make_story(ws, "ronin-rescue", "## Panel 1 — Café\n")
    detail = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/stories/ronin-rescue?ws=" + ws)[2]
    assert detail["story_md"]["text"] == "## Panel 1 — Café\n"
    text = detail["story_md"]["text"] + "Image: 夜の市場、提灯 🏮\n"
    payload = mt.api(s, "PUT", "/api/stories/ronin-rescue/story.md",
                     body={"ws": ws, "text": text, "base_sha256": detail["story_md"]["sha256"]})[2]
    with open(os.path.join(sdir, "story.md"), "rb") as f:
        assert f.read() == text.encode("utf-8")
    again = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/stories/ronin-rescue?ws=" + ws)[2]["story_md"]
    assert again == {"text": text, "sha256": payload["sha256"]}


def test_review_focus_2_files_with_spaces_and_non_ascii_names(env):
    """Review Focus 2: the page percent-encodes paths such as ".../My Movies/café still.png"."""
    s = env["srv"]
    ws = os.path.join(env["tmp"], "My Movies", "ws é")
    shutil.copytree(env["ws"], ws, symlinks=True)
    ws = os.path.realpath(ws)
    sdir = _make_story(ws, "ronin-rescue", files=[("images/café still+1.png", mt.PNG)])

    def enc(text):
        return "".join(c if (c.isascii() and c.isalnum()) or c in "/-_." else "".join("%%%02X" % b for b in c.encode("utf-8"))
                       for c in text)
    path = os.path.join(sdir, "images", "café still+1.png")
    status, headers, body = mt.api(s, "GET", "/files?ws=%s&path=%s" % (enc(ws), enc(path)))
    assert (status, headers["content-type"], body) == (200, "image/png", mt.PNG)
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/stories?ws=" + enc(ws))[2]["stories"][0]["id"] == "ronin-rescue"
```

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Stories, story.md edits and /files (MU54, MU69, MU70)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

PANEL_STILL_RE = re.compile(r"^panel_\d{2}\.png$")
PANEL_CLIP_RE = re.compile(r"^panel_\d{2}\.mp4$")


def _matching(directory, regex):
    try:
        return sorted(os.path.join(directory, n) for n in os.listdir(directory) if regex.fullmatch(n))
    except OSError:
        return []


def _checks_summary(checks):
    if not (isinstance(checks, dict) and isinstance(checks.get("hard"), list)
            and isinstance(checks.get("advisory"), list)):
        return None
    return {"source": checks.get("source"), "hard": len(checks["hard"]), "advisory": len(checks["advisory"]),
            "verdict": checks.get("verdict")}


def _h_stories(self, groups, query, body):
    real = self.ws(query.get("ws"))
    root = os.path.join(real, "generated", "stories")
    try:
        names = os.listdir(root)
    except OSError:
        names = []
    stories = []
    for name in names:
        sdir = os.path.join(root, name)
        if not (lib.valid_story_id(name) and name != lib.SMOKE_STORY_ID and os.path.isdir(sdir)):
            continue
        mtime = os.stat(sdir).st_mtime
        stories.append((mtime, {
            "id": name, "story_md": os.path.isfile(os.path.join(sdir, "story.md")),
            "checks": _checks_summary(lib.read_json(os.path.join(sdir, "story_checks.json"))),
            "stills": len(_matching(os.path.join(sdir, "images"), PANEL_STILL_RE)),
            "clips": len(_matching(os.path.join(sdir, "clips"), PANEL_CLIP_RE)),
            "movie": os.path.isfile(os.path.join(sdir, "movie.mp4")),
            "mtime": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime(mtime))}))
    stories.sort(key=lambda pair: (-pair[0], pair[1]["id"]))
    return 200, {"stories_dir": root, "stories": [s for _m, s in stories]}


def _story_dir(self, groups, value):
    real = self.ws(value)
    story_id = self.story_id(groups[0])
    sdir = lib.story_dir(real, story_id)
    if not os.path.isdir(sdir):
        raise ApiError(404, "no story %s in %s" % (story_id, os.path.dirname(sdir)))
    return real, story_id, sdir


def _read_bytes(path):
    try:
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            return f.read()
    except OSError:
        return None


def _h_story(self, groups, query, body):
    real, story_id, sdir = _story_dir(self, groups, query.get("ws"))
    raw = _read_bytes(os.path.join(sdir, "story.md"))
    plan = lib.read_json(os.path.join(sdir, "plan.json"))
    plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else {}
    runs = os.path.join(sdir, "runs")
    try:
        run_names = sorted((n for n in os.listdir(runs)
                            if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(runs, n, "story_summary.json"))), reverse=True)
    except OSError:
        run_names = []
    movie = os.path.join(sdir, "movie.mp4")
    job = newest_job(self.server, ("story", "movie"), real, "story_id", story_id)
    return 200, {"id": story_id, "dir": sdir, "files": sorted(os.listdir(sdir)),
                 "story_md": None if raw is None else {"text": raw.decode("utf-8", "replace"),
                                                       "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()},
                 "checks": lib.read_json(os.path.join(sdir, "story_checks.json")),
                 "plan_loras": plan.get("loras"), "plan_mode": plan.get("mode"),
                 "catalog_loras": lib.read_json(os.path.join(sdir, "catalog_loras.json")),
                 "stills": _matching(os.path.join(sdir, "images"), PANEL_STILL_RE),
                 "clips": _matching(os.path.join(sdir, "clips"), PANEL_CLIP_RE),
                 "movie": movie if os.path.isfile(movie) else None,
                 "summary": lib.read_json(os.path.join(runs, run_names[0], "story_summary.json")) if run_names else None,
                 "last_fields": job["fields"] if job is not None else None}


def _h_story_save(self, groups, query, body):
    real, story_id, sdir = _story_dir(self, groups, body.get("ws"))
    text, base = body.get("text"), body.get("base_sha256")
    if not isinstance(text, str):
        raise ApiError(400, "text must be a string")
    if base is not None and not (isinstance(base, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", base)):
        raise ApiError(400, "base_sha256 must be 64 hex digits or null")
    path = os.path.join(sdir, "story.md")
    with STORE_LOCK:
        for job_id in lib.active_job_ids(self.server.state):
            job = self.server.job(job_id)
            if job is not None and job["workspace"] == real and job["story_id"] == story_id:
                raise ApiError(409, "story %s is in use by job %s" % (story_id, job_id))
        raw = _read_bytes(path)
        current = None if raw is None else hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if current != base:
            raise ApiError(409, "story.md changed since it was loaded; reload it", sha256=current)
        backup = None
        if raw is not None:
            stem = "%s.bak-%s" % (path, time.strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ", time.gmtime()))
            backup, n = stem, 1
            while os.path.lexists(backup):
                n += 1
                backup = "%s-%d" % (stem, n)
            lib.write_bytes_atomic(backup, raw)
        data = text.encode("utf-8")
        lib.write_bytes_atomic(path, data)
    return 200, {"sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), "backup": backup}


def _h_files(self, groups, query, body):
    path = query.get("path")
    real_ws = self.ws(query.get("ws"))
    if not (isinstance(path, str) and os.path.isabs(path)):
        raise ApiError(400, "path must be an absolute path")
    real = os.path.realpath(path)
    roots = (os.path.realpath(os.path.join(real_ws, "generated")),
             os.path.realpath(os.path.join(self.server.state, "uploads")))
    if not any(real.startswith(root + os.sep) for root in roots):
        raise ApiError(403, "path not allowed")
    if not os.path.isfile(real):
        raise ApiError(404, "not found")
    size = os.path.getsize(real)
    ctype = FILE_TYPES.get(os.path.splitext(real)[1].lower(), "application/octet-stream")
    start, end, status = 0, size - 1, 200
    m = RANGE_RE.match(self.headers.get("Range") or "")
    if m and (m.group(1) or m.group(2)):
        if m.group(1):
            start = int(m.group(1))
            end = min(int(m.group(2)), size - 1) if m.group(2) else size - 1
        else:
            start, end = max(size - int(m.group(2)), 0), size - 1
            if int(m.group(2)) == 0:
                start = size
        if start > end or start >= size:
            self.send_response(416)
            for key, value in (("Content-Range", "bytes */%d" % size), ("Content-Length", "0"),
                               ("Accept-Ranges", "bytes"), ("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff"),
                               ("Cache-Control", "no-store")):
                self.send_header(key, value)
            self.end_headers()
            return None
        status = 206
    self.send_response(status)
    self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(end - start + 1))
    if status == 206:
        self.send_header("Content-Range", "bytes %d-%d/%d" % (start, end, size))
    for key, value in (("Accept-Ranges", "bytes"), ("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff"),
                       ("Cache-Control", "no-store")):
        self.send_header(key, value)
    self.end_headers()
    try:
        with open(real, "rb") as f:
            f.seek(start)
            left = end - start + 1
            while left > 0:
                chunk = f.read(min(FILE_CHUNK, left))
                if not chunk:
                    break
                self.wfile.write(chunk)
                left -= len(chunk)
    except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
        pass
    return None


MovieUIHandler.h_stories = _h_stories
MovieUIHandler.h_story = _h_story
MovieUIHandler.h_story_save = _h_story_save
MovieUIHandler.h_files = _h_files
```

**Files:**

- Modify: `tests/movie_ui_testutil.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `fleet_lib.agent_ids_on_disk`, `deploy_root`, `agent_port`, `DEFAULT_PORT`, `WS_SUBDIR`, `fetch_recent`, `fetch_trace`, `render_trace`, `valid_trace_id`, `valid_trace_result`, `TRACE_RECENT_DEFAULT`, `TRACE_RECENT_MAX`; Task 11's `default_agent`; Task 2's `read_json`, `write_json_atomic`, `utc_now`, `SAVED_TRACE_SCHEMA`.
- Produces: `trace_agents(ui_ws) -> [{"id", "port", "port_source", "url", "error"}]`, `trace_error_text(message)`, `trace_skew(result) -> [span_id]`, `trace_view(agent, result, with_text)`, `load_saved(path, trace_id) -> (envelope or None, valid)`, handlers `h_trace_agents`, `h_traces_recent`, `h_trace`, `h_trace_save`, `h_traces_saved`, `h_trace_saved`, `h_trace_saved_delete`. Testutil: `trace_record(trace_id, span_id, parent, agent, start, kind="turn", name="planner", dur_ms=1000, status="ok")`, `FakeDaemon(agent, recent=None, traces=None, status=200)` (`.port`, `.requests`, `close()`).

**1.1.** **Append** to `tests/movie_ui_testutil.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Trace records and a fake daemon
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def trace_record(trace_id, span_id, parent, agent, start, kind="turn", name="planner", dur_ms=1000,
                 status="ok"):
    attrs = {"turn": {"rounds": 1, "tool_calls": 0},
             "run": {"skill": "planner", "request_id": "r1", "http": 200, "images": 0, "image_bytes": 0}}[kind]
    return {"v": 1, "trace_id": trace_id, "span_id": span_id, "parent_span_id": parent, "agent": agent,
            "kind": kind, "name": name, "start": start, "dur_ms": dur_ms, "status": status, "attrs": attrs}


class FakeDaemon(object):
    """http.server on 127.0.0.1:<free port> answering GET /traces/recent?n= and GET /trace?id= with
    canned bodies (recent: a dict, traces: {trace_id: result dict}); records each request's headers."""

    def __init__(self, agent, recent=None, traces=None, status=200):
        self.agent, self.recent, self.traces, self.status = agent, recent, traces or {}, status
        self.requests = []
        outer = self

        class Handler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
                pass

            def do_GET(self):
                outer.requests.append({"path": self.path, "headers": dict(self.headers.items())})
                if self.path.startswith("/traces/recent"):
                    obj = outer.recent if outer.recent is not None else {
                        "schema": fleet_lib.RECENT_SCHEMA, "agent": outer.agent, "traces": []}
                elif self.path.startswith("/trace?id="):
                    obj = outer.traces.get(self.path[len("/trace?id="):])
                else:
                    obj = None
                code = outer.status if obj is not None else 404
                data = json.dumps(obj if obj is not None else {"error": "not found"}).encode("utf-8")
                self.send_response(code)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(data)

        self.httpd = http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
        self.port = self.httpd.server_address[1]
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.httpd.serve_forever, daemon=True)
        self.thread.start()

    def close(self):
        self.httpd.shutdown()
        self.httpd.server_close()
```

**1.2.** **Create** `tests/test_movie_ui_traces.py` with exactly this content:

```python
"""movie-ui trace proxy tests (movie-ui spec MUT22, MU20, MU71-MU74, D9).
Run from the workspace root: python3 -m pytest tests/test_movie_ui_traces.py -v
Daemons are FakeDaemon instances on 127.0.0.1; no real daemon is contacted."""

import json
import os

import pytest

import fleet_lib
import movie_ui_testutil as mt
import movie_ui_lib as lib

T1 = "11111111111111111111111111111111"
T2 = "22222222222222222222222222222222"
T3 = "33333333333333333333333333333333"


@pytest.fixture
def env(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    state = mt.set_home(monkeypatch, tmp_path)
    running = mt.Running(state)
    daemons = []
    yield {"state": state, "srv": running, "home": os.environ["HOME"], "tmp": str(tmp_path), "daemons": daemons}
    running.close()
    for d in daemons:
        d.close()


def _summary(trace_id, start, delegated=(), status="ok"):
    return {"trace_id": trace_id, "start": start, "dur_ms": 1500, "status": status, "kind": "turn",
            "name": "planner", "spans": 3, "delegated": list(delegated)}


def _recent(agent, traces):
    return {"schema": fleet_lib.RECENT_SCHEMA, "agent": agent, "traces": traces}


def _result(trace_id, records, missing=(), truncated=()):
    return {"schema": fleet_lib.TRACE_SCHEMA, "trace_id": trace_id, "records": records,
            "missing": list(missing), "truncated": list(truncated)}


def _agents(env, ports):
    """Two agents on disk whose registries name the given ports (None: no registry file)."""
    spec = {}
    for agent, port in ports.items():
        spec[agent] = {"current": "v2026.10.01", "releases": {"v2026.10.01": {}}}
        if port is not None:
            spec[agent]["port"] = port
    mt.make_agents_home(env["home"], spec)


def test_mut22_agents_registry_default_and_disagreeing_ports(env):
    _agents(env, {"alpha": 9001, "beta": None})
    gamma = os.path.join(env["home"], "agents", "gamma")
    mt.make_agents_home(env["home"], {"gamma": {"current": "v2026.10.01", "releases": {"v2026.10.01": {}},
                                                "ports": [9100, 9200]}})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(env["srv"], "GET", "/api/traces/agents")
    assert status == 200 and payload["default"] is None
    fleets = os.path.join(gamma, "current", "qwen-agent-workspace", "fleets")
    assert payload["agents"] == [
        {"id": "alpha", "port": 9001, "port_source": "registry", "url": "http://127.0.0.1:9001", "error": None},
        {"id": "beta", "port": fleet_lib.DEFAULT_PORT, "port_source": "default",
         "url": "http://127.0.0.1:%d" % fleet_lib.DEFAULT_PORT, "error": None},
        {"id": "gamma", "port": None, "port_source": None, "url": None,
         "error": "agent 'gamma' has different ports in f0.json, f1.json in %s" % fleets}]


def test_mut22_recent_merge_errors_and_wire_headers(env):
    a = mt.FakeDaemon("alpha", recent=_recent("alpha", [_summary(T1, 100.0, ["analyst@beta"]), _summary(T2, 300.0)]))
    b = mt.FakeDaemon("beta", recent=_recent("beta", [_summary(T1, 99.5), _summary(T3, 200.0)]))
    env["daemons"] += [a, b]
    dead = mt.free_port()
    _agents(env, {"alpha": a.port, "beta": b.port, "zeta": dead})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(env["srv"], "GET", "/api/traces/recent?agent=all&n=10")
    assert status == 200
    assert [(t["trace_id"], t["start"], t["seen_on"], t["agents"]) for t in payload["traces"]] == [
        (T2, 300.0, ["alpha"], ["alpha"]), (T3, 200.0, ["beta"], ["beta"]), (T1, 99.5, ["alpha", "beta"], ["alpha", "beta"])]
    assert payload["errors"] == [{"agent": "zeta", "error": "daemon: not reachable at http://127.0.0.1:%d" % dead}]
    for daemon in (a, b):
        request = daemon.requests[-1]
        assert request["path"] == "/traces/recent?n=10"
        assert request["headers"]["Host"] == "127.0.0.1:%d" % daemon.port
        assert "Origin" not in request["headers"]
    assert len(mt.api(env["srv"], "GET", "/api/traces/recent?n=1")[2]["traces"]) == 1
    only = mt.api(env["srv"], "GET", "/api/traces/recent?agent=beta")[2]
    assert [t["trace_id"] for t in only["traces"]] == [T3, T1] and only["errors"] == []
    assert mt.api(env["srv"], "GET", "/api/traces/recent?agent=nobody")[:3:2] == (404, {"error": "unknown agent"})
    for bad in ("0", "101", "x", "1000"):
        assert mt.api(env["srv"], "GET", "/api/traces/recent?n=" + bad)[:3:2] == (400, {"error": "n must be 1 to 100"})


def test_mut22_one_trace_text_skew_save_list_load_delete(env):
    records = [mt.trace_record(T1, "a" * 16, None, "alpha", 100.0, dur_ms=5000),
               mt.trace_record(T1, "b" * 16, "a" * 16, "beta", 99.0, kind="run", dur_ms=2000),
               mt.trace_record(T1, "c" * 16, "f" * 16, "beta", 101.0)]
    result = _result(T1, records, missing=[{"agent": "gamma", "reason": "timeout"}])
    a = mt.FakeDaemon("alpha", traces={T1: result})
    env["daemons"].append(a)
    _agents(env, {"alpha": a.port})
    s = env["srv"]
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/traces/%s?agent=alpha&format=text" % T1)
    assert status == 200 and payload["agent"] == "alpha" and payload["result"] == result
    assert payload["skew"] == ["b" * 16]
    assert payload["text"] == "\n".join(fleet_lib.render_trace(result)) + "\n"
    assert a.requests[-1]["path"] == "/trace?id=" + T1 and "Origin" not in a.requests[-1]["headers"]
    assert "text" not in mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/traces/%s?agent=alpha" % T1)[2]
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/traces/%s" % T1)[2]["agent"] == "alpha"
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/traces/%s?agent=nobody" % T1)[:3:2] == (404, {"error": "unknown agent"})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/traces/%s?agent=alpha" % T2)
    assert (status, payload) == (502, {"error": "the daemon at http://127.0.0.1:%d has no trace support (http 404)"
                                                % a.port})
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "POST", "/api/traces/%s/save" % T1, body={"agent": "alpha"})
    path = os.path.join(env["state"], "traces", T1 + ".json")
    assert (status, payload) == (200, {"saved": T1, "path": path})
    saved = lib.read_json(path)
    assert saved["schema"] == "movie-ui.saved-trace.v1" and saved["result"] == result and saved["agent"] == "alpha"
    a.close()
    env["daemons"].remove(a)
    status, _h, payload = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/traces/%s?agent=alpha" % T1)
    assert (status, payload) == (502, {"error": "daemon: not reachable at http://127.0.0.1:%d" % a.port})
    with open(os.path.join(env["state"], "traces", T2 + ".json"), "w") as f:
        f.write("{broken")
    listing = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/traces/saved")[2]["saved"]
    assert listing == [
        {"trace_id": T1, "agent": "alpha", "saved_at": saved["saved_at"], "spans": 3, "start": 99.0, "invalid": False},
        {"trace_id": T2, "agent": None, "saved_at": None, "spans": 0, "start": None, "invalid": True}]
    view = mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/traces/saved/%s" % T1)[2]
    assert view["result"] == result and view["skew"] == ["b" * 16]
    assert view["text"] == "\n".join(fleet_lib.render_trace(result)) + "\n"
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/traces/saved/%s" % T2)[:3:2] == (422, {"error": "saved trace is invalid"})
    assert mt.api(s, "GET", "/api/traces/saved/%s" % T3)[0] == 404
    assert mt.api(s, "DELETE", "/api/traces/saved/%s" % T1)[:3:2] == (200, {"deleted": T1})
    assert mt.api(s, "DELETE", "/api/traces/saved/%s" % T1)[0] == 404
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

**3.1.** **Append** to `movie_ui_server.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Traces (MU20, MU71-MU74)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def trace_agents(ui_ws):
    """MU71: one entry per agent on disk, with its loopback daemon URL from the registry."""
    out = []
    for agent in fleet_lib.agent_ids_on_disk():
        a = fleet_lib.deploy_root(agent)
        current = os.path.join(a, "current")
        fleets = (os.path.join(current, fleet_lib.WS_SUBDIR, "fleets") if os.path.exists(current)
                  else os.path.join(ui_ws, "fleets"))
        port, files = fleet_lib.agent_port(fleets, agent)
        entry = {"id": agent, "port": port, "port_source": "registry", "url": None, "error": None}
        if not files:
            entry["port"], entry["port_source"] = fleet_lib.DEFAULT_PORT, "default"
        elif port is None:
            entry["port_source"] = None
            entry["error"] = "agent '%s' has different ports in %s in %s" % (agent, ", ".join(files), fleets)
        if entry["port"] is not None:
            entry["url"] = "http://127.0.0.1:%d" % entry["port"]
        out.append(entry)
    return out


def trace_error_text(message):
    """D9: fleet_lib's "daemon not reachable at" is shown as qwen-agentd status words it."""
    prefix = "daemon not reachable at "
    return "daemon: not reachable at " + message[len(prefix):] if message.startswith(prefix) else message


def trace_skew(result):
    """span_id of every record whose parent is present and starts after it (MU73)."""
    starts = dict((r["span_id"], r["start"]) for r in result["records"])
    return [r["span_id"] for r in result["records"]
            if r["parent_span_id"] in starts and r["start"] < starts[r["parent_span_id"]]]


def trace_view(agent, result, with_text):
    view = {"agent": agent, "result": result, "skew": trace_skew(result)}
    if with_text:
        view["text"] = "\n".join(fleet_lib.render_trace(result)) + "\n"
    return view


def _h_trace_agents(self, groups, query, body):
    return 200, {"default": default_agent(), "agents": trace_agents(self.server.ui_ws)}


def _h_traces_recent(self, groups, query, body):
    n_text = query.get("n", str(fleet_lib.TRACE_RECENT_DEFAULT))
    if not (N_RE.fullmatch(n_text) and 1 <= int(n_text) <= fleet_lib.TRACE_RECENT_MAX):
        raise ApiError(400, "n must be 1 to 100")
    n = int(n_text)
    agents = trace_agents(self.server.ui_ws)
    wanted = query.get("agent", "all")
    if wanted != "all":
        agents = [a for a in agents if a["id"] == wanted]
        if not agents:
            raise ApiError(404, "unknown agent")
    best, seen, errors = {}, {}, []
    for agent in agents:
        if agent["url"] is None:
            errors.append({"agent": agent["id"], "error": agent["error"]})
            continue
        result, error = fleet_lib.fetch_recent(agent["url"], n)
        if error is not None:
            errors.append({"agent": agent["id"], "error": trace_error_text(error)})
            continue
        for item in result["traces"]:
            tid = item["trace_id"]
            seen.setdefault(tid, set()).add(agent["id"])
            old = best.get(tid)
            if old is None or (item["start"], agent["id"]) < (old[0]["start"], old[1]):
                best[tid] = (item, agent["id"])
    traces = []
    for tid, (item, _agent) in best.items():
        entry = dict(item)
        entry["seen_on"] = sorted(seen[tid])
        entry["agents"] = sorted(set(seen[tid]) | set(h.rpartition("@")[2] for h in item["delegated"]))
        traces.append(entry)
    traces.sort(key=lambda t: (-t["start"], t["trace_id"]))
    return 200, {"traces": traces[:n], "errors": errors}


def _agent_url(self, query):
    agents = dict((a["id"], a) for a in trace_agents(self.server.ui_ws))
    agent = query.get("agent") or default_agent()
    if agent is None or agent not in agents:
        raise ApiError(404, "unknown agent")
    if agents[agent]["url"] is None:
        raise ApiError(502, agents[agent]["error"])
    return agent, agents[agent]["url"]


def _fetch(self, groups, query):
    agent, url = _agent_url(self, query)
    result, error = fleet_lib.fetch_trace(url, groups[0])
    if error is not None:
        raise ApiError(502, trace_error_text(error))
    return agent, result


def _h_trace(self, groups, query, body):
    agent, result = _fetch(self, groups, query)
    return 200, trace_view(agent, result, query.get("format") == "text")


def _saved_path(server, trace_id):
    return os.path.join(server.state, "traces", trace_id + ".json")


def _h_trace_save(self, groups, query, body):
    agent, result = _fetch(self, groups, {"agent": body.get("agent")})
    path = _saved_path(self.server, groups[0])
    lib.write_json_atomic(path, {"schema": lib.SAVED_TRACE_SCHEMA, "trace_id": groups[0], "agent": agent,
                                 "saved_at": lib.utc_now(), "result": result})
    return 200, {"saved": groups[0], "path": path}


def load_saved(path, trace_id):
    """(envelope, valid) for one saved trace file; envelope is None when it does not parse."""
    obj = lib.read_json(path)
    if not isinstance(obj, dict):
        return None, False
    valid = (obj.get("schema") == lib.SAVED_TRACE_SCHEMA and obj.get("trace_id") == trace_id
             and fleet_lib.valid_trace_result(obj.get("result"), trace_id))
    return obj, valid


def _h_traces_saved(self, groups, query, body):
    root = os.path.join(self.server.state, "traces")
    try:
        names = sorted(os.listdir(root))
    except OSError:
        names = []
    out = []
    for name in names:
        trace_id = name[:-5]
        if not (name.endswith(".json") and fleet_lib.valid_trace_id(trace_id)):
            continue
        obj, valid = load_saved(os.path.join(root, name), trace_id)
        records = obj["result"]["records"] if valid else []
        out.append({"trace_id": trace_id, "agent": (obj or {}).get("agent") if valid else None,
                    "saved_at": (obj or {}).get("saved_at") if valid else None, "spans": len(records),
                    "start": min(r["start"] for r in records) if records else None, "invalid": not valid})
    out.sort(key=lambda s: (s["saved_at"] or "", s["trace_id"]), reverse=True)
    return 200, {"saved": out}


def _h_trace_saved(self, groups, query, body):
    path = _saved_path(self.server, groups[0])
    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        raise ApiError(404, "no saved trace %s" % groups[0])
    obj, valid = load_saved(path, groups[0])
    if not valid:
        raise ApiError(422, "saved trace is invalid")
    return 200, trace_view(obj.get("agent"), obj["result"], True)


def _h_trace_saved_delete(self, groups, query, body):
    path = _saved_path(self.server, groups[0])
    try:
        os.unlink(path)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        raise ApiError(404, "no saved trace %s" % groups[0])
    return 200, {"deleted": groups[0]}


MovieUIHandler.h_trace_agents = _h_trace_agents
MovieUIHandler.h_traces_recent = _h_traces_recent
MovieUIHandler.h_trace = _h_trace
MovieUIHandler.h_trace_save = _h_trace_save
MovieUIHandler.h_traces_saved = _h_traces_saved
MovieUIHandler.h_trace_saved = _h_trace_saved
MovieUIHandler.h_trace_saved_delete = _h_trace_saved_delete
```

### Task 16: The page: `index.html`, `app.js`, `app.css`

Implements MU75-MU85 (the token handshake, the header and badges, six tabs, Compose with live preview, Stories with the editor, Characters, LoRAs & Models, Jobs with the live log and job traces, Traces with the waterfall, save and download) and MU12's page half. The smoke button and V7 line arrive with Task 17. Tests: MUT24 (headers and asset rules); the behaviour is checked by MUT27 in Task 18.

**Files:**
- Create: `static/movie-ui/index.html`
- Create: `static/movie-ui/app.css`
- Create: `static/movie-ui/app.js`
- Modify: `tests/test_movie_ui_http.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every `/api/*` route of Tasks 11-15 with the JSON shapes their tests pin; `/files?ws=&path=&t=`.
- Produces: the element IDs of MU76-MU85 (plus `#c-write-story-reason`, `#c-run-all-reason`, `#c-seed-status`, `#c-command-for`, `#s-title`, `#s-redo-box`, `#s-action-status`, `#ch-title`, `#ch-status`, `#ch-create-status`, `#ch-train-reason`, `#j-log-title`, `#t-header`, `#t-meta`, `#t-status`); in `app.js`, inside one IIFE: `S` (page state), `$`, `el`, `clear`, `show`, `enc`, `api(method, path, body, raw)`, `askConfirm(text) -> Promise<bool>`, `capability(name)`, `gate(button, reasonNode, needs)`, `updateComposeButtons()`, `composeFields(action)`, `submit(fields)`, `preview` (debounced 300 ms), `pollJobs`, `pollLog`, `selectJob`, `openStory`, `openTrace`, `renderTrace`, `setupCompose`, `setupStories`, `setupCharacters`, `setupLoras`, `setupJobs`, `setupTraces`, `start`. Task 17 edits `updateComposeButtons`, inserts a section before `// --- stories (MU81)`, and adds `setupSmoke();` after `setupCompose();`.

**1.1.** **Append** to `tests/test_movie_ui_http.py`:

```python
PAGE_HEADERS = {
    "content-security-policy": ("default-src 'self'; img-src 'self' blob:; media-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; "
                                "base-uri 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'; form-action 'self'"),
    "x-frame-options": "DENY", "x-content-type-options": "nosniff", "referrer-policy": "no-referrer",
    "cache-control": "no-store"}


def test_mut24_page_headers_and_asset_rules(env):
    s = env["srv"]
    static = os.path.join(mt.WS, "static", "movie-ui")
    for path, name, ctype in (("/", "index.html", "text/html; charset=utf-8"),
                              ("/static/app.js", "app.js", "text/javascript; charset=utf-8"),
                              ("/static/app.css", "app.css", "text/css; charset=utf-8")):
        status, headers, body = mt.api(s, "GET", path, token=None)
        assert status == 200 and headers["content-type"] == ctype
        for key, value in PAGE_HEADERS.items():
            assert headers[key] == value, (path, key)
        with open(os.path.join(static, name), "rb") as f:
            assert body == f.read()
        text = body.decode("utf-8")
        for pattern in (r"https?://", r"\son[a-z]+=", r"style=", r"\balert\(", r"\bconfirm\(", r"\bprompt\(",
                        r"\beval\(", r"new Function"):
            assert not re.search(pattern, text), (name, pattern)
    html = mt.api(s, "GET", "/", token=None)[2].decode("utf-8")
    assert re.findall(r"<script[^>]*>", html) == ['<script src="/static/app.js" defer>']
    assert "<script src=\"/static/app.js\" defer></script>" in html
    assert html.count('<link rel="stylesheet" href="/static/app.css">') == 1
    for element in ("banner", "ws-select", "ws-typed", "ws-typed-use", "badge-render", "badge-stills", "badge-story",
                    "badge-reasons", "badge-refresh", "badge-apikey", "header-job", "header-queue", "confirm",
                    "confirm-text", "confirm-yes", "confirm-no", "c-command", "c-errors", "c-checks", "s-editor",
                    "j-log", "t-waterfall"):
        assert 'id="%s"' % element in html, element
    for tab in ("compose", "stories", "characters", "loras", "jobs", "traces"):
        assert 'id="tab-%s"' % tab in html and 'id="panel-%s"' % tab in html
    css = mt.api(s, "GET", "/static/app.css", token=None)[2].decode("utf-8")
    assert "@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)" in css
    assert mt.api(s, "POST", "/", body={}, token=None)[0] == 404
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

**3.1.** **Create** `static/movie-ui/index.html` with exactly this content:

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>movie-ui</title>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="/static/app.css">

</head>
<body>

<header>

    <h1>movie-ui</h1>

  </div>

</header>

<nav>
```
