### Task 18: Guards, the full suite, both interpreters, the zero diff and the end-to-end check

Implements MUT25 and MUT26 as pytest guards (MUK3, MUK7), then verifies MUK1, MUK2, MUK4-MUK6 on the whole tree and hands MUT27/MUK8 (Playwright) to the controller with an exact checklist.

**Files:**
- Modify: `tests/test_movie_ui_builder.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every earlier task.
- Produces: `NEW_PY`, `CHANGED_PY`, `PIPELINE_FILES`, `test_mut25_every_new_and_changed_file_compiles[/usr/bin/python3]`, `test_mut25_every_new_and_changed_file_compiles[<python3>]`, `test_mut25_source_scan_of_new_python_files`, `test_mut26_pipeline_files_are_unchanged`; the records `final-pytest.txt`, `py39-smoke.txt` and the MUT27 screenshots under `/Users/reubenpatterson/local_agent_harness/.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-19-movie-ui/`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests (guards).**

**1.1.** **Append** to `tests/test_movie_ui_builder.py`:

```python
NEW_PY = ("movie_ui_lib.py", "movie_ui_server.py", "bin/movie-ui", "bin/movie-ui-job", "tests/movie_ui_testutil.py",
          "tests/test_movie_ui_builder.py", "tests/test_movie_ui_checks.py", "tests/test_movie_ui_jobs.py",
          "tests/test_movie_ui_progress.py", "tests/test_movie_ui_http.py", "tests/test_movie_ui_traces.py")
CHANGED_PY = ("fleet_lib.py", "bin/qwen-agentd", "tests/test_fleet_lib.py", "tests/test_fleet_self_update.py")
PIPELINE_FILES = ("bin/ltx-movie", "bin/ltx-mlx-render", "bin/ltx-story-images", "bin/ltx-story-manifest",
                  "bin/ltx-story-video", "bin/ltx-chain", "bin/ltx-generate", "bin/ltx-host-prep",
                  "bin/ltx-host-restore", "bin/character", "bin/lora-catalog", "bin/judge-story", "bin/judge-clips",
                  "bin/judge-stills", "bin/iterate-story", "bin/qwen-agent", "story_engine.py", "character_lib.py",
                  "character_dataset.py", "lora_catalog.py", "pipeline_log.py", "z_image_skill.py",
                  "ltx2_mlx_video_skill.py")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("python", ["/usr/bin/python3", sys.executable])
def test_mut25_every_new_and_changed_file_compiles(python, tmp_path):
    if not os.path.isfile(python):
        pytest.skip("%s is not installed" % python)
    paths = [os.path.join(mt.WS, rel) for rel in NEW_PY + CHANGED_PY]
    proc = subprocess.run([python, "-m", "py_compile"] + paths, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120,
                          env=dict(os.environ, PYTHONPYCACHEPREFIX=str(tmp_path)))
    assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stderr


def test_mut25_source_scan_of_new_python_files():
    forbidden = [(re.compile(r"^\s*(import|from)\s+cgi\b", re.M), "cgi"),
                 (re.compile(r"\burllib\b"), "urllib"),
                 (re.compile(r"shell\s*=\s*True"), "shell=True"),
                 (re.compile(r"^\s*match\s+\S.*:\s*$", re.M), "match statement"),
                 (re.compile(r"->[^:\n]*\|"), "X | Y return annotation"),
                 (re.compile(r":\s*[A-Za-z_][\w.\[\], ]*\|\s*None"), "X | None annotation"),
                 (re.compile(r"strict\s*=\s*True\s*\)"), "zip(strict=)"),
                 (re.compile(r"load_module\("), "load_module")]
    for rel in NEW_PY[:4]:
        with open(os.path.join(mt.WS, rel), encoding="utf-8") as f:
            source = f.read()
        for regex, what in forbidden:
            assert not regex.search(source), (rel, what)
    for rel in NEW_PY[:2]:
        with open(os.path.join(mt.WS, rel), encoding="utf-8") as f:
            source = f.read()
        imported = set(re.findall(r"^(?:import|from)\s+([A-Za-z_][\w.]*)", source, re.M))
        allowed = {"argparse", "calendar", "errno", "fcntl", "hashlib", "hmac", "http.server", "json", "os",
                   "platform", "re", "secrets", "shlex", "shutil", "signal", "socket", "struct", "subprocess",
                   "sys", "threading", "time", "fleet_lib", "movie_ui_lib"}
        assert imported <= allowed, (rel, imported - allowed)
        for loader in ("importlib", "SourceFileLoader", "runpy", "exec(", "__import__"):
            assert loader not in source, (rel, loader)


def test_mut26_pipeline_files_are_unchanged():
    root = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", mt.WS, "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"], capture_output=True, text=True)
    if root.returncode != 0:
        pytest.skip("not a git checkout")
    if subprocess.run(["git", "-C", mt.WS, "cat-file", "-e", "22dc4a1^{commit}"], capture_output=True).returncode:
        pytest.skip("commit 22dc4a1 is not in this checkout")
    proc = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", mt.WS, "diff", "--quiet", "22dc4a1", "--"] + list(PIPELINE_FILES),
                          capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
```

- [ ] **Step 8: MUK1 and MUK7, every new and changed file under both interpreters, and a 3.9 runtime smoke.**

`/usr/bin/python3` (3.9.6) has no pytest on this Mac, so MUK1's "every test passes under 3.9.6" is covered by MUT25's compile case plus this runtime smoke, which starts `bin/movie-ui` under 3.9 from a copied UI with stub tools, submits a story job over HTTP, lets the 3.9 dispatcher and `bin/movie-ui-job` run it to `done`, and stops the server with SIGTERM (Plan decision 24).

```bash
D=/Users/reubenpatterson/local_agent_harness/.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-19-movie-ui
FILES="movie_ui_lib.py movie_ui_server.py bin/movie-ui bin/movie-ui-job fleet_lib.py bin/qwen-agentd tests/movie_ui_testutil.py tests/test_movie_ui_builder.py tests/test_movie_ui_checks.py tests/test_movie_ui_jobs.py tests/test_movie_ui_progress.py tests/test_movie_ui_http.py tests/test_movie_ui_traces.py tests/test_fleet_lib.py tests/test_fleet_self_update.py"

cat > $D/py39_smoke.py <<'PYEOF'
import http.client
import json
import os
import signal
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

sys.path.insert(0, "tests")
import movie_ui_testutil as mt  # noqa: E402

tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mu-py39-")
home = os.path.join(tmp, "home")
os.makedirs(home)
ui = mt.copy_ui(tmp)
port = mt.free_port()
env = dict(os.environ, HOME=home, PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1")
proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, os.path.join(ui, "bin", "movie-ui"), "--port", str(port)],
                        stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True, env=env)
token = proc.stdout.readline().strip().rsplit("?t=", 1)[1]
proc.stdout.readline()


def call(method, path, body=None):
    conn = http.client.HTTPConnection("127.0.0.1", port, timeout=60)
    data = json.dumps(body).encode() if body is not None else None
    headers = {"X-Movie-UI-Token": token}
    if data is not None:
        headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json"
    conn.request(method, path, body=data, headers=headers)
    resp = conn.getresponse()
    out = (resp.status, json.loads(resp.read()))
    conn.close()
    return out


try:
    status, created = call("POST", "/api/jobs", mt.fields(os.path.realpath(ui), panels=2))
    assert status == 201, created
    job_id = created["job"]["id"]
    deadline = time.time() + 60
    while time.time() < deadline:
        status, detail = call("GET", "/api/jobs/" + job_id)
        if detail["status"]["state"] in ("done", "failed", "cancelled", "lost"):
            break
        time.sleep(0.5)
    assert detail["status"]["state"] == "done", detail
    assert os.path.isfile(os.path.join(ui, "generated", "stories", "ronin-rescue", "story.md"))
    print("py%d.%d runtime ok: job %s done" % (sys.version_info[0], sys.version_info[1], job_id))
finally:
    proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)
    out, err = proc.communicate(timeout=30)
    assert proc.returncode == 0 and out == "movie-ui: stopped\n", (proc.returncode, out, err)
PYEOF
/usr/bin/python3 $D/py39_smoke.py | tee $D/py39-smoke.txt
python3 $D/py39_smoke.py
```

- [ ] **Step 10: MUT27 and MUK8, the Playwright end-to-end check (controller-run, not an executor step).**

Runs a copied UI with stub tools and an agent home whose daemon is a `FakeDaemon` holding one canned three-span trace, starts `bin/movie-ui` on a free port, and drives chromium headless through: open the printed URL (token handshake, address bar cleaned); compose `e2e-story` (2 panels, no-stills mode); Validate with every check `pass` or `skip`; Write story until the job is `done`; open the story, edit and save `story.md` (backup line shown); Render (Compose prefilled from `last_fields`, narrative kept), Run all until the movie job is `done` with panels 1..2 ok; the gallery shows the clips and `movie.mp4`; queue two more story jobs and cancel the queued one through the in-page confirm (`cancelled` in history); the Traces tab lists the canned trace, its waterfall has 3 rows, `#t-text` holds the `render_trace` text, a span click fills `#t-attrs`, Save writes it; three screenshots. The context sets `bypass_csp=True` only because Playwright's string predicates are evaluated as script; the page itself never needs it. The run uses `PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH` from the real home because the script switches `HOME` to a temp directory.

```bash
D=/Users/reubenpatterson/local_agent_harness/.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-19-movie-ui
cat > $D/run_e2e.py <<'PYEOF'
"""MUT27 driver: stub workspace + FakeDaemon + bin/movie-ui + Playwright (chromium headless)."""
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

WS = sys.argv[1]
OUT = sys.argv[2]
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(WS, "tests"))
import movie_ui_testutil as mt  # noqa: E402
import fleet_lib  # noqa: E402

tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mu-e2e-")
home = os.path.join(tmp, "home")
os.makedirs(home)
os.environ.setdefault("PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH", os.path.expanduser("~/Library/Caches/ms-playwright"))
os.environ["HOME"] = home
ui = mt.copy_ui(tmp)
tools = os.path.join(tmp, "tools")
os.makedirs(tools)
for name in ("ffmpeg", "ffprobe", "ltx-2-mlx"):
    p = os.path.join(tools, name)
    open(p, "w").write("#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n")
    os.chmod(p, 0o755)
cache = os.path.join(tmp, "ltxcache")
for repo in ("MLXBits/ltx-2.3-10eros-v1.2-dmd-mlx-q8", "mlx-community/gemma-3-12b-it-4bit"):
    mt.make_hf_cache(cache, repo, {"model.safetensors": b"\x01" * 8})
T1 = "abcdefabcdefabcdefabcdefabcdef12"
now = time.time()
records = [mt.trace_record(T1, "a" * 16, None, "mac-local", now - 30, dur_ms=8000),
           mt.trace_record(T1, "b" * 16, "a" * 16, "ollama-168", now - 28, kind="run", dur_ms=5000),
           mt.trace_record(T1, "c" * 16, "b" * 16, "ollama-168", now - 27, dur_ms=3000)]
result = {"schema": fleet_lib.TRACE_SCHEMA, "trace_id": T1, "records": records, "missing": [], "truncated": []}
recent = {"schema": fleet_lib.RECENT_SCHEMA, "agent": "mac-local", "traces": [
    {"trace_id": T1, "start": now - 30, "dur_ms": 8000, "status": "ok", "kind": "turn", "name": "planner",
     "spans": 3, "delegated": ["planner@ollama-168"]}]}
daemon = mt.FakeDaemon("mac-local", recent=recent, traces={T1: result})
mt.make_agents_home(home, {"mac-local": {"current": "v2026.10.19", "releases": {"v2026.10.19": {}},
                                         "port": daemon.port}})
port = mt.free_port()
env = dict(os.environ, HOME=home, PATH=tools + ":/usr/bin:/bin", LTX2_MLX_BIN=os.path.join(tools, "ltx-2-mlx"),
           LTX2_MLX_HF_HOME=cache, PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1")
env.pop("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", None)
server = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, os.path.join(ui, "bin", "movie-ui"), "--port", str(port)],
                          stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True, env=env)
url = server.stdout.readline().split(" ", 1)[1].strip()
print("URL", url)
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright  # noqa: E402

errors = []


def wait(page, predicate, timeout=30000):
    """page.wait_for_function, naming the predicate when it times out."""
    try:
        page.wait_for_function(predicate, timeout=timeout)
    except Exception:
        print("TIMED OUT waiting for:", predicate)
        raise


try:
    with sync_playwright() as p:
        browser = p.chromium.launch()
        context = browser.new_context(viewport={"width": 1400, "height": 1000}, bypass_csp=True)
        page = context.new_page()
        page.on("console", lambda m: errors.append(m.text) if m.type == "error" else None)
        page.on("pageerror", lambda e: errors.append(str(e)))
        page.goto(url)
        wait(page, "document.querySelector('#badge-render').textContent === 'render: ok'", 60000)
        assert page.evaluate("location.pathname + location.search") == "/"
        wait(page, "document.querySelectorAll('#ws-select option').length > 1")
        options = page.eval_on_selector_all("#ws-select option", "els => els.map(e => [e.value, e.textContent])")
        print("workspaces", options)
        page.select_option("#ws-select", os.path.realpath(ui))
        page.fill("#c-story-id", "e2e-story")
        page.fill("#c-narrative", "A lantern seller crosses a night market.")
        page.fill("#c-panels", "2")
        page.select_option("#c-mode", "no_stills")
        wait(page, "document.querySelector('#c-command').textContent.includes('--story-id e2e-story')")
        print("command", page.text_content("#c-command"))
        page.click("#c-validate")
        wait(page, "document.querySelectorAll('#c-checks li').length > 5", 60000)
        checks = page.eval_on_selector_all("#c-checks li", "els => els.map(e => e.textContent)")
        print("\n".join(checks))
        assert not any(c.startswith("fail") for c in checks), checks
        page.click("#c-write-story")
        wait(page, "!document.querySelector('#panel-jobs').hidden")
        wait(page, "Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#j-history td')).some(td => td.textContent === 'done')", 60000)
        page.click("#tab-stories")
        page.wait_for_selector("#s-list tr[data-story-id='e2e-story']")
        page.click("#s-list tr[data-story-id='e2e-story']")
        wait(page, "document.querySelector('#s-editor').value.includes('## Panel 1')")
        page.fill("#s-editor", page.input_value("#s-editor") + "\nEdited in the page.\n")
        page.click("#s-save")
        wait(page, "document.querySelector('#s-save-status').textContent.startsWith('saved')")
        print("save", page.text_content("#s-save-status"))
        page.click("#s-render")
        wait(page, "document.querySelector('#c-story-id').value === 'e2e-story'")
        assert page.input_value("#c-narrative") == "A lantern seller crosses a night market."
        wait(page, "!document.querySelector('#c-run-all').disabled")
        page.click("#c-run-all")
        wait(page, "Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#j-history tr')).filter(tr => tr.textContent.includes('movie') && tr.textContent.includes('done')).length === 1", 60000)
        page.click("#tab-stories")
        page.click("#s-list tr[data-story-id='e2e-story']")
        page.wait_for_selector("#s-gallery video")
        # queue a second job and cancel it while queued (a slow first job holds the runner)
        page.click("#tab-compose")
        page.fill("#c-story-id", "e2e-two")
        page.click("#c-write-story")
        page.fill("#c-story-id", "e2e-three")
        page.click("#c-write-story")
        wait(page, "document.querySelectorAll('#j-queue tbody tr').length >= 1 || document.querySelectorAll('#j-history tbody tr').length >= 4", 30000)
        rows = page.eval_on_selector_all("#j-queue tbody tr", "els => els.map(e => e.dataset.jobId)")
        print("queued", rows)
        if rows:
            page.click("#j-queue tbody tr button:has-text('Cancel')")
            page.click("#confirm-yes")
            wait(page, "Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#j-history td')).some(td => td.textContent === 'cancelled')", 30000)
        page.wait_for_timeout(1500)
        page.screenshot(path=os.path.join(OUT, "jobs.png"), full_page=True)
        page.click("#tab-traces")
        page.wait_for_selector("#t-recent tr[data-trace-id='%s']" % T1, timeout=30000)
        page.click("#t-recent tr[data-trace-id='%s']" % T1)
        page.wait_for_selector("#t-waterfall .span-row")
        print("spans", page.eval_on_selector_all("#t-waterfall .span-row", "els => els.length"))
        print("text", page.text_content("#t-text")[:200])
        page.click("#t-waterfall .span-row")
        print("attrs", page.text_content("#t-attrs")[:120])
        page.click("#t-save")
        wait(page, "document.querySelector('#t-status').textContent.startsWith('saved')")
        page.screenshot(path=os.path.join(OUT, "traces.png"), full_page=True)
        page.click("#tab-compose")
        page.screenshot(path=os.path.join(OUT, "compose.png"), full_page=True)
        browser.close()
finally:
    server.terminate()
    out, err = server.communicate(timeout=30)
    daemon.close()
    print("server stdout tail:", out[-200:])
    print("server stderr:", err[-2000:])
print("console errors:", errors)
PYEOF
python3 $D/run_e2e.py "$PWD" $D
```

## Plan decisions (where the spec is silent, split across sections, or self-contradictory)

The spec's design is unchanged. Each item is pinned by code shown in its task; each is a proposed ruling.

**Contradictions resolved (spec wins; the reading chosen):**

1. **G3 vs GC2: workspace discovery.** Spec 0.3 lists "workspace discovery" in `movie_ui_lib.py`, but MU33-MU35 need `fleet_lib`, which GC2 allows only in `movie_ui_server.py`. The global constraint wins: `default_agent`, `default_workspace` and `list_workspaces` live in `movie_ui_server.py` (Task 11); `movie_ui_lib` imports no `fleet_lib`.
2. **MU70 step 3 vs GC10: the `story.md` backup.** MU70 says "copy it (bytes, `shutil.copyfile`)"; GC10 says every file movie-ui writes is atomic and 0600. The backup is the same bytes written by `write_bytes_atomic` (Task 14). The new `story.md` is also 0600 after a save.
3. **Q3's summary sentence vs V3 (a)-(e).** The resolution line at `a378d33` paraphrases intact as "every index-listed shard resolves non-empty, or at least one non-empty .safetensors without an index, and no .incomplete blob". V3's detailed rules also report (c) `broken file: <relpath>` for any snapshot entry whose target is missing (for example a broken `embedded_config.json` link) and `empty file: <relpath>` for any empty `.safetensors`, and (d) `no .safetensors file` even when an index exists. The plan implements V3's rules exactly (the `config.json` clause is not applied); if the user means the narrower reading, delete the `broken file` branch and restrict the `empty file` check to index-less snapshots in `intact_problems` (Task 7) and the matching rows of `test_mut5_*`.
4. **MU67 vs V7: progress after the job ends.** MU67's summaries carry `progress` only for starting/running jobs, but V7 passes "when the smoke job is `done` and `progress.smoke.clip_ok` is true". `GET /api/jobs/<id>` therefore returns the MU56 object for every job that has a `status.json` (final ones included); summaries keep MU67's rule (Task 12). The page's V7 line reads the detail (Task 17).
5. **MUT11's "within 2 s".** The runner checks the marker once per `child.wait(timeout=1)` (MU22 step 6), so "TERM-ignoring child with grace 1 s -> cancelled within 2 s" is read as measured from the runner's first sight of the marker; the tests allow `1.0 + 1.0 + 1.0` s from marker creation (poll, grace, slack), and MUK4's own-session case asserts the 16 s bound.
6. **Spec 0.3's six test files vs the Q4 isolation.** The smoke tests are appended to `test_movie_ui_builder.py`, `test_movie_ui_checks.py` and `test_movie_ui_http.py` by Task 17, so no seventh test file appears.

**Readings where the spec is silent:**

7. **V1 rule 18** runs only when rules 1-17 produced no failure (the argv it inspects can only be built from valid fields). **V1 rule 11** messages the spec does not give: `<video|stills> LoRAs must be a list of at most 8 items` and `<video|stills> LoRA <k>: must be an object`.
8. **MU37:** `help_flags` returns `(flags, error)`; a run with no exit code (timeout, `OSError`) caches `cannot read bin/ltx-movie --help (exit none): ...`. The parser calls `re.split(r"\s{2,}", line[2:], maxsplit=1)`: identical to the spec's positional `1`, which Python 3.13 deprecates with a warning.
9. **MU22 step 2** with no `status.json`: `[movie-ui] job is unknown; nothing to run`. **MU31** also drops duplicate queue IDs. `launch_runner` keeps runner `Popen`s in `RUNNERS` and polls (never waits for) them at each launch, so finished runners are reaped.
10. **MU33:** a current link whose target is not a release tag is labelled `<agent> current (unknown tag)`. The current release is listed once, as the current candidate (first occurrence wins), so its `prune` is null.
11. **MU71/MU72/MU73:** disagreeing registries give `port_source: null`; an agent without a URL is listed in `/api/traces/recent`'s `errors` with its registry error, and `GET /api/traces/<id>?agent=<that agent>` answers 502 with it.
12. **Texts the spec leaves open:** `unknown job <id>` (404), `no story <id> in <dir>` (404), `not found` for a URL story ID containing `..`, `no saved trace <id>` (404), `invalid Content-Length` (400), `text must be a string`, `base_sha256 must be 64 hex digits or null`, `ids must be a list of strings`, `direction must be up or down`, `path must be an absolute path` (`/files`), `lora-catalog printed unparsable output` and `character printed unparsable output` (502); MU65's `class noun must match [a-z]{3,12}`, `trigger must match [a-z][a-z0-9]{3,15}`, `seed must be an integer >= 0`, `descriptor must be a string`, `force must be true or false`.
13. **Check details the spec leaves open:** V2 render passes with the binary path, stills with `stills dependencies importable`, judge key with `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is set`; a `story-server status` without a state line fails as `bin/story-server status exited <n>: <line or ->`. V3: an absolute model path that is not a directory fails `<path> is not a directory`. V4: a free path passes `<path>: safetensors header ok`; a failing `lora-catalog list` fails the record that needed it with `bin/lora-catalog list --model <M> --json exited <n>: <last stderr line or "unparsable output">`. V5: pass details `every catalog LoRA is compatible`, `trained`, `trained on <base>`; with no cast, one `skip` record named `characters` (`no character cast`); for smoke, one `skip` record named `characters` (`not needed for this action`, MU52's "every other record is skip"); a character's `video base model` record exists only when its `loras.video` is set; an unparsable `character show` fails `cannot parse bin/character show <name>`.
14. **MU10/MU53 ordering:** `POST /api/uploads` checks the media type (415) before the 20 MiB limit (413); a body over 25 MiB answers MU10's 413 first. A missing static file answers 404 `{"error": "not found"}`. `POST /` is not a route (404).
15. **MU66:** `POST /api/preview` for action `smoke` returns `argv: null` with no errors (the smoke argv names `STATE/smoke/<id>/`, which exists only once the job does).
16. **MU7:** the SIGTERM handler raises `KeyboardInterrupt`; `serve_forever` returns through it and `main` calls `server_close()` (no helper thread is needed).
17. **The page (MU76-MU85):** reason spans next to gated buttons (`#c-write-story-reason`, `#c-run-all-reason`, `#c-smoke-reason`, `#ch-create-status`, `#ch-train-reason`); `#c-command-for` names the action being previewed: `story` by default, `movie` after Render or Run all, and Validate checks that same action; switching to Stories or Characters reloads that list; the V7 line is `#c-smoke-check`; Regenerate and Redo need an earlier job's `last_fields` and say so otherwise.
18. **Spec 14.2 helpers:** the stub `bin/story-server` is `#!/bin/sh` exec'ing a Python stub; stubs record `{"tool", "sub", "argv", "cwd", "env"}` (env: `STORY_PIPELINE_LOGGED`, `PYTHONUNBUFFERED`, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`); config keys may name the full argument list. Added helpers: `fields`, `registry`, `copy_ui` (MUT15's subprocess runs a copy of the UI so its capability probe runs a stub `story-server`, never the real one: GC8), `trace_record`, `smoke_host`.
19. **Upload IDs in V1 rule 7** are checked for existence in `STATE/uploads`; the `fields` stored in `job.json` are `compose_fields(body)` with `ws` replaced by the realpath (MU15).
20. **Test seams:** `MovieUIServer.probe_ws` and `.stills_py` (the capability probe's workspace and interpreter; default the UI workspace and `sys.executable`), `movie_ui_lib.CANCEL_GRACE_S` and `DRY_RUN_TIMEOUT` (monkeypatched), and `job_alive(jdir, now=None)`.

**Execution notes:**

21. **Q1 and Q4 isolation:** Task 3 is the whole of Q1 (one Edit of Task 2's `cancel_groups`); Task 17 is the whole of Q4 (five Edits of `movie_ui_lib.py`, two of `movie_ui_server.py`, two of `index.html`, three of `app.js`, and appends). Both are confirmed at `a378d33`; neither should be skipped.
22. **Interim 500s:** Task 10 registers all 35 routes so MUT18 can pin the token rule for every one; a matched route whose handler a later task adds answers 500 until that task lands. No interim commit is shipped.
23. **Ordering:** the runner entry point `bin/movie-ui-job` is created in Task 2 (MUT10/MUT12 need it), and `bin/movie-ui` with `main()` in Task 11 (MUT15 needs `/api/host`).
24. **MUK1 on 3.9:** `/usr/bin/python3` has no pytest here. Every task compiles under 3.9, MUT25 does so as a test, and Task 18 Step 8 runs movie-ui end to end under 3.9. Running the MUT/MPT functions themselves under 3.9 requires installing pytest for `/usr/bin/python3`; that is a user decision this plan does not take.
25. **Task size:** 18 tasks; the sweep (Q1) and the smoke render (Q4) are their own tasks so either answer flips one task, and the five server areas are separate reviewer gates.

**Accepted risks (spec 19, unchanged):** MUX1 (sweep race), MUX2 (pgid reuse; kill-orphan is manual), MUX3 (the UI's own release pruned), MUX4 (real caches), MUX5 (long validations), MUX6 (test hooks in the environment). Live checks MUR2-MUR5 are human-run after the merge.

## Execution handoff

Plan complete; the controller has chosen subagent-driven execution: one Sonnet implementer per task that sees only that task plus Global Constraints, then an Opus reviewer per task, then Task 18's whole-branch review.
